### [73] [Improving Text-Instance Alignment Of Foreground Conditioned Out-Painting Via Customized Concept Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10892)
*Yihao Zhao,Xuan Han,Bin He,Mingyu You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对前景条件式图像外延（FCO）中生成背景存在与前景同语义的伪影问题，提出CCE-Diffusion框架，通过自定义概念嵌入模块、实例感知损失和语义保持提示模板，显著减少伪影，可作为即插即用模块提升多种FCO方法效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动的FCO方法生成的背景中常出现与前景实例同语义的伪影区域，削弱了目标的突出性并降低了图像质量，作者将该问题归因于给定实例与文本衍生的概念嵌入之间的不对齐。

Method: 提出CCE-Diffusion框架，核心是CCE-Module（自定义概念嵌入模块）用以弥合通用名词语义与特定视觉实例之间的差距；引入实例感知损失（Instance-Aware Loss）指导模块优化；设计语义保持提示模板（Semantic-Preserving Prompt Template）防止自定义嵌入扭曲提示中的其他词汇。

Result: 定性和定量评估均表明CCE-Diffusion显著减少了输出中的伪影，且CCE-Module作为即插即用组件可与多种FCO方法集成，提升其性能。

Conclusion: 提出了一种通过自定义概念嵌入解决FCO中前景-背景语义不对齐问题的有效框架，在减少伪影的同时保持模型可扩展性。

Abstract: To showcase products, merchants often incur substantial costs creating high-quality display images. Foreground Conditioned Outpainting (FCO) meets this demand, allowing users to create desired backgrounds for foreground instances at a low cost by adjusting the text prompt. However, existing text-driven FCO methods exhibit critical flaws in their outputs, most notably the presence of artifacts, which refer to regions in the synthesized background that share the same semantics as the foreground instance. Such artifacts diminish the object's prominence and degrade image quality. We attribute the issue to the misalignment between the given instance and text-derived concept embeddings. To address this, we propose the Customized Concept Embedding Diffusion (CCE-Diffusion) framework. Its core is a CCE-Module to customize concept embeddings, bridging the gap between generic noun semantics and a specific visual instance. An Instance-Aware Loss guides the module's optimization, while a Semantic-Preserving Prompt Template prevents customized embeddings from distorting other words in the prompt. Both qualitative and quantitative evaluations demonstrate that CCE-Diffusion significantly reduces artifacts in the outputs. As a plug-and-play component, the CCE-Module can integrate with various FCO methods, enhancing their performance.

</details>


### [74] [The 1st PortraitCraft Challenge: A CVPR 2026 Workshop Competition on Portrait Composition Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10894)
*Zijie Lou,Youyun Tang,Xiaochao Qu,Haoxiang Li,Ting Liu,Luoqi Liu,Xun Zhu,Zheng Zhang,Xi Chen,Miao Li,Ji Wu,Dizhe Zhang,Xian Ge,Sujia Wang,Ruiyang Zhang,Jiaming Wang,Xianshun Wang,Lu Qi,Boao Kang,Wei Zhou,Jinghui Sun,Zhenyu Yan,Jiliang Zhao,Rui Yang,Yipo Huang,Boyuan Liu,Shanglin Li,Zifan Xie,Yichen Zhang,Anlan Wang,Wenfeng Lin,Mingyu Guo,Dong Li,Xinghao Wang,Yanting Li,Shanzhao Tong,Shuai He,Qiu Zhou,Yongqi Yang,Taoyang Mu,Dianqiao Lei,Anlong Ming,Huadong Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CVPR 2026官方竞赛PortraitCraft Challenge，该挑战聚焦于肖像构图理解与生成，包含两个互补赛道（构图理解与构图生成），并发布了约50,000张带多层次标注的真实肖像数据集，为肖像美学分析和可控图像合成提供了标准化评估平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集和任务主要关注全局美学评分，缺乏对肖像构图的结构化理解和可控生成的统一评估框架。

Method: 提出包含两个互补赛道的统一评估框架：Track 1（结构化肖像构图理解）和Track 2（从结构化构图描述生成肖像图像），并构建了约50,000张带多层次标注的真实肖像数据集。

Result: 发布了大规模肖像构图数据集（约50,000张），描述了挑战设置、评估协议、数据集构成及最终结果，并分析了提交方案的技术特征。

Conclusion: PortraitCraft Challenge为肖像构图理解与生成提供了标准化、可复现的研究平台，有望推动肖像美学和可控图像生成领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: This paper presents an overview of the inaugural PortraitCraft Challenge, held as one of the official competitions at CVPR 2026. The challenge focuses on portrait composition understanding and generation, aiming to advance AI research in portrait aesthetics analysis and controllable image synthesis. Unlike existing datasets and tasks that primarily focus on global aesthetic scoring, PortraitCraft introduces a unified evaluation framework comprising two complementary tracks. Track 1 requires models to perform structured portrait composition understanding, and Track 2 requires models to generate portrait images from structured composition descriptions under explicit compositional constraints. To support the challenge, we constructed and publicly released a large-scale portrait composition dataset consisting of approximately 50,000 curated real portrait images, providing multi-level supervision. This report describes the challenge setup, evaluation protocols, dataset composition, and final results, along with an analysis of the technical characteristics of the submitted solutions. The PortraitCraft Challenge provides a standardized and reproducible platform for research on portrait composition understanding and generation, and is expected to foster further progress in the fields of portrait aesthetics and controllable image generation.

</details>


### [75] [Pose-ICL: 3D-Aware In-Context Learning for Pose-Controllable Subject Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10902)
*Xuan Han,Yihao Zhao,Mingyu You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Pose-ICL提出了一种基于3D感知上下文学习的免调优框架，通过表面锚定位置嵌入（SAPE）实现定制化图像生成中的精确姿态控制，在姿态准确性和身份一致性上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定制化图像生成方法在姿态控制方面表现不佳，常出现姿态不准确或跨姿态外观不一致的问题，说明2D骨干网络难以实现物体的体素级理解。

Method: 提出Pose-ICL框架，采用3D感知上下文学习（ICL）机制，核心是表面锚定位置嵌入（SAPE），将图像token锚定到体素包围盒的表面坐标上，赋予模型显式3D感知能力，并通过专门优化确保与现有DiT模型无缝兼容。

Result: 在3D资产和真实世界物体上的大量评估表明，Pose-ICL在姿态准确性和身份一致性方面显著优于当前方法。

Conclusion: Pose-ICL通过3D感知上下文学习有效解决了定制化图像生成中的姿态控制难题，为2D骨干网络赋予了体素级物体理解能力。

Abstract: Subject Customization is a foundational task in modern image generation. By providing a few reference images and a text prompt, users can generate images of a specific object in any desired scene. However, existing methods still struggle to achieve effective pose control for customized subjects. In practice, they often exhibit inaccurate poses or inconsistent cross-pose appearances. These limitations suggest that understanding objects in a volumetric manner remains a significant challenge for 2D-native backbones. To address this challenge, we propose Pose-ICL, a tuning-free framework that leverages 3D-aware In-Context Learning (ICL) to directly adapt to new subjects through multiple paired image-pose references. Its core mechanism,Surface-Anchored Position Embedding (SAPE), equips the model with explicit 3D awareness by anchoring image tokens to the surface coordinates of a volumetric bounding box. Dedicated refinements ensure its seamless compatibility with existing DiT models. Extensive evaluations on both 3D assets and real-world subjects demonstrate that Pose-ICL significantly outperforms current methods in both pose accuracy and identity consistency.

</details>


### [76] [Beyond Model Size: Probing the Gaps in Visual in-Context Learning by Training a Tiny Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10905)
*Sunil Khatri,Steven Landgraf,Markus Ulrich,Simon Reiß*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过训练一个仅1百万参数、7万张图像的极小视觉上下文学习（VICL）模型，与7000倍大的VICL模型在多种自适应设置下对比，发现当前VICL评估体系在任务编码方式、预训练任务选择及评估指标上存在缺陷，挑战了"模型和数据规模是关键"的主流观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验模型和数据规模是否是VICL能力形成的关键因素，揭示当前大规模VICL模型评估体系的潜在缺陷。

Method: 构建极端反例：训练一个仅1M参数、70K图像的极小VICL模型，与7000倍大的模型在三种自适应设置（小分布偏移、未见任务编码、全新任务）下进行对比分析。

Result: 小模型在多种自适应场景下的表现挑战了"规模是关键"的假设，揭示了当前VICL评估体系在任务编码、预训练任务选择及评估指标方面的不足。

Conclusion: 当前VICL基准测试在评估自适应能力方面存在根本性缺陷，需要创新评估方法来真实衡量视觉模型的上下文学习能力。

Abstract: Visual in-Context Learning (VICL) aims at making progress towards adaptive vision models, that can -- based on a few examples -- adapt to a new task at test-time. With the history of in-context learning in natural language processing research, where large, parameter-heavy models are in use, one pathway that current VICL methods take is model- and data-scaling as key ingredients. Yet, it is not clear, whether these ingredients are the key for in-context learning to take shape in vision models. To stress-test such large models, we challenge them with an extreme counterexample: we train a tiny visual in-context model with merely $1$ million parameters and a modest amount of $70,000$ images. We compare the results of this severely capacity capped tiny model to $7,000\times$ larger VICL models in different adaptive settings, (1) on image data with small distribution shifts, (2) on unseen task encodings and (3) on a completely new task, i.e., the setting VICL envisions. With the chasm of training resources between the tiny- and large models, our experiments showcase a lack in how adaptive capabilities are measured, with respect to how tasks are encoded, which tasks were used in pre-training and the choice of metrics. These gaps in current VICL benchmarking underscore a need for innovation in evaluation of adaptive capabilities.

</details>


### [77] [PENet+: A Lightweight Residual Transformer Framework for Efficient Image Steganalysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10939)
*Jincheol AN,Dongsu Kim,Haneol Jang,YoungJoon Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PENet+，一种轻量级图像隐写分析框架，在保留PENet自注意力拓扑结构的基础上，通过精简SPP到FC1的通道数、引入激活感知的高通滤波选择机制、将骨干网络替换为MobileNetV2风格的反转残差网络以及使用PReLU激活函数，实现了参数减少45.5%、FLOPs降低约97%，且检测精度几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有残差Transformer架构（如PENet）在图像隐写分析中检测精度高，但计算和内存开销大，难以部署于资源受限场景。因此，本文旨在设计一种轻量级框架，在保持检测性能的同时大幅提升计算效率。

Method: 1) 保留PENet的自注意力拓扑结构以保证可复现性；2) 添加分类器精简阶段，逐步缩窄SPP到FC1的输入通道，减少参数量和FLOPs；3) 改进高通滤波（HPF）模块，引入激活感知机制，早期聚合HPF响应并选择平衡的SRM-Gabor top-K子集；4) 将骨干网络替换为MobileNetV2风格的反转残差网络；5) 使用PReLU激活函数，保留负响应以捕获ReLU抑制的弱隐写线索。

Result: 在ALASKA2 JPEG QF90协议、512×512分辨率下，PENet+相比重新评估的PENet基线，参数减少高达45.5%，FLOPs降低约97%，检测精度基本持平或更优。K=31滤波器（16 Gabor + 15 SRM）的平衡配置在更低计算量下达到或超越更重的设置。

Conclusion: PENet+为资源受限场景下的隐写分析提供了一条高效的计算方向，显著降低了计算开销。设备级延迟和功耗测量留作未来工作。

Abstract: Image steganalysis, the detection of hidden information embedded in digital images, is a core component of modern cybersecurity and digital forensics. Recent residual Transformer architectures, such as the Pixel-Difference-Convolution and Enhanced-Transformer-Network (PENet) [1], achieve strong detection accuracy, but their computational and memory demands hinder deployment in resource-constrained settings. We present PENet+, a lightweight steganalysis framework that preserves PENet's discriminative structure while substantially improving efficiency. Rather than redesigning or compressing the attention blocks, we retain PENet's self-attention topology for reproducibility and add a classifier-streamlining stage that progressively narrows the SPP-to-FC1 input channels (SPP: spatial pyramid pooling; FC1: first fully connected layer), yielding large reductions in parameters and FLOPs with negligible accuracy loss. We further refine the high-pass-filter (HPF) stem with an activation-aware mechanism that aggregates HPF responses early and selects a balanced SRM-Gabor top-K subset, and we replace PENet's backbone with a MobileNetV2-style inverted residual network. A balanced configuration with K=31 filters (16 Gabor + 15 SRM) matches or surpasses heavier settings at lower compute. Finally, we motivate PReLU from a steganalysis standpoint, arguing that preserving negative responses helps capture weak stego cues that ReLU suppresses. On a disjoint ALASKA2 JPEG QF90 protocol at 512x512 resolution (5,000 cover images for training, validation, and internal testing; a separate 19,000-cover evaluation set), PENet+ achieves up to 45.5% fewer parameters and about 97% fewer FLOPs than the re-evaluated PENet baseline, offering a computationally efficient direction for resource-constrained steganalysis. Device-level latency and power measurements remain future work.

</details>


### [78] [Democratising Camera Trap AI: An Open-Source Model for Detecting UK Mammals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10940)
*Paul Fergus,Philip Stephens,Russell A. Hill,Lee Oliver,Katie Appleby,Sarah Beatham,Naomi Davies Walsh,Stuart Nixon,Naomi Matthews,Chris Sutherland,Kelly Hitchcock*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个开源的YOLO26x目标检测模型，用于识别英国常见的28种哺乳动物和鸟类以及人类、校准杆和车辆等共31个类别。模型基于10年间从多个地点收集的48,165个标注实例训练，在验证集上mAP@0.5达到0.984，精度0.988，召回0.965，并以ONNX格式开源，旨在为生态学家提供免费替代商业平台的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前红外相机监测产生的海量图像数据依赖AI处理，但现有AI模型要么被锁定在商业平台中，要么训练数据与英国地区的动物种类不匹配，这阻碍了生态学研究的普及和推广。本文旨在消除这些障碍，提供一个免费、开源且面向非机器学习背景生态学家的模型。

Method: 使用YOLO26x检测器，基于从Conservation AI及其后续平台Trap Tracker十年运营中收集的48,165个标注实例（涵盖31个类别），按80/10/10进行类别分层划分训练、验证和测试集。

Result: 验证集上mAP@0.5达0.984，mAP@0.5-0.95达0.956，精度0.988，召回0.965。测试集上每个类别的平均置信度在0.96-0.99之间，假阴性率仅0.17%，主要集中在夜间、远距离或遮挡图像。模型以ONNX格式在非商业许可下开源，支持本地桌面和实时摄像头。

Conclusion: 该开源模型在已知站点数据上表现优异，有效降低了生态学家使用AI进行物种识别门槛，是针对过去十年多个付费模型的有意制衡。但模型在全新站点的泛化性能尚待验证，这是未来工作方向。

Abstract: Camera traps have become a cornerstone of biodiversity monitoring, but the artificial intelligence that turns vast quantities of images into usable ecological data is often locked behind commercial platforms or trained on fauna that does not match that of the British Isles. In an attempt to remove barriers and increase uptake, we release an open-source object detection model for 31 classes, 28 common UK mammal and bird species, plus utility classes for humans, calibration poles, and vehicles, drawn from a curated dataset of 48,165 labelled instances assembled from multiple sites over a decade of operational deployment through Conservation AI and its successor, Trap Tracker. The model, a YOLO26x detector trained and tested on an 80/10/10 class-stratified split, achieves a mean Average Precision of 0.984 at Intersection over Union (IoU) of 0.5 (0.956 at IoU 0.5-0.95) on the held-out validation set, with precision 0.988 and recall 0.965. On an unseen held-out test split, mean per-species confidence ranged from 0.96 to 0.99 across the 31 classes, with a 0.17% false-negative rate concentrated in difficult night-time, distant, or occluded images. These metrics are from data from the same pool of sites and cameras as training, so performance at entirely new sites is left to future work. We release the trained weights in ONNX format under a non-commercial licence, with local desktop and real-time camera support, aimed explicitly at ecologists with no machine-learning experience. This release is a deliberate counterweight to the multiple paid for models that have developed over the last decade.

</details>


### [79] [Quo Vadis, Visual In-Context Learning? A Unified Benchmark Across Domains and Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10967)
*Pradnya Halady,Jiale Wei,Zdravko Marinov,Alexander Jaus,Simon Reiß*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向多样化成像领域和广泛任务的视觉上下文学习基准（VIBE），通过统一协议评估6个模型在14个数据集、12个任务（共106种组合）上的单样本自适应能力，揭示了当前视觉上下文学习的局限性、系统故障模式和未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉上下文学习（Visual In-Context Learning）模型的评估局限于狭窄的设置，主要反映预训练时已见过的任务或图像域，无法真正检验模型的适应能力。作者旨在构建一个广泛、多样化的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建VIBE基准，涵盖多样化的成像领域和广泛任务；在统一、可复现的评估协议下，以单样本（one-shot）设置对6个模型在14个数据集、12个任务（共106种数据集-任务组合）上进行压力测试。

Result: 揭示了视觉上下文学习的关键见解，包括模型的局限性、系统性故障模式以及有希望的研究方向。

Conclusion: 现有视觉上下文学习模型在面对全新图像和任务分布时适应能力有限；VIBE基准为更全面评估提供了工具，同时将开源VIBE工具包以促进更广泛的评估。

Abstract: Visual in-context learning has been proposed as a pathway towards dynamic models that can generate predictions based on a provided context and thereby can adapt to new vision tasks at test-time. Yet, the evaluation of the adaptation capabilities of these models has been limited to narrow setups that mainly mirror tasks or image domains from pre-training for which real adaptation is not required. We address this gap by constructing a broad Visual In-Context BEnchmark (VIBE) with a focus on diverse imaging domains and a wide range of tasks. With this, we are able to get a much clearer picture of the adaptive capabilities of visual in-context models when faced with new image- and task distributions. We stress test six models on $14$ datasets and $12$ tasks (in total, we explore $106$ dataset-task combinations) and compare them under a unified, reproducible evaluation protocol, in an one-shot setting. Our evaluation uncovers key insights on the state of visual in-context learning, including limitations, systematic failure modes and promising directions. To foster broader evaluation, we will openly release our VIBE toolkit.

</details>


### [80] [AnimaSpark: A Feed-Forward Method for Animating Arbitrary 3D Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10988)
*Yiming Zhao,Haoyu Sun,Aoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnimaSpark 提出了一种新的类别无关3D动画生成流程，通过将3D模型渲染为多层图像表示，利用视频生成模型和关键点跟踪，将2D运动提升到3D空间，在文本-运动对齐、运动质量和计算效率上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前类别无关的3D动画生成在推理速度、运动质量和文本提示遵循性方面存在严重局限，导致仍依赖耗时的人工操作。

Method: 1) 将绑定好的静态3D模型渲染为包含网格和骨架的多层图像表示；2) 输入到视频生成模型中；3) 使用关键点跟踪算法捕获骨架关节在摄像机成像平面上的投影运动；4) 从跟踪的关键点中提取平面平移和旋转，将其从2D提升到3D空间以驱动角色动画。

Result: 在文本-运动对齐、运动质量和计算效率等关键指标上均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: AnimaSpark 有效解决了类别无关3D动画生成的瓶颈问题，证明了3D世界中许多基本运动的关节变换可在二维子空间内有效建模这一核心洞察的可行性。

Abstract: While recent advancements in generative AI have substantially accelerated static 3D model creation workflows, the synthesis of category-agnostic 3D animations remains a significant bottleneck in 3D asset production. Current methods for category-agnostic animation generation exhibit critical limitations in inference speed, motion quality, and adherence to textual prompts, thereby leaving the process dependent on labor-intensive manual artistry. To address these challenges, this paper introduces AnimaSpark, a novel pipeline for category-agnostic 3D animation generation. Our approach is motivated by the key insight that for many fundamental motions in the 3D world, the corresponding joint transformations can often be effectively modeled within a two-dimensional subspace. The pipeline begins by rendering a rigged static 3D model into multi-layered image representations of its mesh and skeleton, which are subsequently fed into a video generation model. We then employ a keypoint tracking algorithm on the generated video to capture the motion of the skeletal joints projected onto the camera's viewing plane. In the final stage, we distill the planar translations and rotations from these tracked keypoints and lift them from the 2D domain into 3D space to animate the character. Comprehensive evaluations reveal that our method achieves superior performance over existing state-of-the-art techniques across key metrics, including text-motion alignment, quality of motion, and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [81] [IPSM-Bench: A New Intermediate Phase Segmentation Benchmark in Microstructure Images of Zinc-Based Absorbable Biomaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11001)
*Jinglin Xu,Shangyan Zhao,Jiabo Wang,Xinghong Mu,Yulong Lei,Jiacheng Zhang,Hongbo Sun,Yageng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对锌基合金中间相分割面临的标注数据稀缺、对比度低、小目标检测难等问题，构建了最大高质量数据集IPSM-Bench，并提出空间上下文先验引导的SAM方法SCoP-SAM，在多个合金基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锌基合金中间相是调控材料宏观性能的关键微观结构组分，但现有方法因标注数据稀缺、图像对比度低、小目标检测困难及形态异质性等问题，难以实现精准分割。

Method: 构建IPSM-Bench数据集；提出SCoP-SAM方法，利用中间相的梯度结构和灰度特性捕获空间上下文先验，并将其融入SAM编码-解码全过程。

Result: SCoP-SAM在IPSM-Bench及公开合金基准上均取得SOTA分割性能，且泛化至其他合金场景表现优异。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了锌基合金中间相分割的关键挑战，为合金微观结构分析提供了新的基准和方法支撑。

Abstract: Zinc-based alloys are indispensable emerging absorbable metallic biomaterials, and their macroscopic performance is governed by microstructural characteristics. Intermediate phases-key microstructural constituents-are pivotal in regulating mechanical and functional properties. However, intermediate phase segmentation in zinc alloy microstructures faces formidable challenges: scarce annotated datasets, low contrast, difficulty detecting small targets, and heterogeneous morphologies. To this end, we construct IPSM-Bench, the largest high-quality dataset for zinc-alloy intermediate phase segmentation. Furthermore, we propose SCoP-SAM, a new Spatial Context Prior-guided SAM method that leverages the gradient structure and grayscale properties of intermediate phases to capture spatial context priors and incorporates them into the entire SAM encoding-decoding process, improving segmentation performance. Based on the proposed IPSM-Bench, we establish a new benchmark for intermediate phase segmentation to systematically evaluate state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods and advance research on zinc alloy microstructure analysis. Extensive experiments on IPSM-Bench and additional public alloy benchmarks demonstrate that our SCoP-SAM not only achieves SOTA performance for zinc-alloy intermediate phase segmentation but also generalizes remarkably well to other alloy scenarios.

</details>


### [82] [An Uncertainty Estimation Framework for Dose Accumulation in Adaptive Radiotherapy: Application to CBCT-Guided Radiotherapy for Cervical Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11012)
*Cedric Hemon,Delphine Lebret,Jean-Claude Nunes,Valentin Boussot,Karine Peignaux,Nathalie Mesgouez-Nebout,Chantal Hanzen,Antoine Simon,Anaïs Barateau,Renaud de Crevoisier,Caroline Lafond*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了IMPACT-DoseAcc框架，用于在线自适应放疗中的不确定性感知剂量累积，通过传播图像配准不确定性到累积剂量指标，在宫颈癌CBCT-guided oART中验证，pDVH覆盖率达96.3±3.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线自适应放疗(oART)虽能每日调整计划以适应分次间解剖变化，但累积剂量估计受限于图像配准(DIR)、分割和解剖不确定性，缺乏有效的不确定性量化方法。

Method: 基于IMPACT框架，集成两种DIR不确定性策略：贝叶斯分割引导的概率模型和分割模型集成；通过体素级不确定性图传播到剂量形变和累积，生成概率性剂量体积直方图(pDVH)，并采用解剖变异加权细化聚合。

Result: 集成DIR不确定性与几何误差相关（CTVt Pearson系数0.63，膀胱0.66）；CTVt的pDVH覆盖率达96.3±3.9%；加权方法稳定了分次间和器官间的估计。

Conclusion: IMPACT-DoseAcc成功将配准驱动的不确定性传播到累积剂量指标，提高了解剖变异下累积剂量的可解释性，3DSlicer集成支持可重复的不确定性感知ART工作流。

Abstract: Background and purpose: oART enables daily plan adaptation to interfraction anatomical variations, but cumulative dose estimation remains limited by DIR, segmentation, and anatomical uncertainties. We introduce IMPACT-DoseAcc, an uncertainty-aware dose accumulation framework, within IMPACT for semantic feature-driven image analysis. The framework is modality- and disease-agnostic and is applied to CBCT-guided oART for cervical cancer (LACC).
  Material and Methods: Nine LACC patients were retrospectively analyzed using daily CBCT-derived virtual CTs for dose recalculation. IMPACT-DoseAcc focuses on uncertainty from DIR, without modeling vCT-generation uncertainty. Two DIR uncertainty strategies were tested within IMPACT-Reg: a Bayesian segmentation-guided approach using one probabilistic model to quantify anatomical uncertainty, and an ensemble of segmentation models targeting structures to capture epistemic variability. Voxel-wise uncertainty maps were propagated through dose warping and accumulation to generate probabilistic dose-volume histograms. Ensemble uncertainty was quantified from voxel-wise standard deviation across deformation fields, and geometric error was assessed using surface distance between warped and validated contours. Anatomical-variability weighting refined aggregation.
  Results: Ensemble DIR uncertainty correlated with geometric error, with Pearson coefficients of 0.63 for CTVt and 0.66 for bladder. For CTVt, pDVHs achieved 96.3 +/- 3.9% coverage, showing calibration of propagated uncertainty. Weighting stabilized estimates across fractions and organs.
  Conclusions: IMPACT-DoseAcc propagates registration-driven uncertainty to cumulative dose metrics, improving interpretation of accumulated dose under anatomical variations. Its 3DSlicer integration supports reproducible, uncertainty-informed ART workflows.

</details>


### [83] [U-TTT: Towards Generalizable PET Image Denoising via Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11032)
*Zhiwen Yang,Jiayin Li,Hao Lu,Hui Zhang,Zihua Wang,Bingzheng Wei,Yan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出U-TTT模型，通过集成测试时训练（TTT）层和双域适应机制（空间域S-TTT和频率域F-TTT），在推理阶段动态调整参数以适配每个测试实例，从而解决PET图像去噪模型在分布偏移下性能退化的问题，在未见剂量水平和扫描仪上均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PET图像去噪深度学习模型在分布偏移（如不同剂量水平或扫描仪类型）下性能严重退化，根本原因在于固定参数模型无法在训练后适应测试数据的变化，限制了其稳健的临床部署。

Method: 提出U形网络U-TTT，集成测试时训练（TTT）层实现推理阶段的参数自适应；设计双域适应机制，包括空间域S-TTT层（捕获和校正空间结构退化）和频率域F-TTT层（抑制全局噪声谱、恢复高频细节）。

Result: 在多种分布偏移（未见过剂量水平、未见过扫描仪）下，U-TTT达到了最先进的PET去噪性能，并展现出卓越的泛化能力。

Conclusion: U-TTT通过测试时训练与双域适应相结合，有效解决了PET去噪模型在分布偏移下的泛化问题，为模型在临床环境中的稳健部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Existing deep learning models for Positron Emission Tomography (PET) image denoising often suffer from severe performance degradation under distribution shifts, fundamentally restricting their robust clinical deployment. This lack of generalization stems from the conventional paradigm of fixed-parameter models that cannot adapt to variations in test data (e.g., dose levels or scanner types) after training. To overcome this limitation and achieve robust generalization, we introduce U-TTT, a novel U-shaped model that integrates Test-Time Training (TTT) layers to dynamically adjust model parameters during inference through self-supervision, thereby adapting to the specific characteristics of each test instance. Furthermore, to comprehensively capture the complex degradations of 3D PET data, U-TTT features a dual-domain adaptation mechanism comprising a Spatial Test-Time Training (S-TTT) layer and a Frequency Test-Time Training (F-TTT) layer. The S-TTT layer captures and corrects spatial structural degradations, while the F-TTT layer suppresses global noise spectra and restores delicate high-frequency details. Extensive experiments demonstrate that U-TTT achieves state-of-the-art PET denoising performance and exhibits superior generalization under challenging distribution shifts, including both unseen dose levels and unseen scanners. Our code will be available at https://github.com/Yaziwel/U-TTT.

</details>


### [84] [IDEAL: In-DEpth ALignment Makes A Discrete Representation AutoEncoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11096)
*Yitong Chen,Zijie Diao,Junke Wang,Lingyu Kong,Yixuan Ren,Bo He,Yu-Gang Jiang,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Ideal框架，通过联合对齐量化的离散token与浅层和深层VFM特征，解决了表示自编码器因深层VFM特征丢失细粒度细节而导致重建质量差的问题，在ImageNet上达到0.61 rFID，自回归图像生成gFID达1.89，均实现最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练视觉基础模型（VFM）的表示自编码器（RAE）在构建语义丰富的潜空间方面表现良好，但由于深层VFM特征未能保留足够的细粒度视觉细节，导致重建质量欠佳；离散化后这一问题更加严重，丢失的低层信息难以恢复。观察到浅层VFM特征保留了更丰富的局部外观和结构细节，与深层特征的高层语义互补。

Method: 提出Ideal（In-depth Alignment framework for discrete representation autoencoding），核心思路是将离散token同时与浅层和深层VFM特征进行联合对齐，使离散视觉token同时保留视觉保真度和丰富的语义信息。

Result: 在ImageNet上达到0.61 rFID，超越此前最佳方法0.28；用于自回归图像生成时，gFID达1.89，刷新该任务最先进水平。

Conclusion: 利用浅层与深层VFM特征的互补性进行联合对齐，能有效提升离散表示自编码的重建质量，并推动自回归图像生成性能达到新高度。

Abstract: Built on pretrained vision foundation models (VFMs), representation autoencoders (RAEs) have recently emerged as a promising approach for constructing semantically rich latent spaces for image generation. However, their reconstruction quality often remains suboptimal, largely because deep VFM representations do not preserve sufficient fine-grained visual detail. This limitation becomes even more severe after discretization, where missing low-level information is difficult to recover. In fact, we observe that shallow VFM features retain considerably richer local appearance and structural detail, which complements the high-level semantics carried by deep features used in existing RAEs. Motivated by this complementary property, we propose Ideal, an In-depth Alignment framework for discrete representation autoencoding. By jointly aligning quantized tokens with both shallow and deep VFM features, Ideal enables the resulting discrete visual tokens to preserve both visual fidelity and rich semantics. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Ideal yields superior reconstruction performance, achieving 0.61 rFID on ImageNet and outperforming the previous best method by 0.28. When used for autoregressive image generation, Ideal further produces a gFID of 1.89, establishing a new state of the art for autoregressive image generation.

</details>


### [85] [FADA: Accessible fetal ultrasound interpretation and annotation with a selectively distilled unified vision-language model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11106)
*Mahmood Alzubaidi,Uzair Shah,Raden Muaz,Ines Abbes,Nader Mohammed,Abdullatif Magram,Khalid Alyafei,Mowafa Househ,Marco Agus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FADA是一个统一的视觉-语言模型，通过单一解释优先流水线实现产前超声的临床解释、分类、检测和分割，无需外部标签，可在单消费级GPU上训练并在智能手机上离线部署，在资源受限环境中实现AI辅助胎儿评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球范围内训练有素的超声技师短缺，限制了中低收入国家的产前超声筛查，超过一半的孕妇未接受专业超声检查。现有深度学习方法将检测、分割或分类孤立处理，每个任务需要独立模型和推理时的专家指定标签，难以实际部署。

Method: 基于Qwen3.5-VL构建统一视觉-语言模型FADA，通过解释优先流水线完成临床解释、分类、检测和分割。从四个领域专用基础模型（FetalCLIP、UltraSAM、USF-MAE、UltraFedFM）离线预计算特征缓存进行知识蒸馏，采用选择性蒸馏策略（仅对标注任务进行特征对齐，解释任务使用标准微调）。推荐变体FADA-SKD，可在单消费级GPU上训练，并压缩至0.8B模型通过GGUF量化在智能手机上离线部署。

Result: FADA-SKD实现分割平均Dice 0.8820，检测mAP@0.50为0.7671，结构化解释合规率100%。专家超声技师验证237张图像，在自主和人机协同模式下均获得临床可接受输出，73.5%的解释在临床医生指导下获得完美评分。压缩后的0.8B模型在骁龙7 Gen 1手机上（12GB RAM）通过llama.cpp运行，完整五阶段流水线约60秒离线完成。

Conclusion: FADA提供了一条将AI辅助胎儿评估与便携式超声设备集成的实际路径，直接解决资源受限环境中的诊断可及性差距，无需云连接即可实现端侧离线部署。

Abstract: A global shortage of trained sonographers limits prenatal ultrasound screening in low- and middle-income countries, where over half of pregnant women receive no skilled sonography. Current deep learning approaches address detection, segmentation, or classification in isolation, each demanding a separate model and expert-specified labels at inference. We present FADA, a unified vision-language model built on Qwen3.5-VL that performs clinical interpretation, classification, detection, and segmentation through a single interpretation-first pipeline without external labels. FADA distills knowledge from four domain-specific foundation models (FetalCLIP, UltraSAM, USF-MAE, UltraFedFM) via offline pre-computed feature caching. Selective distillation, which applies feature alignment only to annotation tasks while interpretation relies on standard fine-tuning, consistently outperforms full distillation across most evaluation axes. The recommended variant, FADA-SKD, achieves 0.8820 mean Dice for segmentation, 0.7671 mAP@0.50 for detection, and 100% structured interpretation compliance. Expert sonographer validation across 237 images confirms clinically acceptable outputs in both autonomous and human-in-the-loop modes, with 73.5% of interpretations scoring perfectly under clinician guidance. The system is trainable on a single consumer GPU and deployable without cloud connectivity. We validate edge deployment by running the compressed 0.8B model on a commodity smartphone (Qualcomm Snapdragon 7 Gen 1, 12 GB RAM) using llama.cpp with GGUF quantization, completing the full 5-phase pipeline in approximately 60 seconds entirely offline. This establishes a practical pathway for integrating AI-assisted fetal assessment with portable ultrasound devices, directly addressing diagnostic access gaps in resource-constrained settings. Code, models, and data are available at https://github.com/mahmoodphd/FADA.

</details>


### [86] [WorldOlympiad: Can Your World Model Survive a Triathlon?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11129)
*Yuke Zhao,Wangbo Zhao,Weijie Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Dakai An,Akide Liu,Yinghao Yu,Jiasheng Tang,Fan Wang,Wei Wang,Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldOlympiad是一个用于诊断视频生成世界模型的基准测试，从物理真实性、几何一致性和交互保真度三个维度评估生成视频，覆盖游戏、机器人和通用真实世界场景，实验表明现有模型在物理推理、3D一致性和长程交互方面存在显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成基准主要关注视觉质量、语义对齐或短期时间一致性，缺乏对视频是否遵循物理规则、保持连贯3D结构以及支持长期可控交互的系统性评估。

Method: 将世界模型评估分解为三个互补维度：物理轨道（使用对象分割和MLLM评判器评估力学、热现象和材料属性）、几何轨道（通过高斯泼溅重建评估结构一致性、跨视角一致性和相机轨迹对齐）、交互轨道（评估生成序列是否遵循复杂动作提示并保持跨片段平滑过渡）。覆盖游戏、机器人和通用真实世界视频三大下游场景。

Result: 对最先进模型的实验揭示了在物理推理、3D一致性以及长程交互方面的显著差距。

Conclusion: 需要更结构化的评估协议来推动生成式世界模型的发展，WorldOlympiad提供了一种可扩展且可解释的评估框架。

Abstract: We introduce WorldOlympiad, a benchmark for diagnosing video-based world models across physical faithfulness, geometric consistency, and interaction fidelity. While existing benchmarks often focus on visual quality, semantic alignment, or short-term temporal coherence, they provide limited insight into whether generated videos obey physical rules, preserve coherent 3D structure, and sustain controllable interactions over long horizons. To address this gap, WorldOlympiad decomposes world-model evaluation into three complementary dimensions. The physical track uses object segmentation and MLLM-as-judge to assess whether generated videos follow interpretable rules in mechanics, thermal phenomena, and material properties. The geometry track reconstructs generated videos with Gaussian splatting and evaluates structural consistency, cross-view coherence, and camera-trajectory alignment. The interaction track assesses whether generated rollouts follow complex action prompts and maintain smooth, coherent transitions across consecutive video chunks. WorldOlympiad further covers three major downstream scenarios, including gaming, robotics, and general real-world videos, capturing diverse challenges from interactive control and embodied manipulation to open-domain motion and camera dynamics. Together, these tracks and scenarios form a scalable and interpretable evaluation suite that exposes failure modes beyond generic video quality. Experiments on state-of-the-art models reveal substantial gaps in physical reasoning, 3D consistency, and long-horizon interaction, underscoring the need for more structured evaluation protocols for generative world models.

</details>


### [87] [UniPET: a universal network for high-quality PET image denoising across varied dose reduction factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11131)
*Zhiwen Yang,Yang Zhou,Haowei Chen,Hui Zhang,Dan Zhao,Bingzheng Wei,Yan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniPET网络，通过引入领域泛化中的风格对齐技术（SAN）和区域感知学习策略（RALS），解决了不同降噪因子（DRF）下PET图像去噪中的风格消除和过度平滑问题，实现了跨DRF的通用高质量PET图像去噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PET图像去噪方法假设固定且已知的降噪因子（DRF），当DRF变化时性能显著下降；已有的通用去噪模型无法处理不同DRF数据间的风格错位问题，导致风格消除和过度平滑效应。

Method: 提出UniPET网络，包含两个核心创新：1）风格对齐网络（SAN），利用领域泛化的风格对齐技术来对齐和恢复不同DRF下的风格；2）区域感知学习策略（RALS），区分平坦区域和风格化区域，仅在风格化区域进行对抗学习，引导模型聚焦于学习风格化区域。

Result: UniPET在特定DRF下性能与针对特定DRF的模型相当，在通用PET图像去噪任务上实现了定量、感知和临床评估中的最先进性能。

Conclusion: UniPET能够自适应地恢复不同DRF的风格，实现跨DRF的高质量PET图像去噪，有效解决了风格消除和过度平滑问题。

Abstract: Most existing deep learning-based PET image denoising methods assume a fixed and known dose reduction factor (DRF) for low-dose PET images. However, these methods encounter significant performance degradation when the DRF varies beyond the assumed one in practical applications. To address the challenge posed by varied DRFs, several preliminary studies focus on the task of universal PET image denoising, aiming to train a universal model over low-dose data across DRFs. Nonetheless, these vanilla universal models often struggle with misaligned styles present in different DRF data, leading to the \textit{style elimination issue} with a significant over-smoothing effect. To deal with this issue, we innovatively introduce domain generalization to PET image denoising and propose a universal PET image denoising network (UniPET) to achieve high-quality PET image denoising across diverse DRFs. UniPET comprises two primary innovations: a style alignment network (SAN) and a region-aware learning strategy (RALS). Specifically, SAN utilizes style alignment techniques derived from domain generalization to align and recover styles across different DRFs, ensuring the model's generalizability across various DRFs while effectively preserving styles. Furthermore, to enhance style recovery, RALS distinguishes between flat and stylized regions, exclusively conducting adversarial learning on the latter, thereby more effectively guiding the model's focus towards learning stylized regions. It is demonstrated that our proposed UniPET can adaptively recover different DRF styles and achieve high-quality PET image denoising across DRFs. Comprehensive experiments show that UniPET exhibits comparable performance to individual DRF-specific models at specific DRFs and realizes state-of-the-art performance in universal PET image denoising quantitatively, perceptually, and clinically.

</details>


### [88] [MOFA-VTON: More Fashion Possibilities with Fine-Grained Adaptations in Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11148)
*Xiaoyu Han,Chenyang Wang,Jing Wang,Shunyuan Zheng,Quanling Meng,Shengping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MOFA-VTON提出了一种基于用户手绘草图的细粒度虚拟试穿方法，通过将草图转换为双区域掩码并利用交叉注意力机制独立学习上下半身的布局对应关系，实现了对目标服装的灵活自适应调整，在VITON-HD和DressCode数据集上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚拟试穿方法仅对原始服装进行直接替换，固定且单一的穿搭模式无法反映真实场景中多样化的穿着风格，缺乏对服装适配方式的灵活控制，导致试穿输出单调、缺乏时尚可能性。

Method: 1) 设计掩码构建策略，将用户手绘曲线草图转换为双区域掩码，替代传统服装无关掩码，提供细粒度布局引导；2) 提出布局调整模块，利用交叉注意力机制独立学习上下半身的布局对应关系，优化两区域的空间排布。

Result: 在VITON-HD和DressCode数据集上的大量实验表明，MOFA-VTON优于先前的最先进方法，提供了更多时尚可能性。

Conclusion: 通过用户草图实现细粒度服装适配调整的虚拟试穿方法有效突破了固定布局的限制，实现了灵活多样的试穿效果，为虚拟试穿领域提供了新的时尚探索方向。

Abstract: Virtual try-on aims to fit an in-shop clothing image onto a specific human body. An optimal virtual try-on method should provide diverse and flexible dressing options, accurately reflecting the varied wearing styles encountered in real-life scenarios, tailored to individual preferences and fashion aspirations. However, current methods predominantly perform a direct replacement of the original clothing with the target clothing, following the same dressing pattern. This limited control over clothing adaptation may result in fixed and monotonous try-on outputs. To delve into More Fashion Possibilities with Fine-Grained Adaptations in Virtual Try-On, we propose a novel virtual try-on method, termed MOFA-VTON, which allows adjustment for clothing adaptations in try-on results through simple sketches by users. Specifically, we first design a mask construction strategy that transforms user-drawn curve sketches into a dual-region mask, replacing the traditional clothing-agnostic mask and providing fine-grained layout guidance for the subsequent generation process. Further, we propose layout adjustment blocks that utilize the cross-attention mechanism to independently learn layout correspondences for upper and lower regions of the human body, refining the spatial arrangement of the two regions. With these implementations, our method enables flexible and fine-grained adaptations of target clothing, overcoming the constraints of a fixed layout. Extensive experiments on VITON-HD and DressCode datasets demonstrate that our proposed MOFA-VTON outperforms previous state-of-the-art methods and provides more fashion possibilities for virtual try-on.

</details>


### [89] [P3D-Bench: Benchmarking MLLMs for Parametric 3D Generation and Structural Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11152)
*Yikang Yang,Zhanpeng Hu,Youtian Lin,Mengqi Zhou,Jingxi Xu,Feihu Zhang,Jiaheng Liu,Yao Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出P3D-Bench基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型通过代码进行参数化3D建模的能力。该基准涵盖文本/图像到3D生成及3D装配三个任务，从可执行性、几何精度、语义对齐和部件级结构等多个维度进行评测。实验发现：3D装配最难，模型能恢复全局形状和语义但难以复现精确参数几何，且部件级建模能力薄弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型能够通过编写代码进行3D建模，但现有基准很少评估这种通过代码实现的3D建模能力。这种建模不仅要求代码可运行，还要求生成的参数化3D程序在几何上精确、语义上对齐且装配一致。因此需要一个新的基准来系统评估这一能力。

Method: 构建P3D-Bench基准，覆盖三个任务族：Text-to-3D、Image-to-3D和Assembly-3D。采用统一评测协议，从可执行性、几何保真度、拓扑结构、文本约束、多视图语义对齐和部件级结构六个维度对输出进行评分。评测对象包括前沿多模态大语言模型和纯文本LLM，使用400个文本案例、400个图像案例和203个标注装配体。

Result: 1) 3D装配是最难的任务，模型仍无法将多个部件组合成连贯结构；2) 模型通常能恢复目标物体的全局形状和语义身份，但无法再现输入指定的精确参数化几何；3) 部件级建模在装配任务上表现薄弱，模型既不能恢复每个部件的几何形状，也无法获得正确的部件数量。

Conclusion: P3D-Bench可作为评估参数化3D生成中精确参数化几何和部件级结构的基准。现有模型在全局形状恢复方面有进展，但在精确参数几何和部件级建模方面仍有显著不足，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models can write code to produce complex programs as well as use programs to do 3D modeling, which opens up a new avenue for 3D generation powered by their priors, world knowledge and reasoning. Yet existing benchmarks rarely evaluate 3D modeling through code. Such modeling demands more than runnable code: from a text or visual specification, a model must generate a parametric 3D program that is geometrically precise, semantically aligned and assembly-consistent. We introduce P3D-Bench, a benchmark for parametric 3D generation. Unlike a 3D mesh, a parametric 3D program exposes explicit dimensions, construction operations and part relations, revealing whether a model recovers a design's structure, not just its appearance. Under a unified protocol, P3D-Bench covers three task families (Text-to-3D, Image-to-3D and Assembly-3D) and scores each output for executability, geometric fidelity, topology, text-grounded constraints, multiview semantic alignment and part-level structure. We evaluate frontier MLLMs and text-only LLMs on 400 text cases, 400 image cases and 203 annotated assemblies, with domain-specific models as reference points. Our extensive evaluation yields three findings. First, assemblies are the hardest setting, where models still fail to compose multiple parts into a coherent structure. Second, models can often recover the global shape and semantic identity of the target object, yet fail to reproduce the precise parametric geometry specified by the input. Third, part-level modeling remains weak on assemblies, where models recover neither the geometry of each part nor the right number of parts. These results position P3D-Bench as a benchmark for evaluating precise parametric geometry and part-level structure in parametric 3D generation.

</details>


### [90] [Mean Flow Distillation: Robust and Stable Distillation for Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11155)
*An Zhao,Shengyuan Zhang,Zhongjian Sun,Yixiang Zhou,Zejian Li,Ling Yang,Tianrun Chen,Lingyun Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出均值流蒸馏（MFD），一种针对流匹配模型的新型蒸馏框架。理论证明MFD可作为时间低通滤波器，抑制变分分数蒸馏中的高频优化噪声，并证明均值流匹配定理——匹配期望平均速度足以实现严格分布对齐。在4D占用预测和文本到图像生成任务上，MFD实现了最先进的单步生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配模型依赖ODE迭代采样导致推理开销大，现有蒸馏方法多从扩散模型借鉴，未能利用流的内在几何结构，存在训练不稳定、高方差和生成质量退化问题。

Method: 提出均值流蒸馏（MFD），作为时间低通滤波器抑制VSD中的高频噪声，并证明均值流匹配定理，表明匹配期望平均速度足以保证严格分布对齐。

Result: 在4D占用预测和文本到图像生成等高维流形任务上取得最先进性能，实现高保真单步生成。

Conclusion: MFD通过理论驱动的蒸馏框架有效解决了流匹配模型的推理效率与生成质量之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Flow Matching models have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of generative tasks. However, their reliance on ODE-based iterative sampling incurs substantial computational overhead in inference, which limits their applicability in real-time scenes. While distillation is a promising solution, existing approaches largely borrow from diffusion-based score matching, often failing to exploit the intrinsic geometric structure of flows and suffering from training instability, high variance, and degraded generation quality. In this paper, we propose Mean Flow Distillation (MFD), a novel distillation framework tailored for flow matching models. We theoretically demonstrate that MFD acts as a temporal low-pass filter, effectively suppressing the high-frequency optimization noise inherent in variational score distillation (VSD) while ensuring global trajectory consistency. We further prove the Mean Flow Matching Theorem, establishing that matching expected average velocities is sufficient for strict distribution alignment. Empirically, on challenging tasks of high-dimensional manifolds including 4D occupancy forecasting and text-to-image generation, MFD achieves state-of-the-art performance, enabling high-fidelity single-step generation.

</details>


### [91] [Data Journalist Agent: Transforming Data into Verifiable Multimodal Stories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11176)
*Kevin Qinghong Lin,Batu EI,Yuhong Shi,Pan Lu,Philip Torr,James Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Data2Story是一个多智能体框架，将不同专业角色组织成一个虚拟新闻编辑室，能够端到端地完成数据新闻工作：从数据分析和事实核查，到生成带有交互式地图、音频等多模态元素的叙事文章。在18篇文章的评估中，Data2Story在证据可追溯性和透明度方面表现出色，但在编辑角度、创意设计和呈现上仍逊于人类记者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agent在数据科学分析或设计生成等单个步骤上表现良好，但缺乏一个能够端到端完成数据新闻全流程的统一智能体。数据新闻制作周期长、环节多，需要一个能整合分析、事实核查、角度选择、视觉设计等各环节的系统化方案。

Method: 提出Data2Story多智能体框架，核心创新包括：(i) 证据溯源机制——Inspector角色将每个数字、角度和素材与数据、代码或外部引用关联；(ii) 多模态生成——根据读者需求推理，自动选择交互式地图、音频等多模态工具生成内容。评估采用18篇配对文章，从人机角度覆盖度、五维度53人评分、计算机使用agent作为读者导航代理、以及编码验证器核查声明四个维度进行。

Result: Data2Story能够生成具有竞争力、证据可追溯的多媒体故事，在透明度和可审计性方面表现突出。人类文章在编辑角度、创意设计和呈现方面仍保持优势。

Conclusion: Data2Story可作为记者的协作工具，帮助实现更基于证据、更透明、更可验证的新闻报道。

Abstract: Data tells stories that shape society; the data journalist's job is to turn raw information into stories non-experts can trust. A high-quality news feature takes a newsroom team weeks: hunting for context, running statistics, choosing an angle, and designing visuals. Recent agents handle individual steps well: data-science agents close the analysis loop, while design agents synthesize beautiful websites. But can an agent serve as a data journalist end to end? We introduce Data Journalist Agent (Data2Story), a multi-agent framework that orchestrates specialized roles into a single virtual newsroom. Data2Story contributes two innovations. (i) Claims are evidence-grounded: an Inspector links every number, angle, and asset back to data, code, or an external reference. (ii) Articles are multimodally generative: rather than defaulting to plain text and static charts, Data2Story reasons about what readers will want to see, then deploys multimodal tools, such as interactive maps for geography and audio for music. We evaluate Data2Story on 18 articles, each paired with the originally published expert piece, along four axes: (a) human-agent angle coverage; (b) rubric evaluation with 53 participants across five dimensions; (c) computer-use agents as judges, a cost-saving proxy for how readers navigate interactive articles; and (d) verifiability, where a coding verifier re-executes statements against the data and checks claims against references. Data2Story produces competitive, evidence-traceable multimedia stories, with particular strength in transparency and auditability. Human articles retain an edge in editorial angle, creative design, and presentation. We position Data2Story as a collaborator for journalists, enabling more evidence-based, transparent, and verifiable reporting. Code and demos are available at https://data2story.github.io.

</details>


### [92] [Lip Forcing: Few-Step Autoregressive Diffusion for Real-time Lip Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11180)
*Paul Hyunbin Cho,Jinhyuk Jang,SeokYoung Lee,Joungbin Lee,Siyoon Jin,Heeseong Shin,Jung Yi,Yunjin Park,Chulmin Park,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Lip Forcing，首个自回归扩散唇同步方法，通过将14B双向扩散教师模型蒸馏为因果学生模型，实现实时视频到视频唇同步，速度提升17.6-39.8倍，时间到首帧低于1毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散的唇同步模型虽然视觉质量和音视频对齐效果好，但全序列双向注意力和多步去噪导致无法实时推理，需要高效方法。

Method: 将14B音频条件双向视频扩散教师蒸馏为因果学生模型；通过教师轨迹分析发现CFG保真度-同步权衡；提出三组件：Sync-Window DMD、两步推理调度、SyncNet奖励。

Result: 1.3B学生模型实现31 FPS实时流式推理，比同等规模双向模型快17.6倍；14B学生模型比教师快39.8倍，保真度相当；时间到首帧低于1毫秒。

Conclusion: Lip Forcing是首个自回归扩散V2V唇同步方法，通过蒸馏和针对性设计在保持质量的同时实现实时推理，验证了大规模扩散模型的实时部署可行性。

Abstract: Diffusion-based lip synchronization models achieve strong visual quality and audio-visual alignment, but full-sequence bidirectional attention and many denoising steps make them impractical for real-time inference. We present Lip Forcing, to our knowledge the first autoregressive diffusion method for video-to-video (V2V) lip synchronization, which distills a 14B audio-conditioned bidirectional video diffusion teacher into causal students. At inference, the students generate each chunk in only two denoising steps without inference-time CFG, enabling real-time lip synchronization. A lip-sync-specific teacher-trajectory analysis reveals a CFG fidelity-sync tradeoff: no-CFG predictions favor reference fidelity, whereas CFG-guided predictions favor synchronization within a mid-trajectory band. Lip Forcing translates this finding into three analysis-derived components: Sync-Window DMD, a two-step inference schedule, and a SyncNet-based reward. We validate Lip Forcing at two student scales, both distilled from the 14B teacher. The 1.3B student crosses into real-time streaming at 31 FPS, $17.6\times$ faster than its same-scale bidirectional model. The 14B student, the largest diffusion model reported for V2V lip synchronization, runs $39.8\times$ faster than its teacher at comparable reference fidelity. Time-to-first-frame is sub-millisecond at both scales, far below every diffusion baseline.

</details>


### [93] [AnyMod-LLVE: Low-Light Video Enhancement with Modality-Agnostic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11186)
*Hangfeng Liang,Yutao Hu,Yanhan Hu,Xiaohan Wu,Wenqi Shao,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AMNet是一个统一的多模态低光视频增强框架，通过空间-光谱双门控转换器学习辅助模态与RGB输入的对应关系，在推理时即使辅助模态缺失也能生成隐式辅助表示进行鲁棒增强，并支持任意模态组合推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低光视频增强的多模态方法在推理时假设辅助模态（如事件流、红外图像）始终可用，但这在真实场景中往往不成立，因此需要一种在模态缺失情况下仍能有效增强的灵活框架。

Method: 提出AMNet统一多模态框架，包含：(1) 空间-光谱双门控转换器，学习辅助模态与RGB输入的对应关系，生成隐式辅助表示；(2) 基于RGB-only数据集合成辅助模态进行大规模多模态预训练，促进跨模态对应学习。

Result: AMNet能够处理任意推理时模态组合，在模态缺失条件下展现出优越的低光视频增强性能。

Conclusion: 通过隐式辅助表示生成和大规模多模态预训练，AMNet有效解决了推理时辅助模态不可用的问题，实现了灵活且鲁棒的低光视频增强。

Abstract: Low-light video enhancement (LLVE) remains a challenging task due to severe information degradation under low-illumination conditions. Recent multimodal approaches have significantly improved enhancement performance by incorporating auxiliary modalities, such as event streams and infrared images. However, these methods typically assume the availability of these modalities at inference, which is often not feasible in real-world scenarios. To solve this problem, in this work, we propose AMNet, a unified multimodal framework for LLVE, to support flexible modality-agnostic inference, where auxiliary modalities may be unavailable. To address the issue of modality absence, we introduce a Spatial-Spectral Dual-Gated Translator that learns the correspondence between auxiliary modalities and RGB inputs, producing implicit auxiliary representations to support the robust enhancement. Additionally, to fully facilitate the learning of cross-modal correspondence, we conduct large-scale multimodal pretraining based on the RGB-only dataset with synthetic auxiliary modalities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AMNet could handle arbitrary inference-time modality combinations and exhibits superior performance for LLVE under modality absence conditions. Code and models are available on the project page.

</details>


### [94] [Next Forcing: Causal World Modeling with Multi-Chunk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11187)
*Gangwei Xu,Qihang Zhang,Jiaming Zhou,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Xin Yang,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Next Forcing框架，通过多块预测（MCP）训练目标，在自回归视频生成中实现更快的训练收敛、更高的精度和2倍推理加速，在多个基准上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频生成方法存在训练收敛慢、精度受限（尤其高帧率下）以及推理速度慢的问题，原因在于训练监督仅限于当前块而缺乏未来动态信号，且推理需要迭代去噪。

Method: 提出Next Forcing框架，采用多块预测（MCP）训练目标：在主模型上附加轻量级MCP模块，同时去噪多个未来时间视野的视频块（next^1, next^2, next^3），形成因果链，利用主模型多层融合的中间特征预测未来动态，提供密集的多尺度时序监督信号。

Result: 在50fps下，相比LingBot-VA，5k训练步数相对提升93.1%，收敛速度提升2.3倍；RoboTwin基准Clean/Random上达到94.1%/93.5% SOTA；实现2倍推理加速；PhyWorld上显著提升物理规律遵循能力；通用视频预训练上FVD降低超50%。

Conclusion: Next Forcing通过多块预测框架有效解决了自回归视频生成中训练慢、精度低、推理慢三大问题，为世界动作模型提供了一种高效且准确的因果世界建模方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive video generation has emerged as a powerful paradigm for World Action Models (WAMs). However, existing approaches suffer from slow training convergence and limited converged accuracy, particularly at high frame rates, as the training supervision is confined to the current chunk without explicit signals about future dynamics; they also suffer from slow inference due to iterative video denoising. In this paper, we present Next Forcing, a multi-chunk prediction (MCP) framework for causal world modeling that enables faster training, higher accuracy, and accelerated inference. Inspired by multi-token prediction in large language models, Next Forcing introduces an MCP training objective that augments the main model with lightweight auxiliary MCP modules to simultaneously denoise video chunks at multiple future temporal horizons (next$^1$, next$^2$, next$^3$ chunks). These MCP modules form a causal chain across prediction depths, where intermediate features fused from multiple layers of the main model are leveraged to predict future dynamics, allowing near-future predictions to inform farther-future ones and providing dense multi-scale temporal supervision back to the main model. During training, the MCP modules significantly accelerate convergence and improve converged accuracy, especially at high frame rates: at 50 fps, Next Forcing achieves a 93.1% relative improvement over LingBot-VA at 5k training steps and 2.3x faster convergence, and establishes new state-of-the-art results on the RoboTwin benchmark (94.1/93.5% on Clean/Random). At inference, the MCP modules can be retained to predict the next video chunk in parallel with the current one, achieving 2x inference acceleration. Next Forcing also demonstrates significant improvements on PhyWorld, a benchmark evaluating adherence to physical laws in video generation, and over 50% FVD reduction on general video pretraining.

</details>


### [95] [ARM: An AutoRegressive Large Multimodal Model with Unified Discrete Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11188)
*Junke Wang,Xiao Wang,Jiacheng Pan,Xuefeng Hu,Feng Li,Jingxiang Sun,Chaorui Deng,Zilong Chen,Yunpeng Chen,Kaibin Tian,Matthew Gwilliam,Hao Chen,Danhui Guan,Kun Xu,Weilin Huang,Zuxuan Wu,Haoqi Fan,Yu-Gang Jiang,Zhenheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ARM，一种基于离散语义视觉tokenizer的7B自回归模型，统一图像理解、生成和编辑在下一token预测框架中，并利用强化学习优化偏好对齐，显著提升多任务性能并展现跨任务协同效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉模型通常将理解、生成和编辑任务分开设计，缺乏统一的框架。本文旨在探索是否可以通过离散表示和自回归建模，在单一模型中同时支持多种视觉任务，并利用强化学习进一步优化偏好对齐行为。

Method: 1) 训练离散语义视觉tokenizer，通过多目标监督（语义判别性、语言对齐、忠实重建）将图像映射为紧凑token序列；2) 在大规模文本和图像token序列上训练7B自回归模型，融合视觉-语言感知与生成能力；3) 应用强化学习优化任务级目标（视觉质量、指令遵循、编辑一致性），实现偏好对齐。

Result: 强化学习显著提升目标任务性能：WISE总体从0.50提升至0.56，GEdit-Bench-EN G_O从5.75提升至6.68。此外，RL还诱导了文本到图像生成与编辑之间的跨任务协同效应。

Conclusion: 自回归建模结合强表示学习和偏好优化，可作为多模态智能的可扩展基础架构。

Abstract: This paper introduces ARM, a discrete representation-based AutoRegressive Model that unifies image understanding, generation, and editing within a next-token prediction framework. ARM is built on three efforts: first, we train a discrete semantic visual tokenizer that maps images into compact token sequences. Our tokenizer is supervised with multiple objectives that jointly promote semantic discriminability, language alignment and faithful reconstruction, thereby supporting diverse tasks in a shared latent space. With this, we train a 7B autoregressive model over large-scale text and image token sequences, seamlessly developing vision-language perception and generation capabilities. Finally, to further improve preference-aligned behavior for text-to-image generation and instruction-guided editing, ARM applies reinforcement learning (RL) to optimize task-level objectives such as visual quality, instruction adherence, and edit consistency. Surprisingly, the results show that RL not only substantially improves performance on the target tasks (e.g., raising WISE overall from 0.50 to 0.56, GEdit-Bench-EN G_O from 5.75 to 6.68), but also induces cross-task synergy between text-to-image generation and editing. Collectively, these findings highlight autoregressive modeling, when paired with strong representations and preference optimization, as a scalable foundation for multimodal intelligence. Code: https://github.com/wdrink/ARM.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Tractogram foundation model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09893)
*Guikun Chen,Yuqian Chen,Yijie Li,Yogesh Rathi,Nikos Makris,Fan Zhang,Wenguan Wang,Lauren J. O'Donnell*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: TractFM是一个直接从全脑流线集学习可复用表示的纤维束成像基础模型，结合局部流线编码器与置换等变纤维束图编码器，通过密集解剖纤维束分割预训练，同时生成流线级嵌入用于纤维束分割和紧凑的受试者级描述符用于表型预测，在多个数据集和任务上实现跨纤维束追踪流程的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将流线分类和受试者级预测视为分离问题：流线分类器只关注几何模式，而受试者级预测依赖手工特征，无法学习连接流线解剖学与全脑跨受试者变异的可复用表示。

Method: TractFM结合局部流线编码器与置换等变纤维束图编码器，在单次前向传播中联合上下文编码受试者的所有流线，通过密集解剖纤维束分割（为每条流线分配解剖标签）进行预训练。

Result: 在三种纤维束追踪算法和五个dMRI数据集上，TractFM的冻结表示实现了准确的纤维束分割，并能跨独立数据集预测年龄和性别，展示了跨纤维束追踪流程、数据集和预测任务的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 全脑几何上下文可一次性学习，并泛化到不同的纤维束追踪流程、数据集和预测任务中，证明纤维束图基础模型的有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion MRI (dMRI) tractography is the only noninvasive approach for mapping white-matter pathways in the living human brain. It represents each brain as a tractogram: a large, unordered set of three-dimensional streamlines that includes information about both local streamline geometry and whole-brain anatomical organization. This structure makes tractograms a natural but challenging target for representation learning. Existing methods treat streamline classification and subject-level prediction as separate problems: streamline classifiers focus on geometric patterns, whereas subject-level prediction often depends on hand-crafted features. As a result, current methods do not learn reusable representations that connect streamline anatomy with whole-brain inter-subject variation. Here we introduce TractFM, a tractogram foundation model that learns reusable representations directly from whole-brain streamline sets. TractFM combines a local streamline encoder with a permutation-equivariant tractogram encoder, allowing all streamlines from a subject to be contextualized jointly in a single forward pass. Pretraining on dense anatomical tract parcellation, i.e., assigning anatomical labels to individual streamlines, yields two complementary representations: contextualized streamline-level embeddings for tract parcellation and compact subject-level descriptors for downstream prediction of subject phenotypes. Across three tractography algorithms and five dMRI datasets, TractFM transfers to both streamline-level and subject-level tasks. Its frozen representations achieve accurate tract parcellation and predict age and sex across independent datasets. These results show that whole-brain geometric context, learned once, can generalize across tractography pipelines, datasets, and prediction tasks.

</details>


### [97] [Deep Slice Interpolation for Reducing Through-Plane Anisotropy and Noise in Head CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09953)
*Luis Cortés Ferre,Miguel A. Gutiérrez-Naranjo,Marcin Balcerzyk*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种深度学习系统，从头部CT相邻轴向切片对合成中间切片，将有效层间距减半，同时实现插值与隐式降噪的双重效果。系统性评估了MSE、L1、SSIM、MS-SSIM及其混合损失函数，发现MS-SSIM+L1表现最优，并分析了SSIM族损失的训练不稳定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头部CT通常具有亚毫米级面内分辨率但层间距为2-5毫米，这种各向异性严重影响了多平面重建、血肿体积测量等下游任务。现有方法无法同时解决插值和降噪问题，亟需一种既能提升分辨率又能降噪的可靠系统。

Method: 采用深度学习系统，利用相邻轴向CT切片对合成中间切片。系统性地对比了像素级损失（MSE、L1）、结构相似性损失（SSIM、MS-SSIM）及其混合组合，使用患者级bootstrap置信区间和配对统计检验进行评估。

Result: 所有收敛模型在所有结构指标上均优于经典插值基线和预训练视频帧插值方法（RIFE、FILM），其中MS-SSIM+L1混合损失在平衡性能上最优。发现SSIM族损失存在训练不稳定性问题，标准数值修复可消除主要失效模式，但小批量下仍有残余发散。

Conclusion: 该系统在头CT插值任务上显著优于传统方法，且具备隐式降噪能力。在外部数据集上的验证表明该方法的泛化性良好，插值质量和隐式降噪效果不局限于训练数据分布。

Abstract: Head computed tomography (CT) typically uses sub-millimeter in-plane resolution but 2-5 mm through-plane spacing, creating substantial anisotropy that degrades multiplanar reconstructions, volumetric measurements such as hematoma volume estimation, and downstream algorithms that assume near-isotropic voxels. We present a deep learning system that synthesizes intermediate CT slices from pairs of neighboring axial slices, halving the effective through-plane spacing. The system improves three-dimensional visualization while simultaneously producing inherently denoised outputs, yielding two complementary benefits from a single inference pass.
  To build a reliable system, we systematically evaluate pixel-wise losses, namely mean squared error (MSE) and mean absolute error (L1); structural-similarity losses, namely the structural similarity index (SSIM) and its multi-scale variant (MS-SSIM); and hybrid combinations. On a held-out test set, all converged models outperform classical interpolation baselines and pretrained video frame interpolation methods (RIFE, FILM) on all structural measures, with MS-SSIM+L1 offering the strongest balanced profile. We also document training instability in SSIM-family losses and identify partial remedies: the standard numerical fixes eliminate the dominant failure mode but leave residual divergence at smaller batch sizes. All results are reported with patient-level bootstrap confidence intervals and paired statistical tests.
  As an illustration, we apply the system to an out-of-distribution head CT series from Hospital Universitario Virgen del Rocío: the model synthesizes intermediate slices and exhibits on the real slices the implicit-denoising signature predicted by our theoretical analysis, supporting in a single external case that interpolation quality and implicit denoising are not confined to the training distribution.

</details>


### [98] [Laplace-Mixture Dipole Inversion for Quantitative Susceptibility Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10240)
*Shuai Huang,James J. Lah,Jason W. Allen,Deqiang Qiu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于双成分拉普拉斯混合先验的自动偶极子反演方法(LAMDI)，用于定量磁化率成像，无需手动调参即可保留精细解剖结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始AMP-PE框架使用单一拉普拉斯先验对图像梯度建模，无法充分捕捉脑磁化率图的重尾分布特性，导致过正则化和块状伪影。

Method: 将双成分拉普拉斯混合先验引入AMP-PE框架，形成LAMDI方法，实现自动参数估计。

Result: 在公共多方向QSM数据集上，LAMDI的NRMSE和SSIM与AMP-PE-L1相当，但HFEN显著降低（更好地保留高频细节）。在参考调优设置下虽不及FANSI和MEDI，但无需参考图和手动调参仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: LAMDI为QSM偶极子反演提供了一种有效的自动参数估计方案，兼具竞争性重建精度和更好的精细解剖结构保留能力。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop an automatic dipole inversion method for quantitative susceptibility mapping (QSM) that preserves fine anatomical structures without the need for manual regularization-parameter tuning.
  Theory: The original approximate message passing with parameter estimation (AMP-PE) framework models image gradients with a single Laplace prior, which does not fully capture the heavy-tailed gradient distribution of brain susceptibility maps. This prior mismatch can lead to over-regularization and blocky reconstructions. We address this limitation by modeling the gradients with a two-component Laplace mixture prior.
  Methods: We propose a Laplace-Mixture Dipole Inversion (LAMDI) method by incorporating a two-component Laplace mixture prior into the AMP-PE framework with automatic parameter estimation. LAMDI was evaluated on a public in vivo dataset. Its performance was compared with FANSI, MEDI, and AMP-PE with a single-Laplace prior (AMP-PE-L1) under both standard default and reference-tuned settings.
  Results: On a public multi-orientation QSM dataset, LAMDI achieved NRMSE and SSIM comparable to AMP-PE-L1 while substantially reducing HFEN, suggesting improved preservation of high-frequency anatomical detail. Under reference-based tuning, FANSI and MEDI achieved the best performance for some metrics, but LAMDI remained competitive without requiring reference maps or manual regularization tuning.
  Conclusion: LAMDI provides an effective and automatic parameter-estimation alternative for QSM dipole inversion by combining competitive reconstruction accuracy with improved preservation of fine anatomical detail.

</details>


### [99] [POPSICLE: Benchmark Datasets for Segmentation and Localization in CryoET](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10255)
*Jonathan Schwartz,Utz Heinrich Ermel,C. Braxton Owens,Zhuowen Zhao,Ariana Peck,Gus L. W. Hart,Grant J. Jensen,Bridget Carragher,Dari Kimanius*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: POPSICLE是一个面向冷冻电子断层扫描(cryoET)数据的分割和大分子定位基准测试套件，基于CryoET Data Portal构建，覆盖真核与原核系统、纯化与原位样本，以及密集体素分割和稀疏定位任务，旨在为机器学习方法提供标准化、可扩展的评估平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷冻电子断层扫描(cryoET)技术虽能直接可视化完整细胞内的分子结构，但其潜力的充分发挥依赖于机器学习计算分析的进步。然而，当前机器学习发展受限于缺乏标准化、良好标注的基准测试，现有评估通常规模小、任务特定且孤立构建，无法进行跨方法的稳健比较。

Method: 从CryoET Data Portal（一个开放、ML就绪的断层数据、元数据和标注库）构建POPSICLE基准测试套件，涵盖真核和原核系统、纯化和完全原位样本，以及密集体素分割和稀疏定位任务。基准基于动态数据资源构建，可随新数据集和标注的加入而扩展。

Result: 基线实验显示模型排名在任务间存在显著差异，表明需要针对cryoET独特特性定制的基准测试，而非直接改编自邻近生物医学成像领域的评估实践。

Conclusion: POPSICLE为cryoET中可重复的机器学习评估提供了开放且可扩展的基础，促进了该领域的标准化和稳健比较。

Abstract: Cryo-electron tomography (cryoET) has emerged as a powerful tool in structural and cellular biology by enabling direct visualization of macromolecular structures within intact cells, thereby linking molecular architecture to cellular organization in a native context. Realizing the full potential of cryoET, however, increasingly depends on advances in computational analysis, particularly machine learning (ML), to interpret its complex and information-rich data. Despite rapid progress, ML development for cryoET remains bottlenecked by the lack of standardized, well-annotated benchmarks. Existing evaluations are typically small, task-specific, and are assembled in isolation, limiting robust comparisons across methods. Here, we present POPSICLE, a benchmark suite for cryoET segmentation and macromolecular localization built from the CryoET Data Portal - an open, ML-ready repository of tomographic data, metadata, and annotations. POPSICLE spans eukaryotic and prokaryotic systems, both purified and fully in situ samples, and dense voxel-wise segmentation as well as sparse localization tasks. Built on a living data resource, it can expand as new datasets and annotations become available. Baseline experiments reveal substantial variation in model rankings across tasks, underscoring the need for benchmarks tailored to the unique characteristics of cryoET rather than evaluation practices adapted from adjacent biomedical imaging domains. POPSICLE thus provides an open and extensible foundation for reproducible ML evaluation in cryoET.

</details>


### [100] [Overlapped Wavelet Diffusion for Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10280)
*Fen Peng,Taizo Suzuki,Seisuke Kyochi*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种重叠小波扩散框架（OWDiff），通过重叠小波变换消除分块伪影，并利用低频引导的高频增强模块恢复细节，在低光照图像增强任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式低光照图像增强方法（如DiffLL）存在两个问题：Haar小波变换导致的分块伪影，以及高频恢复模块（HFRM）导致的边缘模糊和纹理过度平滑。

Method: 1）提出重叠小波变换（OWT），通过引入相邻区域的相关性来结构性防止分块伪影；2）设计低频引导的高频增强块（HFEBlock），强化细节恢复，生成更锐利的边缘和更可靠的纹理。

Result: 在LOLv1和LOLv2-real数据集上，相比DiffLL，PSNR平均提升0.58 dB，SSIM相对提升1.64%，LPIPS相对降低5.9%，同时保持计算效率。

Conclusion: OWDiff有效解决了Haar小波变换和高频恢复模块的结构性局限，在定性和定量上均优于现有低光照图像增强方法。

Abstract: In this study, we propose an overlapped wavelet diffusion framework for Low-Light Image Enhancement (LLIE), which incorporates two complementary components to achieve blocking artifact-free and detail-preserving enhancement. Although recent diffusion-based LLIE methods have demonstrated remarkable performance compared with traditional approaches, DiffLL still suffers from blocking artifacts caused by the Haar Wavelet Transform (WT) and blurred edges or over-smoothed textures due to the limitations of its High-Frequency Restoration Module (HFRM). To overcome these issues, we introduce an Overlapped WT (OWT) that incorporates correlations across neighboring regions, thereby structurally preventing blocking artifacts. Furthermore, we integrate a low-frequency-guided High-Frequency Enhance Block (HFEBlock) to strengthen detail recovery, yielding sharper edges and more reliable textures. Extensive experiments on the LOLv1 and LOLv2-real datasets demonstrate that our framework, termed OWDiff, consistently outperforms existing LLIE methods both qualitatively and quantitatively, achieving superior visual quality while maintaining computational efficiency. OWDiff effectively addresses the structural limitations of the Haar WT and the HFRM, achieving an average PSNR gain of 0.58 dB, along with a 1.64% relative improvement in SSIM and a 5.9% relative reduction in LPIPS, compared to DiffLL across both the LOLv1 and LOLv2-real datasets.

</details>


### [101] [Unsupervised Deep Learning for Limited-Angle STEM-EDX Tomography -- Application to 3D Chemical Analysis of Phase-Change Memory Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10547)
*Daniel del Pozo Bueno,Serge Brosset,Theo Monniez,Gabriele Navarro,Philippe Ciuciu,Zineb Saghi*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度图像先验与全变分正则化的无监督深度学习框架（DIP-TV及其多通道扩展DIPm-TV），用于解决有限角度STEM-EDX层析成像中的缺失楔伪影和噪声问题。在合成数据上该方法优于SIRT和压缩感知方法，并在GST存储器件真实数据上实现了近各向同性空间分辨率的3D元素重构，揭示了器件操作引起的成分异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STEM-EDX层析成像受限于有限倾斜角度和低剂量条件，导致缺失楔伪影（如拉长和各向异性分辨率）和噪声退化，限制了3D成分与元素图谱的定量重构质量。现有方法在严重角度限制下失效，亟需无需外部结构先验且能处理多元素空间关联的重建方法。

Method: 提出无监督深度学习框架DIP-TV，基于深度图像先验结合全变分正则化；进一步扩展为多通道版本DIPm-TV，利用多元素图谱间的空间相关性进行联合重建。在合成3通道幻影上验证性能，并与SIRT和压缩感知方法对比；应用于GST存储器件（原始态和SET态）的有限角度（-40°至+40°，步长5°）实验数据。

Result: DIP-TV/DIPm-TV在合成数据上能补偿约100°缺失角度范围引起的严重缺失楔伪影，且性能优于SIRT和压缩感知方法。在GST器件真实数据上实现了近各向同性的空间分辨率，成功重建了仅基于EDX信号的逐体素元素分布，揭示了器件操作导致的成分异质性。

Conclusion: 该无监督深度学习框架能在严重角度限制和低噪声条件下实现高质量3D元素重构，无需外部结构先验（如HAADF成像），为实验上难以实现全角度扫描的样品几何提供了可行的3D化学表征方案。

Abstract: Energy Dispersive X-ray (EDX) tomography in Scanning Transmission Electron Microscopy (STEM) enables 3D compositional and elemental mapping at the nanoscale, but its use is limited by restricted tilt ranges and low-dose conditions required to avoid beam damage. Limited-angle acquisition introduces missing-wedge artefacts such as elongation and anisotropic resolution, while noisy low-dose data further degrade reconstruction quality and quantitative reliability. Here, we introduce an unsupervised deep learning framework based on Deep Image Prior with total variation regularization (DIP-TV) for limited-angle STEM-EDX tomography. We extend it to a multi-channel formulation (DIPm-TV) that jointly reconstructs multiple elemental maps by exploiting spatial correlations. Using a synthetic 3-channel phantom, we show that the method compensates for severe missing-wedge artefacts corresponding to approximately $100^\circ$ of missing angular range under moderate noise, outperforming simultaneous iterative reconstruction technique and compressed sensing approaches. We apply the method to 3D chemical analysis of Ge-Sb-Te (GST) memory devices in virgin (as-fabricated) and SET (crystalline) operational states. Samples were prepared as cross-sectional focused ion beam lamellae and acquired under a limited-angle tilt range from $-40^\circ$ to $+40^\circ$ with $5^\circ$ steps and a dose of $2.0\times10^5$ $e^-/Ang^2$. The multi-channel approach enables voxel-by-voxel elemental reconstruction using only EDX signals without external structural priors such as high-angle annular dark-field imaging. The reconstructed volumes show near-isotropic spatial resolution and reveal compositional heterogeneities associated with device operation. This approach enables 3D chemical characterization in experimentally accessible sample geometries where conventional methods fail due to severe angular limitations.

</details>


### [102] [++nnU-Net: Scaling nnU-Net with Prefix-Based Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10713)
*Ana Sofia Santos,André Ferreira,Gijs Luijten,Naida Solak,Lisle Faray de Paiva,Behrus Hinrichs-Puladi,Jens Kleesiek,Jan Egger,Victor Alves*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出++nnU-Net，一种基于图像配准的新型数据增强模块，在预处理和训练前对2D医学图像进行两阶段配准生成形变图像，在五个2D数据集上相比基线nnU-Net提升了Dice分数，最高提升约22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学分割任务严重依赖标注数据的多样性和可用性，但受限于隐私法规和标注成本，医疗影像数据收集困难。数据增强虽能增加数据量，但需要保持解剖学可行性，现有方法存在不足。

Method: 提出++nnU-Net，在预处理和训练前引入基于图像配准的数据增强模块。工作流程包括：两阶段配准过程生成新形变图像，将变换应用于对应分割图，此外还计算可用磁盘空间、生成辅助二进制合成掩膜和检查点。

Result: 在五个不同2D数据集上评估，++nnU-Net优于nnU-Net基线，Dice相似系数提升，最显著案例中性能提升约22%。

Conclusion: 基于配准的数据增强对2D医学影像数据集有效，++nnU-Net为数据受限场景下提升分割性能提供了一种实用且可扩展的方法。

Abstract: The nnU-Net has demonstrated continuous success in medical segmentation tasks, which heavily rely on the availability and diversity of annotated biomedical data. However, assembling medical imaging cohorts remains challenging due to numerous factors such as privacy regulations and annotation costs. As a result, data augmentation plays a crucial role in increasing data availability while maintaining anatomical feasibility. Hence, we propose the ++nnU-Net, a novel data augmentation module based on image registration that operates prior to preprocessing and training take place. Our framework was evaluated across five different 2D datasets. In this workflow, image data go through a two-stage registration process, generating new warped images. The transformations are then applied to the respective segmentation. In addition, the pipeline computes available disk space, generates supplementary binary synthetic masks and generates checkpoints. We demonstrate that the ++nnU-Net outperforms the nnU-Net baseline, yielding improvements in Dice Similarity Coefficient scores. In the most prominent cases, we observe performance gains of approximately 22\%. These findings highlight the effectiveness of registration-based data augmentation, particularly for 2D medical imaging datasets and suggest that the ++nnU-Net provides a practical and scalable approach for enhancing segmentation performance in data-limited settings. The source code for the ++nnU-Net is available at: https://github.com/sofia-adelie/plusplusnnunet.git

</details>


### [103] [Low-Dose 3D Bonding Mapping Through "Soft" Core-Loss EELS Tomography and Unsupervised Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10893)
*Mario Pelaez-Fernandez,Daniel del-Pozo-Bueno,Adrien Teurtrie,Serge Brosset,Maya Marinova,Phillipe Ciuciu,Marta Estrader,German Salazar-Alvarez,Francesca Peiró,Raul Arenal,Sonia Estradé,Zineb Saghi,Francisco De la Peña*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合"软"芯损失EELS方法（利用Fe-M₂₃边，剂量效率比传统Fe-L₂₃边高约50倍）和多通道深度图像先验与全变分正则化（DIPm-TV）的无监督光谱层析成像方法，实现了高分辨率、低剂量下的FeO/Fe₃O₄核壳纳米立方体3D化学键合映射，仅需9个投影即可获得约1 nm各向同性分辨率的氧化态体积重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决扫描透射电子显微镜（STEM）中对束流敏感纳米材料进行高空间分辨率3D化学构型解析时，分析信号所需的高电子剂量与层析重建所需的大量投影之间的权衡问题。

Method: 1) 采用标准less "软"芯损失EELS方法，利用Fe-M₂₃边代替Fe-L₂₃边，实现约50倍的剂量效率提升；2) 提出多通道深度图像先验与全变分正则化（DIPm-TV）的无监督光谱层析成像方法，在稀疏视角和低剂量条件下联合重建多个通道，利用空间相关性。

Result: 仅需9个投影（-70°到+70°）即可获得高质量重建，无需HAADF-STEM信号或对称性约束。对FeO/Fe₃O₄纳米立方体的实验显示，Fe-M₂₃ EELS图信噪比和空间分辨率显著提升，揭示了薄的外层FeO壳层；DIPm-TV实现了约1 nm各向同性分辨率的氧化态体积重建，恢复了外层FeO壳层和内部小空隙，这些是传统方法无法获得的。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了利用浅层芯损失边对束流敏感材料进行低剂量2D和3D分析映射的途径，可在保持光谱保真度和可靠3D信息的同时实现数量级的剂量降低。

Abstract: Resolving the 3D chemical configuration of beam-sensitive nanomaterials at high spatial resolution remains a persistent frontier in scanning transmission electron microscopy (STEM). The main limitation lies in the trade-off between high electron dose required for analytical signals and the large number of projections needed for tomographic reconstruction. Here, we achieve dose-efficient 3D bonding mapping of FeO/Fe$_3$O$_4$ core-shell nanocubes with high resolution via electron energy loss spectroscopy (EELS). Our approach relies on two developments. First, a standardless "soft" core-loss EELS methodology exploiting Fe-M$_{2,3}$ edges provides ${\sim}50\times$ higher dose efficiency than conventional Fe-L$_{2,3}$ edges, using the latter only as a source of FeO and Fe$_3$O$_4$ standards. Second, we introduce multi-channel deep image prior with total variation regularization (DIPm-TV), an unsupervised method for spectroscopic tomography that jointly reconstructs multiple channels by exploiting spatial correlations under sparse-view and low-dose conditions. Using simulated datasets, high-quality reconstructions are obtained from as few as nine projections over $-70^\circ$ to $+70^\circ$, without HAADF-STEM signal or symmetry constraints. Applied to FeO/Fe$_3$O$_4$ nanocubes, Fe-M$_{2,3}$ EELS maps show improved SNR and spatial resolution, revealing a thin outer FeO shell surrounding the magnetite shell. DIPm-TV yields ${\sim}1$ nm isotropic resolution oxidation-state volumes preserving cubic morphology, recovering the outer FeO shell, and revealing a small internal void, features not accessible with conventional reconstruction methods. This work establishes a pathway for low-dose 2D and 3D analytical mapping of beam-sensitive materials using shallow core-loss edges, enabling orders-of-magnitude dose reduction while maintaining spectral fidelity and reliable 3D information.

</details>


### [104] [Multimodal Brain Tumour Classification Using Feature Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11107)
*Wajih ul Islam,Muhammad Yaqoob,Javed Ali Khan,Volker Steuber*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出双分支多模态网络，融合MRI图像与91项影像组学特征进行脑肿瘤分类，采用门控融合策略达到96.13%的准确率，优于单模态基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型仅依赖MRI/CT图像，未能像临床医生那样综合多模态信息（症状、病史、影像数据）进行诊断，缺乏多模态推理能力。

Method: 构建双分支网络：一支使用预训练CNN编码MRI图像，另一支使用MLP编码91项影像组学特征（强度、纹理、形状、边界描述符）。两支路通过拼接、门控或双向交叉模态注意力机制进行融合，分类脑肿瘤为胶质瘤、脑膜瘤、垂体瘤或无肿瘤。

Result: 在7,200张平衡图像数据集上进行9次实验，所有多模态配置均优于单模态基线，门控融合策略取得最佳准确率96.13%。

Conclusion: 融合影像组学特征与图像的多模态方法能更有效模拟临床医生的综合诊断推理过程，显著提升脑肿瘤分类性能。

Abstract: Clinicians diagnose brain tumors by synthesizing patient symptoms, medical history, and quantitative imaging data from modalities such as MRI and CT scans into a unified clinical judgement. However, most deep learning models rely on MRI/CT images alone, failing to replicate the clinicians multimodal reasoning. We explore a two-branch multimodal network combining raw MRI scans with 91 extracted radiomic features (intensity, texture, shape, and boundary descriptors) to classify brain tumors into glioma, meningioma, pituitary, and no-tumor. A pre-trained CNN backbone encodes the image stream, whereas a dedicated MLP encodes the radiomic stream. Both streams are fused via concatenation, gated, or bidirectional cross-modal attention strategies. Across nine experimental runs on a balanced 7,200 image dataset, all multimodal configurations outperform unimodal baselines with gated fusion achieving the best accuracy of 96.13%.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Uncertainty-Aware Motion Planning for Autonomous Driving in Mixed Traffic Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09958)
*Ming Cheng,Hao Chen,Ziyi Yang,Ziluowen Luo,Senzhang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出UAMP框架，通过量化人类意图不确定性并校正值函数学习偏差，提升混合交通场景中自动驾驶运动规划的安全性和舒适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法将预测的人类意图视为确定性状态，忽略了人类意图固有的不确定性（行为多样性、感知噪声、部分可观测性），可能导致自动驾驶车辆做出不安全决策。

Method: 1) 邻近感知不确定性估计器，量化交互条件下的意图不确定性；2) 构建不确定性引导的联合意图分布；3) 不确定性校准值学习(UCVL)，校正因直接引入不确定人类意图预测而导致的值函数学习偏差。

Result: 在多种混合交通场景下的实验表明，与现有方法相比，UAMP显著提升了安全性和驾驶舒适性，同时保持了交通效率。

Conclusion: 量化人类意图不确定性并据此校正决策学习过程，是提升混合交通场景下自动驾驶安全性和舒适性的有效途径。

Abstract: In mixed-traffic environments where autonomous and human-driven vehicles may co-exist, motion planning for autonomous vehicles requires anticipating the future behaviors of surrounding human drivers. Existing reinforcement learning-based methods generally directly incorporate the predicted human intents into the observation to enable a proactive planning. However, human intent is inherently uncertain due to the behavioral diversity, perception noise, and partial observability. Treating predicted intends as deterministic states can result in unsafe decisions for autonomous vehicles. To address this problem, we propose Uncertainty-Aware Motion Planning (UAMP), which incorporates uncertainty in human intent prediction for AV decision-making. Specifically, UAMP first introduces a proximity-aware uncertainty estimator to quantify the interaction-conditioned intent uncertainty and constructs an uncertainty-guided joint intent distribution over surrounding human-driven vehicles. Within this uncertainty set, UAMP further introduces Uncertainty-Calibrated Value Learning (UCVL) to correct value function learning biases arising from directly incorporating uncertain human intent predictions into the observation. Extensive experiments in various mixed-traffic scenarios show that UAMP significantly improves safety and driving comfort, while maintaining traffic efficiency compared with existing approaches. The code is released at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/UAMP-5638.

</details>


### [106] [GHOST: Hierarchical Sub-Goal Policies for Generalizing Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10025)
*Sriram Krishna,Ben Eisner,Haotian Zhan,Ying Yuan,Haoyu Zhen,Chuang Gan,Shubham Tulsiani,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GHOST提出了一种分层框架，通过将控制分解为高层策略（从多视角RGB-D观测中预测3D末端执行器姿态的子目标分布）和低层目标条件控制器（执行具体动作），实现了超越训练分布的视觉运动操控策略泛化。该框架通过将3D目标投影到图像平面并表示为末端执行器热图的简单空间接口，以及利用子目标的跨 embodiment 特性在高层策略中融入人类演示，显著提升了操作任务的性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
