Motivation: 传统逆最优控制（IOC）方法依赖双层优化，需反复求解嵌套的最优控制问题，计算成本极高；而近期基于投影的方法虽提供了替代方案，但使用梯度方法求解时因违反标准约束规范而导致数值不稳定。因此，需要一种更高效、更稳定的IOC方法。

Method: 论文揭示了IOC可行集的几何结构——满足最优性条件的轨迹集合自然构成一个流形；在此基础上，将IOC重新表述为该流形上的优化问题，并提出了黎曼逆最优控制（RIOC）方法，该方法将观测轨迹投影到最优解流形上，同时通过构造保证可行性。

Result: 在真实人体手臂轨迹上的实验表明，RIOC方法在重构精度上与经典双层IOC相当或更优，同时计算时间减少约四倍。

Conclusion: 几何优化方法有潜力提升IOC在机器人学和人体运动分析中的可扩展性与可靠性。

Abstract: Inverse Optimal Control (IOC) aims to recover the cost function that explains observed trajectories as solutions of an optimal control problem. Classical IOC formulations rely on bilevel optimization, which repeatedly solves a nested optimal control problem and quickly becomes computationally prohibitive for realistic systems. Recent projection-based approaches offer a promising alternative but suffer from numerical instability when solved with gradient-based methods due to violations of standard constraint qualifications.
  In this paper, we show that these difficulties stem from the geometric structure of the IOC feasible set. We demonstrate that the set of trajectories satisfying the optimality conditions naturally forms a manifold and reformulate IOC as an optimization problem on this manifold. Based on this insight, we propose a Riemannian Inverse Optimal Control (RIOC) method that projects observed trajectories onto the manifold of optimal solutions while preserving feasibility by construction. Experiments on real human arm trajectories show that the proposed method achieves comparable or better reconstruction accuracy than classical bilevel IOC while reducing computation time by about a factor of four. These results highlight the potential of geometric optimization methods to improve the scalability and reliability of IOC for robotics and human motion analysis.

</details>


### [144] [An Exposure-Time-Aligned Primary-Path Architecture for Autonomous-Driving ECUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10856)
*Toru Saito,Yuki Hagura,Tatsuya Konishi,Satoru Mizusawa,Takumi Yajima*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向生产级自动驾驶的混合架构设计，在模块化多NN流水线与端到端（E2E）路径之间实现共存与渐进迁移，通过主路径优先、曝光时间对齐、共路径共存三条原则，在量产双SoC域控制器上将延迟控制在296ms（预算350ms），并支持从模块化主路径向E2E路径的分阶段替换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在端到端自动驾驶成为主流研究方向的背景下，量产车仍处于依赖模块化多NN管线的过渡期。现有平等后融合架构在量产SoC上计算效率低下，且无法为逐步替换为E2E路径提供自然单元，因此需要一种既能支持模块化与E2E路径共存、又能实现分阶段迁移的架构设计。

Method: 提出三条设计原则：(i) 主路径优先——明确选择一条主感知链，优先将其封装在单一SoC对中；(ii) 曝光时间对齐——将主传感器的曝光时间τ_exp作为标签沿链路传播，融合节点基于匹配的τ_exp事件驱动而非固定周期触发；(iii) 共路径共存——基于(i)(ii)，让E2E输出路径在同一τ_exp周期内与模块化管线共同运行。

Result: 在量产双SoC自动驾驶域控制器上实现相机快门到规划器输出的平均延迟为296ms，满足350ms设计预算。在共路径共存模式下，模块化管线作为生产主路径，E2E路径作为影子模式在实车上运行，E2E范围随评估证据积累逐步扩大。

Conclusion: 该架构在量产硬件上验证了模块化与E2E路径共存及渐进迁移的可行性，为自动驾驶从模块化向E2E过渡提供了实用的工程化方案。

Abstract: While end-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving has become the dominant research direction, production vehicles continue to rely on modular multi-NN pipelines for a non-trivial transitional period. The subject of this paper is the design of an architecture that, during this phase, supports a modular pipeline and an E2E path side by side and embeds a path for staged migration. Transplanted to a production SoC, egalitarian late fusion is compute-inefficient and offers no natural unit for staged E2E substitution. As an alternative, we propose three design principles: (i) Primary-Path, which explicitly selects a primary perception chain and prioritizes its enclosure within a single SoC pair over the non-critical paths (ii) Exposure-Time-Aligned, which propagates the primary sensor's exposure time $τ_{\rm exp}$ as a tag along the chain and event-drives the fusion node on matched $τ_{\rm exp}$ rather than a fixed cycle and (iii) Co-Path Coexistence, which, building on (i) and (ii), lets an E2E output path co-run with the modular pipeline within the same $τ_{\rm exp}$ cycle. On a Dual-SoC production AD-ECU, the implementation closes camera-shutter to planner-output latency at a mean of 296 ms within the 350 ms design budget. Under (iii), the modular pipeline is primary at production launch and the E2E path runs as shadow on real vehicles, and the E2E scope is expanded as evaluation evidence accumulates.

</details>


### [145] [Embodiment-conditioned Generalist Control for Multirotor Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10857)
*Orestis Konstantaropoulos,Welf Rehberg,Mihir Kulkarni,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用位置控制策略，能够使用单一网络权重控制任意多旋翼构型（如六旋翼、四旋翼）。该策略基于物理基础的形态描述符——质量与惯性归一化的控制分配矩阵，并通过PPO算法在广泛的多旋翼构型分布上训练。训练仅需在RTX 3090 GPU上花费5分钟。实验证明，形态条件化实现了跨任意形态的鲁棒通用控制，并在三种不同的六旋翼系统上实现了零样本真实世界迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多旋翼控制策略通常针对特定构型设计，缺乏泛化能力，无法在任意多旋翼形态（包括非平面、非对称系统）之间迁移。本文旨在开发一种通用控制策略，能够以单一网络权重适应多种多旋翼构型。

Method: 使用质量与惯性归一化的控制分配矩阵作为形态描述符，该矩阵捕捉电机推力如何在机体坐标系中产生线性和角加速度。从广泛的多旋翼构型分布中采样，包括非平面和非对称系统，使用近端策略优化（PPO）训练单一紧凑网络。利用基于NVIDIA Warp的自定义动力学模拟器加速训练。

Result: 在广泛仿真实验中，形态条件化实现了跨任意形态的鲁棒通用控制。在三种不同的六旋翼系统（平面机器人、部分对称非平面系统、随机非对称非平面系统）上实现了零样本真实世界迁移。

Conclusion: 提出的通用位置控制策略能够有效泛化至任意多旋翼构型，训练高效且支持零样本迁移到真实世界不同形态的机器人上。

Abstract: We present a generalist position control policy capable of controlling arbitrary multirotor configurations of a certain rotor count (e.g., hexarotors or quadrotors) with a single set of network weights. The policy is conditioned on a physics-grounded embodiment descriptor: a mass and inertia-normalized control allocation matrix that captures how mass-normalized motor thrusts generate linear and angular accelerations in the body-frame. To train the policy, we sample from a broad distribution of arbitrary multirotor configurations, including non-planar and asymmetric systems, and optimize a single, compact network using Proximal Policy Optimization. Training requires only five minutes on an RTX 3090 GPU using a custom NVIDIA Warp-based dynamics simulator. Through extensive simulation experiments, we show that embodiment conditioning enables robust generalist control across arbitrary morphologies. We demonstrate zero-shot real-world transfer of this generalist policy on three diverse hexarotor systems, including a planar robot, a partially symmetric non-planar system, and a random asymmetric, non-planar configuration.

</details>


### [146] [MV-Actor: Aligning Multi-View Semantics and Spatial Awareness for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10899)
*Yinchen Tian,Huan Li,Muyao Peng,Xi Wang,Yan Wang,You Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MV-Actor，一种面向双臂操作的多视角感知框架，通过多视角语义交互、语义-空间Token交互和引导式度量深度修复模块，在PerAct2基准测试中达到87.8%的平均成功率，并在真实场景中优于RGB和RGB-D基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双臂操作的多视角策略存在两个问题：一是各视角独立编码或特征融合较浅，导致语义感知共享有限；二是空间感知不可靠。因此需要构建统一的语义-空间表征来提升双臂操作的性能。

Method: MV-Actor包含三个核心模块：1) 多视角语义交互（Multi-view Semantic Interaction），在各视角间共享语义感知；2) 语义-空间Token交互（Semantic-Spatial Token Interaction），将视觉语义与前馈重建模型特征对齐，获取可靠空间感知；3) 引导式度量深度修复模块（Guided Metric Depth Repair），在消费级深度噪声下修复退化传感器深度，提供更可靠的度量锚点。

Result: 在PerAct2双臂操作基准仿真实验中达到87.8%的平均成功率，达到当前最优水平；在真实场景评估中，面对更频繁的视角变化和不稳定的消费级深度，优于RGB和RGB-D基线方法。

Conclusion: 共享语义感知和可靠空间感知对双臂操作具有显著收益，MV-Actor通过统一语义-空间表征有效提升了双臂操作性能。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation has been widely applied in industrial scenarios. Compared with single-arm manipulation, bimanual manipulation is equipped with multiple cameras to capture information from different viewpoints. However, existing multi-view policies encode each view independently or fuse view features shallowly, resulting in limited sharing semantic perception and unreliable spatial awareness. In this paper, we propose \textbf{MV-Actor}, a multi-view perception framework that builds a unified semantic-spatial representation for bimanual manipulation. First, MV-Actor performs Multi-view Semantic Interaction to share semantic perception across views. Then it uses Semantic-Spatial Token Interaction to ground visual semantics with feed-forward reconstruction model features and acquire reliable spatial awareness. Finally, a Guided Metric Depth Repair module refines degraded sensor depth to provide more reliable metric anchors under consumer-grade depth noise. In simulation experiments conducted on the PerAct2 bimanual benchmark, MV-Actor achieves a state-of-the-art average success rate of 87.8\%. In real-world evaluations with more frequent viewpoint changes and unstable consumer-grade depth, MV-Actor outperforms both RGB and RGB-D baselines, further demonstrating the benefit of sharing semantic perception and reliable spatial awareness for bimanual manipulation.

</details>


### [147] [AgniNav: Configuration-Driven Cross-Embodiment Local Planning for Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10903)
*Tianhao Zang,Siwei Cheng,Haidong Huang,Shanze Wang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AgniNav提出了一种基于碰撞包络配置的单目局部导航框架，通过四参数安全包络（高度、前长、后长、半宽）统一不同机器人形态（轮式、四足、人形）的导航策略，无需重新训练即可实现跨实体迁移，在Turtlebot2、Unitree Go2和K1上验证了高成功率和低碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的导航策略将感知与特定机器人本体（如相机高度、足迹）耦合，从轮式基地迁移到腿式平台需要重新训练或依赖主动深度硬件，缺乏通用的跨实体迁移方法。

Method: 提出配置驱动的局部导航框架：1）定义四参数碰撞包络（高度、前长、后长、半宽）；2）高度参数条件化图像-伪激光扫描网络，从单目彩色图像预测一维碰撞相关伪激光扫描；3）剩余足迹参数配置维度感知的局部规划器进行碰撞检测；4）使用高度条件化的列最小扫描标签（来自配对的彩色-深度数据）训练，无需收集机器人特定数据。

Result: 在Turtlebot2（39/40成功，0/40碰撞）、Unitree Go2（18/20成功，1/20碰撞）和Accelerated Evolution K1（18/20成功，2/20碰撞）上验证，运行频率30 Hz（Jetson Orin平台）。

Conclusion: AgniNav是首个联合条件化感知和规划于共享碰撞包络配置的单目局部导航框架，实现了轮式、四足和人形平台的零重训练部署。

Abstract: Monocular local navigation is attractive for lightweight robots, but existing vision-based policies often couple perception to a specific body, camera height, and footprint, making transfer from wheeled bases to legged platforms dependent on retraining or active depth hardware. This paper introduces AgniNav, a configuration-driven local navigation framework that standardizes cross-embodiment transfer at the collision-envelope level. Each robot is specified by a measurable four-parameter safety envelope: collision-relevant height, front length, rear length, and half width. The height parameter conditions an image-to-scan network to predict a one-dimensional, collision-relevant pseudo-laserscan from a monocular color image, while the remaining footprint parameters configure a dimension-aware local planner for collision checking. Training uses height-conditioned column-minimum scan labels generated from paired color-depth data, allowing the same image to supervise different safety envelopes without collecting robot-specific data. To the best of our knowledge, AgniNav is the first monocular local-navigation framework that jointly conditions perception and planning on a shared collision-envelope configuration for zero-retraining deployment across wheeled, quadruped, and humanoid platforms. Real-robot experiments on a Turtlebot2, Unitree Go2, and Accelerated Evolution K1 achieve 39/40, 18/20, and 18/20 successes with 0/40, 1/20, and 2/20 collisions, respectively, while running at 30 Hz on Jetson Orin.

</details>


### [148] [Task Robustness via Re-Labelling Vision-Action Robot Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10918)
*Artur Kuramshin,Özgür Aslan,Cyrus Neary,Glen Berseth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TREAD是一种利用预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）对现有机器人数据集进行重标注和增强的框架，通过三个步骤（生成语义子任务、分割演示视频、生成多样化指令）提升机器人策略的指令跟随能力和泛化性能，在LIBERO基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人学习策略虽然规模扩大后能处理多种操作任务并泛化到新场景，但因现有数据集的语言标注和动作序列多样性不足，导致指令跟随能力仍然较差。

Method: TREAD框架利用预训练VLM分三个阶段增强数据：1）从原始指令标签和初始场景生成语义子任务；2）根据子任务条件分割演示视频；3）生成包含物体属性的多样化指令，将长演示分解为语言-动作对。此外还通过语言多样性版本增强数据鲁棒性。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上的评估表明，使用TREAD增强数据集训练的策略在未见过的任务和目标上性能提升，实现了规划泛化（通过轨迹分解）和语言条件策略泛化（通过语言多样性增强）。

Conclusion: TREAD通过VLM驱动的数据重标注和增强，有效提升了机器人策略的指令跟随能力和泛化性能，无需额外数据收集即可利用VLM的迁移知识。

Abstract: The recent trend in scaling models for robot learning has resulted in impressive policies that can perform various manipulation tasks and generalize to novel scenarios. However, these policies continue to struggle with following instructions, likely due to the limited linguistic and action sequence diversity in existing robotics datasets. This paper introduces Task Robustness via Re-Labelling Vision-Action Robot Data (TREAD), a scalable framework that leverages large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to augment existing robotics datasets without additional data collection, harnessing the transferable knowledge embedded in these models. Our approach leverages a pretrained VLM through three stages: generating semantic sub-tasks from original instruction labels and initial scenes, segmenting demonstration videos conditioned on these sub-tasks, and producing diverse instructions that incorporate object properties, effectively decomposing longer demonstrations into grounded language-action pairs. We further enhance robustness by augmenting the data with linguistically diverse versions of the text goals. Evaluations on LIBERO demonstrate that policies trained on our augmented datasets exhibit improved performance on novel, unseen tasks and goals. Our results show that TREAD enhances both planning generalization through trajectory decomposition and language-conditioned policy generalization through increased linguistic diversity.

</details>


### [149] [AllDayNav: Lifelong Navigation via Real-World Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10927)
*Hang Yin,Yinan Liang,Jiazhao Zhang,Jiahang Liu,Minghan Li,Zhizheng Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AllDayNav是一种通过强化学习将场景动态隐式编码到大模型参数中的终身自学习导航框架，结合自进化多模态记忆，在跨房间、跨回合和跨任务场景中接近100%成功率，优于基于地图、VLM和RL的强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有依赖显式地图或场景图的导航方法难以在动态环境中泛化，机器人需要从碎片化观察中形成持久场景理解，而现有方法在非结构化场景下表现不佳。

Method: 提出AllDayNav框架，通过强化学习将场景动态隐式编码到大模型的十亿级参数中，核心是自进化多模态记忆模块，维护并更新视觉关键帧、语义描述和时间上下文，同时自主生成开放词汇指令、图像目标和结构化奖励。

Result: 在合成和真实环境的跨房间、跨回合、跨任务实验中，成功率接近100%，在路径效率和鲁棒性上持续超越基于地图、VLM和RL的强基线方法。

Conclusion: 证明了隐式、记忆驱动的强化学习可作为显式建图的可扩展替代方案，实现可靠的终身导航。

Abstract: Lifelong embodied navigation in dynamic environments requires robots to form persistent scene understanding from fragmentary observations, which remains difficult for existing methods that rely on explicit maps or scene graphs and struggle to generalize beyond structured settings. We propose AllDayNav, a lifelong self-learning navigation framework that implicitly encodes scene dynamics into the billion-scale parameters of a large model via reinforcement learning, powered by a self-evolving multimodal memory that maintains and updates visual keyframes, semantic descriptions, and temporal context while autonomously generating open-vocabulary instructions, image goals, and structured rewards. Experiments in both synthetic and real-world environments across cross-room, cross-episode, and cross-task scenarios show that AllDayNav achieves success rates approaching $100\%$ and consistently surpasses strong map-based, VLM, and RL baselines in path efficiency and robustness, demonstrating implicit, memory-driven reinforcement learning as a scalable alternative to explicit mapping for reliable lifelong navigation.

</details>


### [150] [Resilient Navigation for Autonomous Farm Robots by Leveraging Jerk-Augmented Models with IMU-Only Disturbance Rejection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10971)
*Batu Candan,Mohammed Atallah,Simone Servadio,Saeed Arabi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于加加速度增强扩展卡尔曼滤波（Jerk-augmented EKF）与多调谐因子（MTF）自适应方法相结合的鲁棒导航算法，用于解决农业自主机器人在传感器中断和剧烈振动环境下的状态估计问题。实验表明该方法相比基线EKF显著降低了3D位置RMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业自主机器人在越野环境中常面临GNSS/LiDAR/视觉传感器中断以及高频振动问题，导致标准EKF的状态估计精度下降，亟需一种能够动态适应测量噪声变化的鲁棒导航算法。

Method: 提出加加速度增强的扩展卡尔曼滤波（Jerk-augmented EKF），并集成多调谐因子（MTF）自适应方法，实时动态调整测量协方差矩阵，以应对突发干扰和传感器异常值。

Result: 在Salin247自主机器人真实数据上验证，加加速度增强与MTF自适应相结合的方法相比基线EKF模型，显著降低了3D位置均方根误差（RMSE），展现出优越的惯性推算能力。

Conclusion: 基于加加速度增强EKF和MTF自适应的导航算法能够有效提升农业自主机器人在传感器中断和振动干扰下的状态估计鲁棒性，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Precise state estimation for navigation of autonomous agricultural robots is often compromised by sensor outages (GNSS/LiDAR/Visual) and high-frequency vibrations inherent in off-road environments. This paper proposes a robust navigation algorithm based on a jerk-augmented Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) integrated with a Multiple Tuning Factor (MTF) adaptation method. Unlike standard EKF approaches that assume constant measurement noise, our method dynamically adjusts the measurement covariance matrix in real-time, allowing the system to cope with sudden disturbances and sensor outliers. We evaluate the algorithm using real-world data from a Salin247 autonomous robot. Results demonstrate that jerk-augmentation combined with MTF adaptation significantly reduces 3D position Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) compared to baseline EKF models, providing superior dead-reckoning capabilities.

</details>


### [151] [Language-Driven Cost Optimization for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10974)
*Diego Martinez-Baselga,Khaled Mustafa,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于大语言模型的框架，通过自然语言用户查询和结构化场景描述自动生成自动驾驶车辆运动规划器的成本函数参数，并引入人机交互验证环节实现行为迭代优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆的成本函数参数调优需要专业技术知识，限制了车辆适应动态交通场景或终端用户偏好的能力，亟需一种更直观的参数调整方法。

Method: 使用LLM解析结构化场景描述和自然语言查询，生成风险感知MPPI控制器的成本函数参数；引入人在环验证阶段，用非技术语言描述行为变更并由用户确认；支持部署前后用户反馈实现迭代优化。

Result: 在多个真实驾驶场景的仿真实验中，该方法成功诱导出与用户意图一致的行为变更，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该语言驱动框架能够以直观方式桥接智能车辆控制系统与终端用户之间的鸿沟，使非技术用户也能便捷地定制自动驾驶车辆的运动行为。

Abstract: The driving behavior of autonomous vehicles is typically governed by the cost function of their motion planner, which encodes objectives such as speed tracking, smoothness, lane keeping, and collision avoidance. However, tuning the parameters that shape this cost function is a challenging task that requires technical expertise, limiting the vehicle's ability to adapt to evolving traffic scenarios or end-user preferences. This work presents a language-driven framework for adaptive cost design in autonomous driving. A Large Language Model (LLM) interprets structured scenario descriptions and natural language user queries to generate the parameters applied to a risk-aware Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller. The system incorporates a human-in-the-loop validation stage in which the proposed behavioral changes are described in non-technical language and confirmed prior to deployment. Users may additionally provide feedback either before or after deployment, enabling iterative refinement of the vehicle's motion behavior. The framework is evaluated across multiple queries in realistic driving scenarios to assess its effectiveness. Simulation results demonstrate that the method successfully induces behavioral changes that align with the intended requirements in an intuitive manner, thereby bridging the gap between intelligent vehicle control systems and end users.

</details>


### [152] [Multi-UAV Active Sensing with Information Gain-based Planning and Belief Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10986)
*S. Habibi,L. Marques*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验验证了一种基于信息增益的多无人机主动感知框架，用于概率性地形二值图构建。在精准农业场景下，通过因子图建模空间依赖，比较了IGbIPP与随机游走、扫描覆盖等基线方法。结果表明，IGbIPP在熵减和建图误差上优于基线，宽视场角提升覆盖精度，简单空间权重比自适应权重更鲁棒，贝叶斯、对数几率与Dempster-Shafer融合在协作建图中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在空间分布式环境中的主动感知受限于飞行时间短、感知不确定性以及空间覆盖与观测精度之间的权衡，现有方法缺乏针对真实环境下多无人机协作概率建图的系统验证。

Method: 将环境建模为概率信念图，通过因子图公式化空间依赖；采用基于信息增益的信息路径规划（IGbIPP）指导无人机决策，并与随机游走和扫描覆盖基线对比；在合成地形与真实无人机农业影像上进行实验，评估空间相关权重及多种概率信念融合规则。

Result: IGbIPP在熵减和建图误差上优于随机游走和扫描覆盖基线；宽视场角提升真实覆盖及地图精度；简单等权或偏置空间权重比自适应权重更鲁棒；贝叶斯、对数几率与Dempster-Shafer融合在协作建图中表现最佳。

Conclusion: 不确定性驱动的规划、感知几何、空间建模与概率融合对于真实世界无人机主动感知至关重要。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly used for active sensing and information gathering in spatially distributed environments. Their performance, however, is constrained by limited flight time, sensing uncertainty, and the trade-off between spatial coverage and observation accuracy. This paper presents a real-world validation of a multi-UAV active sensing framework for probabilistic binary terrain mapping, with precision agriculture used as the application case. The environment is represented as a probabilistic belief map, where spatial dependencies are modeled through a factor-graph formulation. UAV decision making is guided by Information Gain based Informative Path Planning (IGbIPP), and the approach is compared with Random Walk and Sweep coverage path planning baselines using both synthetic terrains and real UAV-derived agricultural imagery. The study also evaluates spatial correlation weights and several probabilistic belief-fusion rules for multi-UAV information sharing. Results show that IGbIPP reduces entropy and mapping error more effectively than the baselines, while a wider field of view improves real-world coverage and map accuracy. The results further show that simple equal or biased spatial weights can be more robust than adaptive weights, and that Bayesian, log-odds, and Dempster--Shafer fusion achieve the best cooperative mapping performance. These findings highlight the importance of uncertainty-driven planning, sensing geometry, spatial modeling, and probabilistic fusion for real-world UAV-based active sensing.

</details>


### [153] [Diffusion Forcing Planner: History-Annealed Planning with Time-Dependent Guidance for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11019)
*Zehan Zhang,Neng Zhang,Yaoyi Li,Jia Cai,Zhiling Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Diffusion Forcing Planner (DFP)，一种基于扩散的规划框架，通过历史引导控制实现连续、稳定且可控的运动规划，在 nuPlan 上取得 competitive 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的运动规划器存在时序不一致问题，微小扰动累积导致不稳定轨迹；而注入历史作为静态条件信号的方法又导致规划器复制历史模式而非适应环境上下文。

Method: DFP 将完整轨迹分解为历史、当前和未来三段，为每段分配独立噪声水平，通过异质联合扩散过程联合去噪历史和未来段；推理时使用分类器无关引导 (CFG) 以可控方式利用退火历史引导未来采样。

Result: 在 nuPlan 上的闭环评估和全面消融实验表明，DFP 在复杂驾驶场景中实现了 competitive 性能，同时产生连续、稳定且可控的运动规划。

Conclusion: DFP 通过历史引导的异质扩散过程有效解决了时序不一致和模式复制问题，为安全舒适的运动规划提供了新范式。

Abstract: Learning-based motion planners, despite recent progress, often suffer from temporal inconsistency. Small perturbations across frames can accumulate into unstable trajectories, degrading comfort and safety in closed-loop driving. Several methods attempt to inject history as a static conditioning signal to stabilize outputs, only to induce the planner to copy historical patterns instead of adapting to environment contexts. To address this limitation, we propose Diffusion Forcing Planner (DFP), a diffusion-based planning framework driven by history-guided control. Specifically, DFP decomposes the full trajectory into history, current and future segments, and assign independent noise levels to each segment. The model jointly denoises the historical and the future segments, enforcing a heterogeneous joint diffusion process. At inference, classifier-free guidance (CFG) is applied to steer future sampling using annealed history in a controllable manner. Closed-loop evaluation and comprehensive ablations on nuPlan show that DFP achieves competitive performance while producing continuous, stable, and controllable motion plans in complex driving scenarios.

</details>


### [154] [A Spiking Neural Architecture for Coordinating Arm and Locomotor Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11034)
*Lea Steffen,Kathryn Simone,Graeme Damberger,Travis DeWolf,Hudson Ly,Chris Eliasmith*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于脉冲神经网络（SNN）的全尺度仿人机器人集成控制器，通过神经工程框架（NEF）和语义指针架构（SPA）协调手臂力控与双足运动，利用脉冲基底 ganglia 模型实现高层动作选择，在Nengo与Isaac Sim联合仿真中验证了目标抓取、数字绘制、路径跟踪及行走与手臂控制切换等功能，为低功耗神经形态硬件部署奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN电机控制系统将双足运动与手臂控制分开处理，缺乏对两者集成控制的方案，无法满足仿人机器人全身协调控制的需求。

Method: 采用神经工程框架（NEF）和语义指针架构（SPA）构建脉冲神经网络，利用脉冲基底 ganglia 模型实现行走与手臂控制之间的高层动作选择，通过Nengo与Isaac Sim联合仿真进行验证。

Result: 成功实现目标抓取、连续数字绘制、路径跟踪行走以及通过基底 ganglia 去抑制机制在行走与手臂控制之间切换。

Conclusion: 本文首次在完整仿人机器人平台上实现集双足运动与手臂控制于一体的全脉冲控制器，全程基于脉冲的实现方式为未来在低功耗神经形态硬件上的部署提供了可能。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) coupled with neuromorphic hardware offer energy-efficient solutions for humanoid robot control. However, existing SNN-based motor control systems address bipedal locomotion and arm control in isolation, leaving integrated control of both unaddressed. We present a spiking architecture that coordinates force-based arm control and bipedal locomotion in a simulated humanoid, using the Neural Engineering Framework (NEF) and Semantic Pointer Architecture (SPA). High-level action selection between locomotor and arm control is mediated by a biologically grounded spiking basal ganglia model. We validate the system through co-simulation of Nengo, for the neural control, and Isaac Sim, demonstrating successful target reaching, continuous digit drawing, path-following locomotion, and finally, switching between walking and arm control via basal ganglia disinhibition. To our knowledge, this is the first integrated spiking controller to combine bipedal locomotion and arm control on a full-scale humanoid platform. The full spike-based implementation enables future deployment on low-power neuromorphic hardware.

</details>


### [155] [Generation of Diverse and Functional Robot Designs using Superquadrics Parametrisation and Quality-Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11037)
*Leni Le Goff,Simon Smith,Emma Hart*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于超二次曲面（SQs）的机器人形态表示方法，结合质量-多样性（QD）算法MAP-Elites，在机器人生成设计中实现了更高的设计多样性与功能性，相比传统进化算法和CPPN表示取得了更优的QD分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统进化算法在机器人生成设计中容易过早收敛到少量次优设计，难以维持种群多样性以发现功能不同的机器人，因此需要一种能保持形态多样性的方法。

Method: 引入超二次曲面（SQs）作为机器人身体的数学表示，SQs具有可解释、紧凑且计算高效的特点；将该表示与质量-多样性算法MAP-Elites结合以鼓励形态多样性；在两种测试环境中与CPPN表示及标准EA进行对比实验。

Result: SQs表示与MAP-Elites算法相结合在两个测试环境中均达到了最高的QD分数，最大化地提升了生成机器人的设计与功能多样性。

Conclusion: 紧凑且可解释的几何表示（SQs）有助于探索复杂设计空间，将其与显式多样性机制（MAP-Elites）结合能提高生成设计的质量和数量。

Abstract: Generative design of robots requires navigating a vast search-space, encompassing physical configurations and behavioural parameters. Evolutionary Algorithms (EAs) have shown promising results, but often converge prematurely to a small set of sub-optimal designs. Most EAs fail to maintain sufficient diversity in the population that would allow the discovery of distinct functional robots. To counter premature convergence, we introduce a superquadrics-based representation (SQs) for robot bodies. SQs are interpretable, compact and computationally efficient mathematical representations of 3D geometrical shapes that can be tuned to specific design-spaces. To encourage morphological diversity, we combine this representation with a quality-diversity (QD) algorithm (MAP-Elites). We compare SQs and Compositional Pattern Producing Networks representations as generators of morphologies, combining them with standard EAs and MAP-Elites. In two test environments, we find that using SQs to generate morphology in conjunction with the MAP-Elites algorithm reaches the highest QD-score across both environments, maximising diversity of design and functionality of generated robots. The findings highlight the benefits of using a compact and interpretable geometric representation for exploring a complex design-space and suggest that combining SQs with an explicit diversity mechanism increases the quality and number of designs generated.

</details>


### [156] [A Distributed Multi-UGV Exploration Framework With Loop-Aware Planning and Descriptor-Aided Localization in Resource-Limited Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11088)
*Zhiwei Li,Haiou Liu,Xijun Zhao,Ji Li,Yingze Wang,Boyang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在未知、GPS拒止、带宽有限环境下多无人地面车辆（UGV）协同探索的完全分布式框架，通过描述子辅助的UGV间闭环检测与闭环感知的分层规划，在保证自主定位与探索的同时，显著降低通信量并提升探索效率（探索时间减少15%，行驶距离减少14%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无先验地图、GPS拒止且带宽受限的环境中，多UGV协同探索面临定位漂移导致地图一致性下降和覆盖冗余的挑战，现有方法难以兼顾鲁棒性、通信效率和探索效率。

Method: 1) 提出轻量级LiDAR全局描述子（结合距离图像预对齐）实现跨UGV位置识别，对大偏航和横向偏移具有鲁棒性；2) 利用验证后的闭环维持全局一致轨迹和稀疏拓扑表示；3) 设计不确定性感知的跨UGV闭环选择模块，在位姿不确定性下对候选闭环评分，保留高效用闭环作为规划锚点，用于全局任务分配和局部路径优化。

Result: 闭环模块AR@1/AR@1%达89.9%/95.5%；分布式优化降低绝对轨迹误差；系统显著减少双向通信量；整体框架相比mTSP基线探索时间减少15%，行驶距离减少14%。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式框架通过闭环感知的层次化规划有效解决了多UGV在退化环境中的协同探索问题，兼顾了地图一致性、通信效率和探索效率。

Abstract: Robust and efficient cooperative exploration with multiple unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) in unknown, GPSdenied, and bandwidth-limited environments without prior maps remains challenging, as localization drift degrades map consistency and induces redundant coverage. This paper presents a fully distributed exploration framework that couples descriptoraided inter-UGV loop closure with loop-aware hierarchical planning while enabling autonomous localization and exploration. We develop a lightweight LiDAR global descriptor with range-image prealignment to enable robust cross-UGV place recognition under large yaw and lateral variations, and use verified loop closures to maintain globally consistent trajectories and a sparse topological representation. We further introduce an uncertainty-aware crossUGV loop-closure selection module that scores candidate loop closures under pose uncertainty and retains high-utility loop closures as planning anchors for global task allocation and local route refinement. Simulations and real-UGV experiments show that the loop-closure module achieves AR@1/AR@1% of 89.9%/95.5%, distributed optimization reduces absolute trajectory error, the system substantially reduces two-way communication volume, and the overall framework reduces exploration time and travel distance by 15% and 14%, respectively, compared with an mTSP baseline.

</details>


### [157] [RoboNaldo: Accurate, Stable and Powerful Humanoid Soccer Shooting via Motion-Guided Curriculum Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11092)
*Yichao Zhong,Yidan Lu,Yuhang Lu,Tianyang Tang,Haoguang Mai,Yixuan Pan,Tianyu Li,Li Chen,Jingbo Wang,Zhongyu Li,Peng Lu,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboNaldo，一个三阶段运动引导课程强化学习框架，用于高冲击人形机器人足球射门。通过单一人类踢球参考引导，逐步从学习全身稳定踢球先验，过渡到固定球自由踢，再到运动球射门，在仿真和真实Unitree G1机器人上均实现了显著优于基线的高精度、高速度射门表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两难：运动跟踪驱动RL依赖固定参考轨迹，难以适应不同球位和击球时机；任务奖励驱动RL从零探索有效踢击动作困难。需要一种既能提供运动引导、又能适应多样化球况的高效框架。

Method: 三阶段课程RL框架：第一阶段以单个人类踢球参考为支架学习全身稳定踢击先验；第二阶段将踢击适应到固定球自由踢场景（球随机静止在不同位置）；第三阶段通过运动指令-踢击触发接口扩展到运动球射门。训练时使用高层启发式规划器控制接口，推理时可由替代高层控制器驱动。

Result: 仿真中自由踢误差降低48.6%，射门速度提升2.96倍。真实场景中，3米外自由踢和运动球平均射门误差分别为0.73m和0.86m，触球后球速达13.10m/s，达到职业球员开放射门速度的59-71%。

Conclusion: RoboNaldo通过运动引导课程RL有效解决了高冲击人形机器人足球射门中稳定性与适应性之间的矛盾，在仿真和实物上均实现了高精度、高速度的射门表现，验证了该框架的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Elite humanoid soccer shooting requires whole-body stability, high-impulse whole-body interactions, and accuracy to targets. Motion tracking-driven reinforcement learning (RL) provides stability in whole-body movement coordination, but a fixed reference makes it hard to adapt to varied ball positions and strike timings; in contrast, task reward-driven RL struggles to explore and discover valid kicks from scratch. We therefore introduce RoboNaldo, a three-stage motion-guided curriculum RL framework for high-impulse humanoid interaction. A single human-kick reference is used as a scaffold and progressively shifts optimization towards shooting performance. The curriculum first learns a stable whole-body kicking prior, then adapts the kick to free-kick settings where the ball is stationary at random positions, and finally extends it to moving-ball shooting through a locomotion-command and kick-trigger interface. A high-level heuristic planner controls this interface during training, while alternative high-level controllers can drive the same low-level policy at inference. In simulation, RoboNaldo demonstrates free-kick shot error 48.6% lower and shoot velocity 2.96x than prior work baselines. In real world on a Unitree G1 with onboard perception, RoboNaldo attains 0.73 m and 0.86 m average target shooting error from 3 m away in free-kick and moving-ball cases, accordingly. And the post-contact ball velocity reaches 13.10 m/s, which is 59-71% of reported professional open-play shot speed. Project page: $\href{https://opendrivelab.com/RoboNaldo}{\text{opendrivelab.com/RoboNaldo}}$.

</details>


### [158] [EM-Fall: Embodied mmWave Sensing for Day-and-Night Fall Detection on Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11109)
*Yanshuo Lu,Yuxuan Hu,Shenghai Yuan,Xinyu Zhou,Kuangji Zuo,Bofan Lyu,XiChen Yuan,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EM-Fall 是一个部署在移动人形机器人上的嵌入式跌倒检测框架，结合毫米波感知与机器人移动性，通过主动调整感知视角来保持目标可观测性，并在复杂家庭环境（宠物干扰、多径伪影）中实现鲁棒的跌倒检测。在8个真实室内环境和4名参与者上的实验表明，该嵌入式移动感知范式能提高监控连续性并在多样环境条件下保持稳健的跌倒检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跌倒检测系统多依赖可穿戴设备或固定传感装置，存在用户依从性低、空间覆盖有限、在遮挡和不良光照条件下性能下降等问题。

Method: 提出 EM-Fall 框架，部署于移动人形机器人，集成毫米波传感与机器人移动性，使机器人能主动调整感知视角；设计以人为中心的感知管道结合轻量级时序建模，捕捉跌倒事件前后及过程中的运动演化，以应对宠物运动和多径伪影等干扰。

Result: 在8个真实室内环境和4名参与者上进行了评估，构建了居家毫米波跌倒检测数据集。实验表明嵌入式移动感知范式提高了监控连续性，在多样环境条件下保持稳健的跌倒检测性能。

Conclusion: EM-Fall 框架为机器人辅助的家庭安全监控提供了一种实用解决方案。

Abstract: Falls are one of the leading causes of injury and hospitalization among elderly individuals, making reliable fall awareness an essential capability for safety monitoring in residential environments. However, existing fall detection systems often rely on wearable devices or fixed sensing installations, which may suffer from low user compliance, limited spatial coverage, or degraded performance under occlusion and poor lighting conditions. In this work, we propose \textbf{EM-Fall}, an embodied fall detection framework deployed on a mobile humanoid robot. The system integrates millimeter-wave (mmWave) sensing with robotic mobility, allowing the robot to actively adjust its sensing viewpoint and maintain target observability across rooms and under occlusion. To address interference in complex residential environments, including pet motion and multipath artifacts, we design a human-centered perception pipeline combined with lightweight temporal modeling to capture motion evolution before, during, and after fall events. We evaluate the proposed system across eight real indoor environments with four participants and construct an in-home mmWave fall detection dataset. Experimental results show that the embodied mobile sensing paradigm improves monitoring continuity and maintains robust fall detection performance under diverse environmental conditions. The proposed framework provides a practical solution for robot-assisted safety monitoring in home environments.

</details>


### [159] [JOIN: Anchor-Grasp-Conditioned Joining via Opposition, Inference, and Navigation for Bimanual Assistive Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11151)
*Drake Moore,Matt Cheng,Xiang Zhi Tan,Taşkın Padır*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出异构按需双手系统JOIN，通过移动辅助臂与轮椅安装锚定臂的临时连接解决日常双手任务，利用VLM和几何工具实现双手连接的三阶段分解（规划-驱动-抓取），在代表性任务中成功率优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单臂辅助系统无法完成大量日常双手任务（如开罐、倒水、端托盘等），而给轮椅加装第二只手臂会带来功耗、成本及空间损失等问题。

Method: 提出JOIN系统，采用异构按需双手架构：轮椅锚定臂固定抓取物体后，召唤移动补臂完成双手连接。将问题分解为规划、驱动、抓取三阶段，结合VLM提供任务级知识与标准几何工具，贡献了轮椅参考对置分数和任务条件方向可操作度两个技术组件。

Result: JOIN在相同物体和不同物体任务中完成19/20次尝试，优于基线方法的14/20，且显著减少操作员干预。

Conclusion: 异构按需双手系统可有效扩展辅助机器人的任务能力范围，VLM与几何工具的结合为不确定条件下的双手协调提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Assistive mobility and manipulation platforms have received increasing attention as a means of restoring independence to individuals with disabilities. While effective for many basic activities of daily living (ADLs), a significant percentage of everyday tasks such as opening a jar, pouring a liquid, lifting a tray, or basic meal preparation, is fundamentally bimanual and remains out of reach for any single-arm system. Adding a second arm to a wheelchair is impractical, due to the additional power draw, cost, and the loss of space required for transfers and mobility. We instead propose a heterogeneous, on-demand bimanual system, in which a wheelchair-mounted anchor arm is joined when needed by a summoned mobile manipulator that serves as a complement arm. The central technical problem, which we call bimanual joining, is conditional: the anchor has already committed to a grasp, and the complement arm must choose where to stand and what to grasp to complete the task. We formulate bimanual joining as a three-phase decomposition (plan, drive, grasp) and show that a vision-language model (VLM), coupled with standard geometric tools, provides task-level knowledge sufficient to solve a representative class of bimanual ADLs. Our system JOIN, contributes (i) a wheelchair-referenced opposition score, and (ii) task-conditioned directional manipulability. We evaluate JOIN on a Kinova Gen3 anchor and a Hello Robot Stretch~3 complement on representative same-object and different-object tasks. JOIN accomplished more attempts (19/20) than state-of-the-art methods (14/20) and required markedly less correction by the operator.

</details>


### [160] [TacForeSight: Force-Guided Tactile World Model for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11184)
*Yujie Zang,Yuhang Zheng,Xian Nie,Yupeng Zheng,Shuai Tian,Songen Gu,Chen Gao,Zining Wang,Shuicheng Yan,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacForeSight 提出了一种轻量级力条件触觉前向推理框架，通过触觉世界模型预测触觉潜动态，并结合预测性触觉条件策略实现实时接触丰富的操作控制，在动态接触扰动下显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法虽能通过触觉或力反馈改善接触感知控制，但很少对全局力传感和局部触觉传感在时空上的非对称角色进行建模，导致在动态接触转换或复杂表面几何下的操作能力受限。

Method: 核心包括 TacForceWM（触觉世界模型，从双指触觉观测中预测短时域触觉潜动态，以高频腕部力/力矩信号为条件）和预测性触觉条件策略（利用预测潜变量作为预期接触先验，通过交叉注意力建模当前到未来的触觉演化，并通过触觉引导门控模块自适应融合视觉-触觉特征），所有预测均在紧凑潜空间内完成。

Result: 在五个代表性任务和三种过程扰动设置的真实机器人实验中，TacForeSight 持续优于现有基线方法，尤其在动态接触扰动下表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过在紧凑潜空间内进行前向预测，TacForeSight 实现了主动接触推理和适用于高频操作控制的实时推理能力。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires robots to continuously perceive and regulate evolving physical interactions under dynamic contact transitions or complex surface geometries. Recent imitation learning methods improve contact-aware control by incorporating tactile or force feedback, but they rarely model the asymmetric spatiotemporal roles of global force and local tactile sensing. To address this, we propose TacForeSight, a lightweight force-conditioned tactile foresight framework for real-time manipulation. The core component is TacForceWM, a tactile world model that predicts short-horizon tactile latent dynamics from dual-finger tactile observations conditioned on high-frequency wrist force and torque signals. Another key component, the Predictive Tactile-Conditioned Policy, leverages the predicted latents as anticipatory contact priors, models the current-to-future tactile evolution via cross-attention, and adaptively fuses visuo-tactile features through a tactile-guided gating module. By forecasting purely within a compact latent space, TacForeSight enables proactive contact reasoning with efficient real-time inference suitable for high-frequency manipulation control. Real-robot experiments on five representative tasks and three in-process perturbation settings show that TacForeSight consistently outperforms existing baselines, particularly under dynamic contact disturbances. All models and datasets will be made publicly available on the project website at https://tacforesight.github.io/ProjectPage.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [Business World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10044)
*Cecil Pang,Hiroki Sayama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了商业世界模型（BWM）的概念与架构，一种专为商业和组织环境设计的智能世界模型，能够从高层战略目标出发自主规划、优化和执行商业行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI工具主要聚焦于自动化预定义任务，而AI的真正变革潜力在于从战略目标出发进行自主规划与执行。现有研究缺乏能够编码商业状态、动态、约束、目标和可行行动空间的统一框架。

Method: 提出以商业语义为中心的公式化方法，将商业状态、动态和行动与关键商业实体关联；构建集成语义数据表示、概率机器学习模型、确定性业务规则和显式行动空间的架构，作为可执行的商业行动内部模拟器。

Result: 建立了一个概念性框架，支持智能体模拟替代行动序列、估计对未来商业结果的影响、在不确定性下评估权衡，并能够进行反事实推理。

Conclusion: BWM奠定了自主商业系统的概念基础，推动从指令执行向目标驱动规划与执行的范式转变。

Abstract: Businesses are increasingly adopting AI-enabled tools to improve productivity, reduce costs, and enhance products and services. However, the transformative potential of AI extends beyond automating predefined tasks: it lies in enabling intelligent systems to plan, optimize, and execute business initiatives from high-level strategic objectives. This paper introduces the concept and architecture of a business world model (BWM), a world model specialized for business and organizational environments. Inspired by world models in artificial intelligence, cognitive science, and control theory, a BWM encodes business states, dynamics, constraints, objectives, and feasible action space to support autonomous decision-making. We propose a business-semantics-centric formulation in which business states, dynamics and actions are linked to key business entities. Within this framework, agents can simulate alternative action sequences, estimate their effects on future business outcomes, and evaluate trade-offs under uncertainty. The proposed architecture integrates semantic data representations, probabilistic machine learning models, deterministic business rules, and explicit action space into a coherent structure for planning and counterfactual reasoning. Although its individual components are not new, the contribution of BWM lies in organizing them as an executable internal simulator for business initiatives. This work establishes a conceptual foundation for autonomous business systems capable of moving from instruction-based execution toward goal-driven planning and execution.

</details>


### [162] [Deployment-Time Memorization in Foundation-Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10062)
*Lei,Chen,Guilin Zhang,Kai Zhao,Dalmo Cirne,Andy Olsen,Xu Chu,Zeke Miller,Alet Blanken,Amine Anoun,Jerry Ting*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了长效智能体记忆系统在部署阶段作为记忆机制时的隐私-效用权衡，通过三项指标（个性化召回、对抗性提取率、遗忘残留分数）系统评估了摘要激进程度、检索广度（k）和删除模式三个记忆设计旋钮的影响。实验表明，关键事实摘要能在几乎不影响个性化召回的情况下大幅降低可提取性，但会导致删除保真度问题——仅删除原始数据后派生摘要仍有约20%可恢复，只有全管道清除才能消除残留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作要么关注参数化记忆，要么审计固定记忆配置，但未能刻画记忆设计选择如何共同影响个性化效用、提取风险和删除保真度。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统研究部署阶段记忆（deployment-time memorization）的隐私-效用权衡。

Method: 将智能体记忆建模为隐私-效用边界，使用个性化召回（PR）和对抗性提取率（AER）度量，引入遗忘残留分数（FRS）量化已删除信息在派生记忆层中的可恢复性。通过扫描三个记忆设计旋钮（摘要激进程度、检索广度k、删除模式），在LongMemEval基准上进行实验。

Result: 关键事实摘要使Gemma 3 12B的金丝雀提取率降低76%，GPT-4o-mini降低64%，同时几乎保留全部个性化召回；但增加k无法恢复已压缩掉的信息泄露。压缩导致删除保真度失败：仅原始删除后派生摘要仍有约20%可恢复，只有全管道清除或墓碑删除才能将最差层的残留降至零。

Conclusion: 持久智能体记忆必须作为第一类记忆机制进行评估——衡量它帮助智能体回忆什么、使什么可被提取、以及真正能擦除什么。

Abstract: Foundation-model agents are increasingly long-lived systems that remember users across interactions, making memorization an explicit deployment-time function rather than solely a property of model weights. Existing work addresses parametric memorization or audits fixed memory configurations, but does not characterize how memory-design choices jointly shape personalization utility, extraction risk, and deletion fidelity. We study this surface as deployment-time memorization, formulating agent memory as a privacy-utility frontier measured by Personalization Recall (PR) and Adversarial Extraction Rate (AER), and sweeping three memory-design knobs: summarization aggressiveness, retrieval breadth (k), and deletion mode. We further introduce the Forgetting Residue Score (FRS) to quantify whether deleted information remains recoverable from derived memory tiers. On LongMemEval, key-fact summarization reduces canary extraction by 76% on Gemma 3 12B and 64% on GPT-4o-mini while preserving nearly all personalization recall; critically, once content is compressed away, increasing k no longer restores leakage. The same compression, however, induces a deletion-fidelity failure: raw-only deletion leaves derived summary copies recoverable in approximately 20% of instances, and only full-pipeline purge or tombstone redaction drives worst-tier residue to zero. Together, these results establish that persistent agent memory must be evaluated as a first-class memorization mechanism -- assessed by what it helps agents recall, what it makes extractable, and what it can truly erase.

</details>


### [163] [Exploratory Responsiveness and Adaptive Rigidity under AI-Assisted Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10086)
*Balaraju Battu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个关于AI辅助优化下探索性适应的理论，通过动力学框架论证AI系统与探索性响应能力的互动决定了长期适应效果，识别了收敛性预测体制导致探索替代和适应性下降的机制，以及探索增强体制的条件，强调有效替代参数依赖于系统自身的响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI辅助优化下探索性适应的长期动态效应，揭示预测性辅助如何与探索性响应能力相互作用，解释为何相同AI能力在不同系统中产生截然不同的适应结果。

Method: 采用动力学框架构建形式化模型，将认知、制度和技术系统视为在崎岖认知景观上演化的动态系统，以适应性响应能力为核心状态变量，分析收敛性预测体制与探索增强体制下的系统行为。

Result: 识别了三种关键动态：探索替代导致适应性下降、亚稳态陷阱、迟滞效应和提前收敛；发现了探索增强体制存在的条件；揭示了有效替代参数具有响应能力依赖性的特征。

Conclusion: AI的长期适应效应不仅取决于AI能力本身，还依赖于制度结构、发展背景和人机交互架构，低响应能力的系统更易陷入探索替代陷阱，高响应能力的系统则可能借助AI扩展探索空间。

Abstract: This paper develops a theory of exploratory adaptation under AI-assisted optimization. The central argument is that the long-run adaptive effects of AI systems depend critically on how predictive assistance interacts with exploratory responsiveness itself. We formalize this mechanism using a dynamical framework in which cognitive, institutional, and technological systems evolve over rugged epistemic landscapes characterized by multiple locally reinforced configurations. A central state variable in the model is adaptive responsiveness, which measures the capacity of a system to traverse unfamiliar conceptual and institutional trajectories under changing conditions. Under convergent predictive regimes, AI systems substitute for exploratory engagement, reducing adaptive responsiveness and generating metastable trapping, hysteresis, premature convergence, and exploration-collapse dynamics in which systems become locally efficient but globally rigid. The framework also identifies contrasting exploration-enhancing regimes in which AI systems amplify exploratory search, conceptual traversal, and adaptive mobility. The effective substitution parameter is therefore responsiveness-dependent: systems possessing weak exploratory routines are more vulnerable to exploratory substitution, whereas systems already possessing high adaptive responsiveness may use AI assistance to expand exploratory mobility across rugged landscapes. The long-run adaptive effects of AI consequently depend not only on AI capability itself, but also on institutional structure, developmental context, and the architecture of human-machine interaction.

</details>


### [164] [Predictive Assistance and the Temporal Dynamics of Exploratory Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10094)
*Balaraju Battu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个几何动力学框架，研究预测性AI系统如何通过外源探索压缩机制稳定决策轨迹，在自生探索展开之前塑造认知策略空间，并导致探索响应性降低、滞后性恢复以及早期干预限制未来探索等效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典认知理论将问题解决描述为结构化问题空间中的探索性搜索，而预测性AI系统引入了一种新机制——在内部探索多样化展开之前就已提供解决方案和决策轨迹。本文旨在建立理论框架来理解这种预测性辅助如何重塑探索性认知的几何结构。

Method: 建立了一个几何动力学框架，其中注意力在由稳定漂移、内源探索扰动和响应性门控学习塑造的策略景观上演化。将预测性辅助建模为外源探索压缩过程，该过程在自生探索拓宽策略空间可达区域之前稳定轨迹。

Result: 三个主要结果：1)持续预测性稳定化降低探索响应性；2)曲率不对称累积和松弛产生滞后及恢复延迟；3)稳定化时机对发展结果至关重要，早期干预在表征多样化之前缩小未来探索遍历范围。

Conclusion: 框架生成了关于探索熵、过早收敛和预测稳定化后延迟恢复的可检验预测。更广泛地，预测系统可能重塑探索性认知本身的几何结构。

Abstract: Classical theories of cognition describe problem solving as exploratory search through structured problem spaces in which repeated interaction gradually compresses search into efficient representational structures. Predictive artificial intelligence systems introduce a distinct regime in which stabilization may occur before exploratory diversification unfolds, supplying solutions and decision trajectories prior to internally generated search. This paper develops a geometric dynamical framework in which attention evolves over a landscape of strategies shaped by stabilizing drift, endogenous exploratory perturbation, and responsiveness-gated learning. Predictive assistance is modeled as a process of exogenous exploratory compression that stabilizes trajectories before self-generated exploration broadens the accessible regions of strategy space. The framework yields three main results. First, sustained predictive stabilization reduces exploratory responsiveness by attenuating the effective influence of intrinsic perturbations even when exploratory variability remains present. Second, curvature accumulates and relaxes asymmetrically, producing hysteresis and delayed recovery of exploratory mobility after assistance withdrawal. Third, developmental outcomes depend critically on the timing of stabilization, with early intervention narrowing future exploratory traversal before broad representational diversification has occurred. The framework generates empirically testable predictions concerning exploratory entropy, premature convergence, and delayed recovery following predictive stabilization. More broadly, the results suggest that predictive systems may reshape the geometry of exploratory cognition itself.

</details>


### [165] [From Senses to Decisions: The Information Flow of Auditory and Visual Perception in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10147)
*Wish Suharitdamrong,Muhammad Awais,Xiatian Zhu,Sara Atito*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了音频-视觉大语言模型（AVLLMs）中音频和视觉信息流的内部路径，发现对于音频-视觉视频，AVLLMs遵循与视觉语言模型（VLMs）和视频大语言模型（VideoLLMs）相同的顺序信息流路径；而对于多个交错音频-视觉项，则转向并行流。研究还表明，信息一旦传递给LLM，音频-视觉及其他类型的token可以被丢弃而不影响预测甚至略有提升，从而实现更高效的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）能够处理音频和视觉信号，但音频和视觉token如何在网络内部传递并影响最终预测的机制尚不明确，缺乏对AVLLMs内部信息流路径的系统理解。

Method: 通过两种输入配置（音频-视觉视频和多个交错音频-视觉项）追踪AVLLMs中音频和视觉信息的路径、利用和整合方式，分析信息流结构，并测试丢弃特定类型token对模型预测的影响。

Result: 音频-视觉视频场景下，AVLLMs采用顺序信息流路径，音频和视觉贡献按任务对各模态的依赖程度成比例流动；多交错音频-视觉项场景下，路径转变为并行流。丢弃已传递信息的音频-视觉及其他类型token对预测影响极小甚至略有提升，该发现在多个模型和规模（Qwen2.5-Omni和Video-SALMONN2 Plus的3B和7B）上一致成立。

Conclusion: 首次揭示了AVLLMs在网络内部协调音频和视觉信息的完整图景，为多模态大语言模型的可解释性、设计和效率优化奠定了基础。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can listen and see, but how do audio and visual signals actually travel through the network to shape an answer? Despite their growing role in research and real-world applications, the internal pathways through which audio and visual tokens influence the final prediction remain poorly understood. In this study, we examine audio-visual information flow inside Audio-Visual Large Language Models (AVLLMs), tracing how AVLLMs route, utilize, and integrate audio and visual information across two input configurations, audio-visual video and multiple interleaved audio-visual items. We find that for audio-visual video, AVLLMs follow the sequential information flow pathway established for VLMs and VideoLLMs, with audio and visual contribution flowing along this pathway in proportion to the task's reliance on each modality. In settings with multiple interleaved audio-visual items, this routing shifts to different parallel streams. Furthermore, we demonstrate that audio-visual and other token types can be discarded once their information is transferred to LLM, with minimal impact on the model's prediction or even slight improvement, generalizing across multiple tasks and datasets, enabling more efficient inference. These findings hold across multiple models and scales, Qwen2.5-Omni and Video-SALMONN2 Plus at 3B and 7B scales, leading to hypotheses on why these flow structures emerge. Together, these results deliver the first coherent picture of how AVLLMs orchestrate sound and sight inside the network and lay the groundwork for the next wave of interpretability, design, and efficiency advances in audio-visual and broader MLLMs.

</details>


### [166] [Less Context, Better Agents: Efficient Context Engineering for Long-Horizon Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10209)
*Abhilasha Lodha,Mahsa Pahlavikhah Varnosfaderani,Abir Chakraborty,Abhinav Mithal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对企业工作流中LLM因冗长工具响应导致的上下文溢出问题，本文在Dynamics 365财务场景中对比四种GPT-5配置，发现保留最近5次工具调用+自动摘要的策略最优：完成率91.6%、金额项化率99.64%，同时大幅降低token消耗和运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为自主代理部署在企业工作流中时，企业系统的冗长工具响应会导致上下文溢出、陈旧状态错误和高推理成本，亟需有效的上下文管理策略。

Method: 在Microsoft Dynamics 365财务运营系统的费用项化任务中，使用MCP工具，评估四种GPT-5配置：无用户模型、完整对话历史、修剪至最近5个工具调用/响应对、修剪+自动摘要。在50任务酒店费用基准上，5次独立运行取平均。

Result: 无用户模型基线完成率仅8.0%；完整上下文提升至71.0%但消耗1,480,996 token和14.56小时；修剪至最近5次调用提升至79.0%（535,274 token, 5.39小时）；添加摘要取得最佳结果：91.6%完成率和99.64%金额项化率（553,374 token, 5.79小时）。

Conclusion: 对于此类企业工具使用工作流，选择性保留最近工具交互并添加紧凑摘要，相比保留完整历史可同时提升可靠性和效率。

Abstract: Large language models deployed as autonomous agents for enterprise workflows face a key challenge: verbose tool responses from enterprise systems can cause context overflow, stale-state errors, and high inference cost. We study this problem in automated expense itemization in Microsoft Dynamics 365 Finance and Operations using Model Context Protocol tools. We evaluate four GPT-5 configurations on a 50-task hotel expense benchmark: no user model, full conversation history, context pruned to the last 5 tool call/response pairs, and pruning with automated summarization. Results are averaged across 5 independent runs, with the user model held constant for the context-engineering comparison. The no-user-model baseline achieves only 8.0% complete itemization. Full-context retention improves completion to 71.0%, but consumes 1,480,996 tokens and 14.56 hours per benchmark. Pruning to the last 5 tool calls improves completion to 79.0% while reducing token use to 535,274 and runtime to 5.39 hours. Adding summarization achieves the best result: 91.6% complete itemization and 99.64% average amount itemized, with 553,374 tokens and 5.79 hours. We further report confidence intervals, effect-size analysis, sensitivity over pruning and summary windows, failure analysis, results across five expense types grouped into three categories, and cross-model evidence with Claude Sonnet 4.5. These results show that, for this class of enterprise tool-use workflow, selective retention of recent tool interactions plus compact summarization can improve both reliability and efficiency compared with full-history retention.

</details>


### [167] [Minimalist Genetic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10237)
*Leonardo Trujillo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出最小化遗传规划（MGP），将遗传规划的搜索问题重新构思为句法推导问题，受语言学中最简方案启发，使用MERGE算子增量构建符号表达式，在易产生膨胀的符号回归任务上表现优于标准GP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统遗传规划将学习任务视为搜索问题，但存在膨胀等固有问题。本文试图改变GP的第二个核心假设，将其从搜索问题重新构想为句法推导任务，以克服标准GP的局限性。

Method: 借鉴语言学最简方案中的MERGE二元集合形成算子，采用马尔可夫过程增量构建复杂句法结构；通过选择合适的原子句法对象词典，使系统能够自动发现符号表达式的核心构建块并逐步组合。

Result: 在标准GP难以处理的符号回归任务上（易产生膨胀），当选择合适原子句法对象词典时，MGP能够一致地产生精确的真实模型，而标准GP难以做到同样效果。

Conclusion: 最简方案提供的洞见对程序归纳问题具有相关性，基于MGP展现的潜力，该方向值得进一步探索。

Abstract: Genetic programming (GP) is based on two important insights. First, that any learning task can fundamentally be posed as a program induction problem, where the goal is to construct a symbolic hierarchical model that is expressed as a syntax tree. Second, to pose this task as a search problem, and use evolution to locate the desired model. Since it was proposed, GP has produced notable results in a wide range of tasks and problem domains. This work presents an alternative view by modifying the second core insight of GP, posing the problem as a syntactic derivation task instead. In particular, this paper presents Minimalist Genetic Programming (MGP), an algorithm that like GP is biologically inspired, but instead of evolution it takes inspiration from the Minimalist Program to human language, in which syntax is understood as an optimal solution to the problem of linking two other mental systems. In minimalism, the core computational process is a binary set formation operator called $MERGE$, than can be used to incrementally construct complex syntactic structures using a simple Markovian process. MGP is able to discover the core building blocks of the symbolic expressions, and to incrementally combined them using $MERGE$. The proposed system is benchmarked on symbolic regression tasks that are known to be difficult to solve with standard GP systems because of the propensity for bloat. Results show that when a proper lexicon of atomic syntactic objects are chosen, MGP is able to consistently produce the exact ground truth model on a set of symbolic regression where standard GP struggles to do the same. The insights provided by minimalism are shown to be relevant to the problem of program induction, and should be explored further based on the potential exhibited by MGP in this work.

</details>


### [168] [Regimes: An Auditable, Held-Out-Gated Improvement Loop Demonstrated on LongMemEval with ActiveGraph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10241)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件溯源代理运行时的可控改进循环框架Regimes，通过将代理状态建模为仅追加事件日志的确定性投影，使失败记录、运行回放、补丁限定、门控审核和决策事件化成为一等公民。在LongMemEval-S上的实验表明，该方法通过修复读者提示，将保留测试准确率提升了0.01-0.10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主改进循环难以信任，因为改进过程通常是外部脚手架附加在代理上的：失败未被记录、诊断无法重放、决策存入侧数据库而非代理自身历史。亟需一种将受控改进变为一流工作流的架构。

Method: 基于ActiveGraph运行时，采用事件溯源架构：代理状态是仅追加事件日志的确定性投影。Regimes循环包含：失败评估→诊断→沿类型化流水线接缝生成候选补丁→静态检查→沙箱执行→样本内评估→保留验证→通过则提升（决策本身也是事件）。

Result: 在LongMemEval-S的五个保留测试上，准确率提升0.01-0.10；种子5（未针对序列提升结构调整）和另一分割具有统计显著性；池化计数仅为描述性，因共享500题池。

Conclusion: 核心贡献包括：ActiveGraph作为可审计基板使受控改进循环可行；门控循环本身；失败机制分类法将失败路由到流水线位置；提示作为发现探测假说。

Abstract: Autonomous improvement loops are hard to trust because the improvement process is usually external scaffolding bolted onto the agent: failures go unlogged, diagnoses cannot be replayed, and promote-or-discard decisions land in a side database rather than the agent's own history. We show that an event-sourced agent runtime removes that friction and turns controlled improvement into a first-class workflow. When the agent's state is a deterministic projection of an append-only event log, failures are recorded, a run replays exactly from its log, candidate patches scope to typed pipeline seams, gates are auditable, and every promotion or discard is itself an event. We demonstrate this with Regimes, a loop on the ActiveGraph runtime that diagnoses failed evaluations, proposes a repair at a pipeline point, and promotes it only after static checks, sandbox execution, in-sample evaluation, and held-out validation. The loop is target-agnostic: the same control flow runs against different tasks through a common interface. On LongMemEval-S the dominant failure is not retrieval but reconciliation: the evidence is already in the assembled context, yet the reader answers incorrectly. Across five seeded held-out splits, Regimes discovers reader-prompt repairs that improve final held-out accuracy by +0.05 to +0.10 in four splits and +0.01 in one over-promotion split; two splits are individually significant (seed 5 unadjusted for its sequential promotion structure), and the pooled count is descriptive only, since the splits share one 500-question pool. The durable contributions are ActiveGraph as an auditable substrate that makes controlled improvement loops tractable, the held-out-gated loop it supports, the failure-regime taxonomy routing each failure to a pipeline location (whose marginal value over an unrouted baseline is the primary open question), and the prompt-as-discovery-probe hypothesis.

</details>


### [169] [RealMath-Eval: Why SOTA Judges Struggle with Real Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10254)
*Yiteng Mao,Kenan Xu,Yijia Lyu,Wenhao Li,Jianlong Chen,Xiangfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了RealMath-Eval基准，包含224道真实高中学生数学考试答案，发现即使最先进的LLM评分员在评估真实学生推理时也表现不佳（MSE~2.96），而对合成LLM生成的解答则准确得多（MSE~1.17）。这种"评估鸿沟"源于人类错误的语义空间更分散、推理步骤的信息量更大，且表面风格迁移无法弥补这一差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在求解高中数学问题上已接近完美，但其评估真实人类学生多样化推理过程的能力尚未得到充分检验。为了弥补这一研究空白，作者构建了真实学生答题基准来系统评估LLM的评分能力。

Method: 1) 构建RealMath-Eval基准：224道经专家标注的真实高中考试答案；2) 对比评估：LLM评分员在真实答案与合成LLM生成答案上的表现差异；3) 语义嵌入分析：比较人类错误与合成错误的语义空间结构；4) 生成概率探测：分析学生推理步骤的信息论惊奇度；5) 表面风格迁移实验：尝试弥合评估鸿沟。

Result: 1) 最先进LLM评分员在真实答案上MSE约2.96，远高于合成答案的1.17；2) 合成错误呈现"结构坍塌"，集中在低维线性子空间，而人类错误分布更分散；3) 人类推理的信息论惊奇度显著更高，超出当前模型的分布范围；4) 表面风格迁移无法消除评估鸿沟。

Conclusion: 当前依赖合成数据的LLM评估流程无法充分捕捉真实学生数学推理的多样性，LLM对真实人类推理的评估能力仍存在根本性局限。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved near-perfect performance in \emph{solving} high-school mathematics, their ability to \emph{evaluate} the diverse reasoning processes of real human students remains under-examined. To bridge this gap, we introduce \textbf{RealMath-Eval}, a rigorously annotated benchmark of 224 real-world exam responses from high schools. Our initial evaluation reveals that even state-of-the-art LLM judges struggle significantly on this task, exhibiting a high Mean Squared Error ($\sim$2.96) against expert human grading. To probe a plausible explanation, we contrast this performance with a control setting where the same judges evaluate synthetic LLM-generated solutions. We identify a stark ``Evaluation Gap'': judges are considerably more accurate and consistent on synthetic text (MSE $\sim$1.17) but struggle to generalize to authentic student reasoning. Through semantic embedding analysis, we find that synthetic errors suffer from a ``structural collapse'' into predictable, low-dimensional linear subspaces, whereas human errors form a more diverse error space. Furthermore, generative probability probes suggest that human reasoning involves significantly higher information-theoretic surprisal, indicating that student reasoning transitions are more out-of-distribution for current models. Finally, we find that surface-level style transfer fails to close this gap. Our findings suggest that current LLM evaluation pipelines relying heavily on synthetic data may not adequately capture the diversity of authentic student mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [170] [Supervised Fine-tuning with Synthetic Rationale Data Hurts Real-World Disease Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10279)
*Buxin Su,Bingxuan Li,Cheng Qian,Yiwei Wang,Jin Jin,Bingxin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实验（504种配置）发现，在五年期阿尔茨海默病及相关痴呆症预测任务中，使用合成理由数据进行监督微调反而会持续且显著地损害预测性能，原因在于叙述合理性与判别性优化之间存在结构性冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验一个广泛存在的假设：在临床预测任务中，使用合成理由数据的监督微调不仅能教模型预测什么，还能教模型为什么预测，从而提升性能。

Method: 在纵向健康病史数据上，针对五年期ADRD预测任务，设计了包含504种配置的大规模对照实验，比较了基于理由的SFT与仅标签微调的效果，并进行了人类专家标注、小样本实验等分析。

Result: 理由-based SFT始终且显著地损害预测性能；该退化跨模型家族和数据规模普遍存在，且无法通过使用推理导向的基础模型解决；理由质量并非原因（人类专家确认其医学准确性），但相同理由作为推理时示例而非训练目标时能提升性能；根本原因是叙述合理性与判别性优化之间的结构性冲突。

Conclusion: 理由监督并非总是有益，在高风险临床预测中应谨慎使用；需要更精确地理解何时以及如何让理由监督发挥作用。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning with synthetic rationale data is widely assumed to improve language model performance on clinical prediction tasks by teaching models not just what to predict but why. We test this assumption on five-year Alzheimer's disease and related dementias (ADRD) prediction from longitudinal health histories. Across a large-scale controlled experiment of 504 configurations, we find that rationale-based SFT consistently and substantially hurts prediction performance relative to label-only fine-tuning. The degradation persists across model families and data scales, and is not resolved by using a reasoning-oriented base model. Crucially, the failure is not explained by poor rationale quality: human expert annotation confirms that the generated rationales are medically accurate and faithfully grounded in patient-specific evidence, and few-shot experiments show that the same rationales improve performance when used as inference-time demonstrations rather than training targets. We identify the root cause as a structural conflict between narrative plausibility and discriminative optimization. We hope our work paves the path toward a more precise understanding of when and how rationale-based supervision helps and when it does not, guiding the responsible development of language models for high-stakes clinical prediction.

</details>


### [171] [Sim2Schedule: A Simulator-Guided LLM Framework for Autonomous Open-Pit Mine Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10286)
*Mustavi Ibne Masum,Thiago Eustaquio Alves de Oliveira,Mahzabeen Emu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模拟器约束的大语言模型（LLM）调度框架，使LLM作为自主决策智能体，在零样本、封闭数据环境下生成露天矿开采与加工调度方案，能够恢复MILP最优NPV的94%-99%，且计算时间呈线性扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合整数线性规划（MILP）在露天矿调度中虽能提供数学最优解，但其指数级计算复杂度和无法实时适应的缺陷限制了其在动态工业环境中的实际部署，亟需一种可扩展且实用的替代方案。

Method: 构建模拟器驱动的LLM调度框架，将地质优先关系、采掘-加工耦合约束和动态容量约束直接编码到动作生成机制中；LLM作为零样本自主决策智能体在封闭数据环境下运行；同时开发了包含实际运营与地质约束的新型MILP公式作为性能基准。

Result: 在不同规模和周期的矿山实例上，LLM框架能够恢复MILP最优NPV的94%-99%，且计算时间呈线性扩展。

Conclusion: 模拟器约束的LLM智能体在复杂运营约束下的长周期工业调度中，可作为经典优化方法的实用且可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Open-pit mine scheduling is a critical process for maximizing economic return under complex geotechnical and operational constraints. While Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) provides mathematically optimal baselines, its exponential computational complexity and inability to adapt in real time limit its practical deployment in dynamic industrial environments. This work introduces a simulator-driven Large Language Model (LLM) scheduling framework in which the LLM acts as an autonomous decision-making agent, guided at each step by a custom simulator that encodes geotechnical precedence, extraction-processing coupling, and dynamic capacity constraints directly into the action generation mechanism. Operating entirely zero-shot within a closed, data-secure environment, the framework produces complete, interpretable extraction and processing schedules without cloud-based inference, domain-specific fine-tuning, or retraining. To provide a trustworthy performance benchmark, a novel MILP formulation is developed that incorporates realistic operational and geotechnical constraints. Evaluated across mining instances of varying scale and time periods, the LLM-based framework recovers between 94\% and 99\% of the MILP optimal NPV while scaling linearly in computation time. These results position simulator-constrained LLM agents as a practical and scalable alternative to classical optimization for long-horizon industrial scheduling under complex operational constraints.

</details>


### [172] [From Context-Aware to Conflict-Aware: Generalizing Contrastive Decoding for Knowledge Conflict in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10298)
*Runze Jiang,Taiqiang Wu,Yan Wang,Bingyu Zhu,Longtao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出冲突感知解码范式，通过动态路由机制在模型先验知识和外部上下文之间分配权重，解决了现有对比解码方法中先验-上下文权力失衡导致的可靠性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比解码方法采用"上下文感知"范式，单方面放大上下文而压制先验知识，当外部上下文错误时会覆盖正确的先验知识，造成可靠性瓶颈。需要一种能根据冲突信号动态分配权威的通用框架。

Method: (1) 证明先验与上下文logits的仿射组合构成"幂族"，存在固有的"模态不对称性"：外推法在先验正确时无限放大错误，内插法在上下文正确时矫正不足。 (2) 提出TriState-Bench评估协议，校准每模型先验知识以测量三种冲突状态：修正、抵抗、一致。 (3) 提出自适应模态路由(ARR)，在每一步动态选择内插或外推模态。

Result: ARR将抵抗状态的EM从低于6提升至16-33，同时不牺牲修正和一致状态的性能。

Conclusion: 冲突感知范式通过动态路由机制有效解决了先验与上下文之间的权力分配问题，为RAG系统中的解码可靠性提供了统一的理论框架和实用方法。

Abstract: When large language models generate from retrieved or augmented contexts, conflicts between external context and parametric priors remain a central reliability bottleneck. Existing contrastive decoding methods follow a \emph{context-aware} paradigm that unilaterally amplifies context over parametric priors, overwriting correct priors when the context is erroneous. We generalize this to the \textbf{conflict-aware} paradigm that dynamically allocates authority between prior and context based on conflict signals, rather than presupposing context trustworthiness. We show that the affine combination of prior and context logits yields a \textbf{power family} with an inherent \textbf{regime asymmetry}: extrapolation amplifies errors unboundedly when the prior is correct, interpolation under-corrects when the context is correct, and no static regime covers both. Existing contrastive decoding methods are instances of this family, mostly extrapolative. To evaluate both conflict directions, we propose TriState-Bench, a model-aware evaluation protocol that calibrates per-model prior knowledge to measure three conflict states: correction, resistance, and agreement. To resolve the asymmetry, we propose Adaptive Regime Routing (ARR), which routes between regimes at each step, lifting resistance EM from below 6 to 16--33 without sacrificing correction or agreement. Our code is available at https://github.com/keith-Jiang/conflict-aware-decoding.

</details>


### [173] [What Spatial Memory Must Store: Occlusion as the Test for Language-Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10299)
*Doeon Kwon,Junho Bang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言智能体"记忆宫殿"系统中几何空间信息对记忆召回和可见性判断的影响。实验表明：空间几何必须在召回中起主导作用（而非与时间重要性线性混合）；可见性与记忆召回需要分离，通过射线-体素DDA算法可实现遮挡感知；预注册实验验证了可见性谓词的有效性，并修复了实际部署缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直觉上认为几何空间信息能为文本记忆系统提供额外价值，但在此之前缺乏可量化的验证。作者希望将这一直觉转化为可测试的假设，系统性地测量几何信息在记忆召回和可见性判断中的实际作用。

Method: 通过三项实验：(1) 预注册召回实验，比较默认混合加权（空间邻近+时效+重要性）与几何主导加权的召回效果；(2) 分离记忆召回与可见性判断，提出射线-体素DDA算法计算遮挡感知的可见性谓词；(3) 在预注册的八脚本化世界中进行可见性谓词实时验证，使用自动化的oracle评分。

Result: (1) 默认混合加权在空间查询场景下表现低于位置盲基线（Delta-Hit@5 -0.0375, p=0.306），几何主导加权显著提升（+0.3208, p<10^-15）；(2) 文本和FoV锥体对849个墙后目标均得0.000，锥体+DDA达0.982（McNemar p<10^-6）；坐标召回对近重复位置达1.000 vs 余弦空模型0.533；(3) 实时验证中，假可见率从1.000降至0.000（McNemar p=2.5x10^-29），并发现并修复了真实的中继锚点缺陷。

Conclusion: 几何信息对空间记忆系统至关重要，但必须分开处理"记忆存储的内容"与"如何读取记忆"两个问题：空间几何应在召回中起主导作用，可见性需基于几何的遮挡计算而非平面混合。遮挡需要几何几乎是同义反复，贡献在于测量与分离。

Abstract: Language-agent "memory palace" systems anchor each memory to a world coordinate, on the intuition that geometry adds something text cannot. We make that intuition testable and report three results. First, the memory-palace default of folding spatial proximity into a linear blend beside recency and importance does not help and can hurt: in a pre-registered recall experiment the shipped blend fails its own frozen test (mean Delta-Hit@5 -0.0375, Wilcoxon p=0.306), sitting at a position-blind baseline, while a geometry-led weighting wins decisively (+0.3208, p<10^-15): geometry must lead recall when the query regime is spatial. Second, memory recall and visibility must be separated: recall is occlusion-blind by design (you correctly remember the next room behind a wall), while visibility is a perception predicate over stored geometry that the live system never computed. A one-line ray-versus-voxel digital differential analyzer (DDA), re-pointed from the gaze ray the agent already casts, supplies it: text and the live FoV cone both score 0.000 on 849 behind-wall targets while cone-plus-DDA reaches 0.982 (exact McNemar p<10^-6); coordinate recall separately resolves near-duplicate locations a cosine null cannot (1.000 vs 0.533, n=150). Third, the visibility predicate is confirmed live under a git-committed pre-registration (SPMEM-OCC-LIVE-v1: eight scripted worlds, automated oracle scoring, 96 behind-wall targets, false-visible 1.000->0.000, pooled exact McNemar p=2.5x10^-29), a run that surfaced and fixed a real relay anchor defect. We concede that occlusion-needs-geometry is near-tautological; the contribution is the measurement and isolation, separating what spatial memory must store from how it is read. These pilots power a frozen confirmatory study (SPMEM-ZERO-REAL-PREREG-v1); the full human-authored multi-world study with blind raters remains future work.

</details>


### [174] [Mobility Anomaly Generation using LLM-Driven Behavior with Kinematic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10314)
*Yueyang Liu,Joon-Seok Kim,Andreas Züfle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对人类轨迹异常研究中缺乏标注真实数据集的难题，本文提出了一种端到端生成框架，利用LLM智能体注入语义异常行为，结合地图约束路由重建和上下文感知空间噪声模型，大规模合成具有真实标注的异常轨迹数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类轨迹异常研究缺乏带有标注异常的真实数据集，异常事件的统计稀缺性使得传统观测方法不可行，而大规模移动数据采集受限于高昂成本和隐私法规。

Method: 构建端到端生成框架：1）在基线模拟轨迹上，利用LLM智能体系统性地注入语义异常行为（如非分布内签到、跳过常规访问）；2）通过地图约束路由重建修正智能体修改后的停留点间物理路径；3）引入上下文感知空间噪声模型，基于环境和位置特定参数模拟异构GPS传感器退化，缩窄仿真与现实的差距。

Result: 生成了带有真实标注的大规模人类异常轨迹数据集，弥合了纯合成移动数据与复杂现实物理约束之间的差距。

Conclusion: 该生成框架有效解决了人类轨迹异常研究中真实标注数据匮乏的核心瓶颈，为空间数据挖掘提供了可靠的数据基础。

Abstract: Although the study of human trajectory anomalies is critical for advancing spatial data mining, empirical research remains severely hindered by a pervasive lack of ground-truth datasets. Despite the availability of several real-world and simulated human trajectory collections, these datasets exclusively capture normal mobility patterns and lack annotated anomalies. This specific scarcity is fundamentally driven by the inherent statistical rarity of anomalous events, precluding the feasibility of conventional observational methods. Compounding this challenge, the systematic acquisition of large-scale mobility data is strictly bottlenecked by prohibitive costs and stringent privacy regulations. To overcome these fundamental limitations and establish a reliable human trajectory anomalies dataset with annotated ground truth, we introduce a novel, end-to-end generative framework designed to synthesize realistic trajectory anomalies at scale. Our architecture bridges the gap between purely synthetic mobility data and complex real-world physical constraints by operating directly on baseline simulated trajectories. We employ Large Language Model (LLM) agents to systematically inject semantically meaningful behavioral anomalies such as irregular out-of-distribution check-ins and skipped routine visits. To ensure rigorous spatial validity, the system leverages map-constrained routing reconstruction to recalculate the physical transitions between these LLM agent-modified staypoints. Moreover, to narrow the simulation-to-reality gap, we augment the resulting trajectories with a context-aware spatial noise model, parameterized by environmental and location-specific variables, to accurately emulate heterogeneous GPS sensor degradation.

</details>


### [175] [Self-Distillation Policy Optimization via Visual Feedback: Bridging Code and Visual Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10334)
*Haoyu Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Visual-SDPO框架，通过视觉反馈自蒸馏优化代码生成LLM产出的视觉artifact质量。该方法利用可渲染视觉反馈作为特权信息，结合视觉归因代码信用加权机制和序列级GRPO奖励，在图表、网页/UI和幻灯片生成任务上显著优于基线和GRPO方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成LLM在产生视觉artifact（图表、网页、幻灯片等）时，代码先于渲染执行，由于渲染器不可微，导致生成的代码虽可执行但常出现视觉缺陷（元素重叠、文本裁剪、对齐错乱、对比度低、溢出等），亟需一种能利用视觉反馈优化代码生成的方法。

Method: 提出Visual-SDPO框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 视觉反馈自蒸馏，将渲染后的视觉反馈作为特权上下文传递给权重共享的教师模型，再将教师知识蒸馏给学生模型；2) 视觉归因代码信用加权（Visual-Grounded Code Credit Weighting），将每个检测到的缺陷追溯至对应代码语句，增强这些语句上的蒸馏信号；3) 序列级GRPO项，补充密集的token级目标，奖励可执行且视觉质量高的rollout。失败执行通过自蒸馏路径将执行错误作为特权上下文传递给教师仍可学习。统一使用Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct骨干网络。

Result: 在ChartMimic、Design2Code和AeSlides三个基准上，Visual-SDPO相比零样本基线在主指标上提升超过10个绝对百分点，相比GRPO提升至少2.4个百分点，且训练步数更少、推理时无额外开销。

Conclusion: Visual-SDPO通过视觉反馈自蒸馏有效提升了代码生成视觉artifact的质量，解决了渲染后才发现缺陷的问题，且无需额外推理代价，为利用视觉信号优化代码生成提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Code-generating large language models (LLMs) increasingly produce visual artifacts such as charts, web pages, and slides by writing programs that are executed by non-differentiable renderers, committing to code before observing the render. As a result, otherwise executable code often yields artifacts with visually salient defects, including overlapping elements, clipped text, broken alignment, low contrast, and overflow. We study visual-feedback self-distillation for code-generated visual artifacts. We propose Visual-SDPO, a self-distillation policy-optimization framework that treats rendered visual feedback as privileged context for a weight-sharing teacher and distills this feedback into a coding student. To make supervision spatially targeted rather than uniform, we introduce Visual-Grounded Code Credit Weighting, which traces each detected defect back to the code statements responsible for the affected elements and amplifies the distillation signal on those statements. A sequence-level GRPO (Group Relative Policy Optimization) term complements the dense token-level objective by rewarding executable, visually high-quality rollouts, while failed executions remain learnable through the self-distillation path by passing execution errors as privileged context to the teacher. We instantiate Visual-SDPO for chart, web/UI, and slide generation with a unified Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct backbone. Across chart-to-code, UI-to-code, and slide-generation benchmarks (ChartMimic, Design2Code, and AeSlides), Visual-SDPO improves over the zero-shot base by more than 10 absolute points in the primary metric and over GRPO by at least 2.4 points, with fewer training steps and no added inference-time cost.

</details>


### [176] [Reasoning or Memorization? Direction-Aware Diversity Exploration in LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10346)
*Jiangnan Xia,Yucheng Shi,Yu Yang,Kishan Panaganti,Zhenwen Liang,Ninghao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DiRL提出了一种方向感知的强化学习框架，通过提取策略模型内部的推理-记忆方向，构建方向加权梯度特征并重塑奖励，从而在GRPO中增强推理导向的探索、抑制记忆导向的探索，在数学和通用推理任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习探索方法通常鼓励语义或梯度空间的多样性，但无法区分这种多样性源于新的推理过程还是对已有记忆模式的变异，导致探索偏向记忆而非真正的推理改进。

Method: 从模型表示中提取推理-记忆方向，构建方向加权梯度特征来刻画轨迹更新的性质，并据此重塑奖励信号，在GRPO框架中增强推理一致性的探索，抑制记忆性变化。

Result: 在数学推理和通用推理基准上，DiRL显著优于多种现有探索方法，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 通过区分并引导探索方向，可有效提升强化学习对大语言模型推理能力的激发效果，避免探索退化为记忆模式的变异。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a key paradigm for eliciting reasoning abilities in large language models, where exploration is crucial for discovering effective solution trajectories. Existing exploration methods typically encourage diversity in semantic or gradient spaces, without distinguishing what drives this diversity. A trajectory may appear novel because it follows a new reasoning process, or because it varies memorized patterns and shortcuts. Rewarding both cases equally may steer exploration toward memorization rather than genuine reasoning improvement. In this paper, we propose DiRL, a Direction-Aware Reinforcement Learning framework that anchors exploration to an internal reasoning-memorization direction of the policy. Specifically, DiRL extracts this direction from model representations, constructs direction-weighted gradient features to characterize rollout updates, and shapes rewards to amplify reasoning-aligned exploration while suppressing memorization-aligned variations. DiRL integrates seamlessly into standard Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Extensive experiments on mathematical and general reasoning benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of DiRL, showing significant improvements over various existing exploration methods.

</details>


### [177] [ReflectiChain: Epistemic Grounding in LLM-Driven World Models for Supply Chain Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10359)
*Jia Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出REFLECTICHAIN框架，通过生成式供应链世界模型（SC-WM）和双环学习机制，弥合LLM策略理解与RL物理优化之间的认知差距，在半导体供应链基准测试中实现33.0%的推理一致性提升，并表现出反脆弱行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体在供应链管理中面临根本性的认知鸿沟：LLM能解释非结构化策略约束但缺乏物理世界基础，RL能优化物资流动但对语义约束"视而不见"，两者无法有效协同。

Method: 构建生成式供应链世界模型（SC-WM），将异构供应网络编码为6维图-潜空间并保持物理守恒；采用双环学习机制，分离认知不确定性（KL信任域约束的策略自适应）与偶然不确定性（随机潜变量 rollout）。

Result: 在含SIR风险传播、6种扰动类型、10种策略约束模板的10节点半导体基准（Semi-Sim）上，推理一致性评分提升33.0%（p<0.0001, d=2.78），对抗性冲击下维持82.3%可操作性，中等压力下表现出反脆弱行为（增益+40.2%）。

Conclusion: 识别了三种操作性的认知机制——不确定性分离、知识边界检测和经验贝叶斯策略更新，并讨论了五类局限性。

Abstract: AI agents in supply chains face a fundamental epistemic gap: large language models (LLMs) interpret policies but lack physical grounding, while reinforcement learning (RL) optimizes flows but is semantically blind to unstructured constraints. We introduce REFLECTICHAIN, bridging this gap through a Generative Supply Chain World Model (SC-WM) - encoding heterogeneous supply networks into a 6-dim graph-latent space with physical conservation - and Double-Loop Learning that separates epistemic uncertainty (KL-trust-region-bounded policy adaptation) from aleatoric uncertainty (stochastic latent rollouts). On Semi-Sim, a 10-node semiconductor benchmark with SIR risk propagation, 6 perturbation types, and 10 policy constraint templates, REFLECTICHAIN improves Rationale Consistency Score by 33.0% (p < 0.0001, d = 2.78), maintains 82.3% operability under adversarial shocks, and exhibits anti-fragile behavior (+40.2% gain under moderate pressure). We identify three operational epistemic mechanisms - uncertainty separation, knowledge-boundary detection, and empirical Bayesian policy updating - and discuss five limitation categories.

</details>


### [178] [Belief-Space Control for Personalized Cancer Treatment via Active Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10376)
*Deniz Sargun,H. Bugra Tulay,C. Emre Koksal*

Main category: cs.AI
