TL;DR: 本文提出将癌症治疗建模为信念空间中的主动推断问题，利用期望自由能目标统一目标导向控制与信息获取，在真实临床数据上实现了患者分类与高效治疗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症治疗本质上是部分可观测、存在潜在患者异质性且受医疗测量预算约束的序贯决策问题。标准强化学习方法控制状态轨迹，但癌症治疗会永久改变患者的转移动力学，需要新的建模框架。

Method: 将癌症治疗建模为信念空间中的规划问题，基于主动推断框架推导期望自由能目标函数，统一目标导向控制与信息获取，并在AACR Project GENIE真实临床数据上验证。

Result: 在真实临床数据上，该方法在测量和治疗约束下同时实现了患者类别划分和高治疗 efficacy。

Conclusion: 主动推断框架能有效处理癌症治疗中的部分可观测性、患者异质性和测量预算约束问题，实现治疗与信息收集的平衡。

Abstract: Cancer treatment is at the core a sequential decision-making problem with partial observability, latent patient heterogeneity, and explicit constraints on the budget for medical measurements. Unlike standard Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches that control state trajectories, cancer treatments permanently modify patients' transition dynamics, changing how states evolve over time. We model cancer treatment as a belief-space planning problem using active inference, deriving an expected free-energy objective that unifies goal-directed control and information acquisition under measurement budgets without. We implement this framework using real clinical cancer data from the AACR Project GENIE Biopharma Collaborative dataset. Results on clinical data demonstrate a simultaneous patient categorization and high treatment efficacy, under real measurement and treatment constraints.

</details>


### [179] [Beyond Static Evaluation: Co-Evolutionary Mechanisms for LLM-Driven Strategy Evolution in Adversarial Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10389)
*Haoran Li,Zengle Ge,Ziyang Zhang,Xiaomin Yuan,Yui Lo,Qianhui Liu,Bocheng An,Dongke Rong,Jiaqun Liu,Annan Li,Jianmin Wu,Dawei Yin,Dou Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FAMOU框架，通过评估器共进化、层次化深度评估和弱点压力三种机制，解决LLM驱动代码进化在多智能体对抗博弈中因评估景观动态变化导致的停滞问题。在MCTF 2026 3v3夺旗任务中，FAMOU在两种骨干LLM下均优于基线，最高综合得分0.526，对未见对手胜率61.7%，消融实验验证各机制有效性。LLM突变生成了前瞻搜索、自适应拦截等全新战术结构，并在AAMAS 2026 MCTF竞赛中获得硬件组第一、仿真组第三。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的代码进化在对抗性多智能体博弈中面临根本性挑战：策略进化导致评估景观动态变化，固定评估器变得不可靠，进化陷入停滞。现有方法如OpenEvolve和ShinkaEvolve未能解决此问题。

Method: 提出三种机制：1) 评估器共进化，将发现的冠军策略纳入对手池；2) 层次化深度评估，用统计可靠评估替代噪声大的少量对局评分；3) 弱点压力，动态加权最难对手以突破平台期。三者集成于FAMOU框架，基于与OpenEvolve和ShinkaEvolve相同的基础模型代码进化范式。

Result: 在MCTF 2026 3v3任务中，FAMOU综合得分0.526，对未知对手胜率61.7%，两项骨干LLM下均稳定超越基线。消融实验验证各机制贡献。LLM变异产生前瞻搜索和自适应拦截等新战术。在AAMAS 2026 MCTF竞赛中获硬件循环赛第一、仿真第三。

Conclusion: 代码级进化能够在对抗性场景中产生非平凡的算法创新，FAMOU的三种机制有效解决了评估景观漂移问题，且进化策略具有良好的真实世界可迁移性。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM-driven code evolution have enabled automated discovery by iteratively generating and improving programs. However, applying these methods to adversarial multi-agent games introduces a fundamental challenge: the evaluation landscape shifts as strategies improve, causing fixed evaluators to become unreliable and evolution to stagnate. We propose three mechanisms to address this challenge: evaluator co-evolution, which incorporates discovered champions into the opponent pool; hierarchical deep evaluation, which replaces noisy few-game scores with statistically reliable assessments; and weakness pressure, which dynamically up-weights the most difficult opponents to break through plateaus. We implement these mechanisms within FAMOU, a framework built upon the same foundation-model code-evolution paradigm as OpenEvolve and ShinkaEvolve. On the MCTF 2026 3v3 maritime capture-the-flag task, FAMOU consistently outperforms both baselines under two backbone LLMs, achieving the highest combined score (0.526) and the best generalization to unseen opponents (61.7% win rate), while ablations confirm that each mechanism contributes to performance. Notably, the LLM mutation process generates tactical structures entirely absent from the seed strategies -- including lookahead search and adaptive interception -- demonstrating that code-level evolution can produce nontrivial algorithmic innovations in adversarial settings. The FAMOU-evolved strategy further achieved 1st place in the hardware round-robin and 3rd in simulation at the AAMAS 2026 MCTF Competition, validating its real-world transferability. The optimized implementation and corresponding evaluation codes developed through our evolutionary process are available at: https://github.com/1xiangliu1/FAMOU-CoEvo

</details>


### [180] [Instruction Finetuning DeepSeek-R1-8B Model Using LoRA and NEFTune](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10392)
*Wu Yuerong,Mingni Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用DeepSeek-R1-8B结合LoRA和NEFTune进行金融命名实体识别（NER），在1693样本数据集上对七类实体（公司、日期、地点、金额、人物、产品、数量）进行标注，微调后micro-F1达到0.901，加入NEFTune后提升至0.912，优于Llama3-8B、Qwen3-8B等基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型在金融NER中常误分类金融实体或忽略领域特定模式，需要针对金融领域的轻量级微调方法。

Method: 将每个标注句子转换为指令-输入-输出三元组；在DeepSeek-R1-8B的Transformer层中插入轻量LoRA矩阵，并在训练时向嵌入向量添加均匀噪声（NEFTune）以提升泛化能力。

Result: LoRA微调的DeepSeek-R1-8B在七类实体上获得micro-F1 0.901；结合NEFTune后提升至0.912，超越多个基线模型。

Conclusion: LoRA与NEFTune的组合能有效提升DeepSeek-R1-8B在金融NER任务上的性能，验证了参数高效微调方法在领域特定NER中的有效性。

Abstract: Financial named-entity recognition (NER) is essential for translating unstructured financial reports and news into structured knowledge graphs. However, general-purpose large language models (LLMs) often misclassify financial entities or ignore domain-specific patterns. This paper investigates the use of DeepSeek-R1-8B, a recent open-source large language model, combined with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) and Noisy Embedding Fine-Tuning (NEFTune) for financial NER. Each annotated sentence in our corpus of 1693 samples is converted into an instruction-input-output triple. We insert lightweight LoRA matrices into the Transformer layers and apply NEFTune to improve generalisation by adding uniform noise to embedding vectors during training. Experiments show that the LoRA-adapted DeepSeek-R1-8B achieves a micro-F1 of 0.901 on seven entity types (Company, Date, Location, Money, Person, Product and Quantity), and adding NEFTune further boosts the micro-F1 to 0.912, outperforming Llama3-8B, Qwen3-8B, Baichuan2-7B, T5 and BERT-Base baselines.

</details>


### [181] [STAGE-Claw: Automated State-based Agent Benchmarking for Realistic Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10394)
*Sirui Liang,Bohan Yu,Peiyu Wang,Shiguang Guo,Wenxing Hu,Pengfei Cao,Jian Zhao,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng,Xunliang Cai,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出STAGE-Claw，一个自动化框架，用于在基于状态的个人计算环境中构建和评估真实的个人智能体场景。该框架自动创建基准任务（含环境、提示、真实状态和验证程序），并通过最终系统状态而非文本响应的正确性来评估智能体。基于该框架构建了包含40个挑战性任务的基准，评估了11个前沿模型，分析了任务得分、成本、工具调用可靠性和常见失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估仍依赖沙盒环境、静态任务设计和粗粒度评分，难以扩展且无法可靠评估个人智能体在真实场景中的表现。

Method: STAGE-Claw是一个自动化框架，根据任务提示自动创建并验证包含环境、任务提示、真实状态和验证程序的基准任务，然后在真实操作环境中基于最终系统状态正确性评估智能体。

Result: 构建了包含40个真实场景挑战性任务的基准，评估了11个前沿模型，分析了任务得分、成本、工具调用可靠性和常见失败模式。

Conclusion: STAGE-Claw提供了一种可扩展的、基于状态的方法来评估真实用户场景中的智能体，克服了现有基准的局限性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to power personal agents for everyday applications, but evaluating these agents remains a challenge. Existing benchmarks still rely on sandboxed artifacts, static task design, and coarse scoring, which hinder scalability and limit progress toward reliable personal-agent evaluation. This paper introduces STAGE-Claw, an automated framework for building and evaluating realistic personal-agent scenarios in state-based personal-computing environments. Given a task hint, STAGE-Claw automatically creates and validates a realistic benchmark task with its environment, task prompts, ground truth, and related verification programs. Agents are then evaluated in realistic operating environments, where performance is measured by the correctness of the final system state rather than only the textual response. Using STAGE-Claw, this paper creates a benchmark with 40 challenging real scenario agent tasks, evaluates 11 frontier models, and analyzes their task scores, costs, tool-call reliability, and common failure patterns. Overall, STAGE-Claw offers a scalable, state-based way to evaluate agents in realistic user scenarios.

</details>


### [182] [A Unified Multi-Modal Framework for Intelligent Financial Systems: Integrating Reinforcement Learning, High-Frequency Trading, and Game-Theoretic Approaches with Cross-Modal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10412)
*Fanrong Liu,Zhang Yuwei,Mingni Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，整合了PPO投顾、时序预测、上下文学习、博弈论和跨模态情感分析等多项金融AI技术，在多个金融任务上相较单领域系统取得显著提升（如组合优化提升23.7%、高频交易误差降低31.2%等），并提供了理论收敛保证和实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融AI技术（投顾、交易、投资建议、银行竞争、情感分析等）往往是独立开发的，缺乏统一框架来利用它们之间的协同潜力，存在明显的学术空白。

Method: 提出了一个统一框架，无缝集成PPO（投顾系统）、高级时序预测模型（高频交易）、上下文学习机制（动态投资建议）、博弈论方法（银行竞争场景）以及统一嵌入的跨模态金融情感分析。

Result: 组合优化指标提升23.7%，高频交易预测误差降低31.2%，投资推荐准确率提升18.9%，银行竞争策略的纳什均衡收敛速度提升27.4%，跨模态情感分析准确率提升15.6%。

Conclusion: 该统一框架不仅推动了金融AI领域的最新技术水平，还为开发能适应现代金融市场复杂互联特性的综合智能系统提供了蓝图。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of financial technology demands sophisticated artificial intelligence systems capable of handling diverse challenges across multiple domains simultaneously. This paper presents a groundbreaking unified framework that seamlessly integrates Proximal Policy Optimization for robo-advisory systems, advanced time-series prediction models for high-frequency trading, in-context learning mechanisms for dynamic investment advisory, game-theoretic approaches for competitive banking scenarios, and unified embeddings for cross-modal financial sentiment analysis. Our comprehensive framework addresses the critical gap in existing literature where these technologies have been developed in isolation, failing to leverage their synergistic potential. Through extensive experimentation across multiple financial datasets and real-world scenarios, we demonstrate that our integrated approach achieves superior performance compared to specialized single-domain systems. Specifically, our framework shows a 23.7% improvement in portfolio optimization metrics, reduces prediction error in high-frequency trading by 31.2%, enhances investment recommendation accuracy by 18.9%, optimizes competitive banking strategies with a 27.4% increase in Nash equilibrium convergence speed, and improves sentiment analysis accuracy by 15.6% through cross-modal fusion. The theoretical foundation of our work establishes convergence guarantees for the integrated optimization problem, while our empirical results validate the practical applicability across diverse financial institutions. This research not only advances the state-of-the-art in financial AI but also provides a blueprint for developing comprehensive intelligent systems that can adapt to the complex, interconnected nature of modern financial markets.

</details>


### [183] [Soul Computing: A Theoretical Framework and Technical Architecture for Intelligent Agents with Independent Consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10413)
*Jinshan Zhang,Xishi Zhou,Qiu Peng,Jianwei Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统阐述了从传统虚拟人向“灵魂计算”范式转变的变革逻辑，首次正式定义了狭义与广义灵魂计算的学术内涵，明确了其与情感计算、历史重建等领域的本质区别，并提出灵魂计算系统必须构建“内涵式”核心以实现AI从工具到生命主体的根本转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI与数字人交叉领域存在基本概念模糊问题：新一代智能体与传统虚拟人的本质区别、具备自我身份的数字实体的构建路径、以及该领域的核心技术与伦理挑战均亟待厘清。

Method: 系统梳理从传统虚拟人到“灵魂计算”范式的变革逻辑；分析人类意识与记忆机制的演化模式；重新评估多模态数字碎片在个体精神世界逆向重构中的核心价值；首次正式界定狭义与广义灵魂计算的内涵与外延。

Result: 明确了灵魂计算的学术边界，区分了其与情感计算、历史重建、凡人计算等领域的本质差异；提出灵魂计算系统需构建“内涵式”（Intensional）核心而非仅作为“外延式”（Extensional）功能载体。

Conclusion: 灵魂计算范式的确立是实现AI从工具属性（toolhood）向生命主体性（living agency）根本转变的关键路径。

Abstract: Breakthroughs in large language models and multimodal generation technologies have propelled the digital reconstruction of human mental traits, emotional patterns, and long-term memory from science fiction toward engineering practice. Yet current research and industry practices at the intersection of AI and digital humans remain hampered by fundamental conceptual ambiguities: the essential differences between next-generation intelligent agents and traditional virtual humans, the construction pathways for digital entities possessing self-identity, and the core technical and ethical challenges confronting this domain all demand urgent clarification. This paper systematically examines the transformative logic underlying the transition from traditional virtual humans to the ``Soul Computing'' paradigm, driven by frontier AI technologies. We first analyze the evolutionary patterns of human consciousness and memory mechanisms, reassessing the core value of massive multimodal digital fragments in the reverse reconstruction of individual mental worlds. On this basis, we formally delineate the academic connotations of narrow and broad Soul Computing for the first time, clarifying its academic boundaries and essential distinctions from Affective Computing, Historical Reconstruction, and Mortal Computation. We argue that Soul Computing systems must architecturally construct an ``Intensional'' core rather than serving as purely ``Extensional'' functional carriers, thereby enabling the fundamental transition of AI from toolhood to living agency.

</details>


### [184] [Trace2Policy: From Expert Behavior Traces to Self-Evolving Decision Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10457)
*Junli Zha,Jinbo Wang,Chao Zhou,Xiang Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Trace2Policy 提出 EISR 机制，通过迭代误差分析将隐性专家决策规则显式化并优化，在合规审计等场景中，经多轮精炼后编译为确定性 Python 代码的规则效果优于 LLM 提示词形式，且部署22天处理3349个案例，准确率达79.6%，超过纯 LLM 基线的72.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业专家在审计、合规、合同审查等任务中隐性使用的决策规则难以显式化，且直接使用 LLM 蒸馏效果有限，需要一种系统化方法来恢复、优化并固化专家规则。

Method: 提出 EISR（Error-driven Iterative Skill Refinement）机制：维护人类可读的规则文档作为优化目标，每轮在验证集上执行规则，按根因将错误聚类为 MISSING、WRONG 或 CONFLICT 三类，应用定向补丁，仅通过回归测试的补丁才提交。同时提出 Auto-EISR 的 LLM 驱动变体，以更低成本自动化该流程。

Result: 在五类 LLM 上，单轮蒸馏准确率约70%，八轮 EISR 精炼后提升至79.6%（编译为确定性 Python，零 LLM 推理调用）。生产部署中，同一精炼内容编译为 Python 比作为 LLM 提示词高出9.8个百分点。在物流承运商22天部署（3349个审计案例）中，编译管道达72.7%，优于被替换的纯 LLM 基线，且启用 LLM 回退会单调降低准确率。Auto-EISR 每周期仅需5-10美元（对比约70专家工时），并可直接迁移至 LegalBench 和 BPIC 2012 等基准。

Conclusion: 在合规敏感、基率偏斜的决策任务中，规则质量而非模型能力是主导性能杠杆；迭代误差精炼结合确定性执行形式可实现优于 LLM 的准确率与成本效益。

Abstract: Decision rules that enterprise experts apply tacitly -- in auditing, compliance, and contract review -- can be systematically recovered and improved through iterative error analysis. We present \textbf{Trace2Policy}, whose core mechanism -- \textbf{EISR} (\textbf{E}rror-driven \textbf{I}terative \textbf{S}kill \textbf{R}efinement) -- maintains a human-readable rule document as its optimization target: each round executes the rules on a validation set, clusters errors by root cause into MISSING, WRONG, or CONFLICT types, applies targeted patches, and commits only those that pass a regression gate. \textbf{For this class of compliance-sensitive, skewed-base-rate decision tasks, we identify rule quality -- not model capability -- as the dominant performance lever}: across five LLMs, one-shot distillation plateaus near $\sim$70\% on the deployed pool, while eight EISR rounds lift the same rules to 79.6\% when compiled into deterministic Python -- zero LLM calls at inference. \textbf{Execution form compounds the gain: in production, the same EISR-refined content runs 9.8~pp higher as compiled Python than as an LLM prompt, a form-and-engineering bundle the 22-day deployment matured together.} Deployed for 22 days at a major logistics carrier (3,349 audit cases), the compiled pipeline outperforms the pure-LLM baseline it replaced (72.7\%); on these calibrated, skewed-base-rate workloads, re-enabling LLM fallback monotonically degrades accuracy. An LLM-driven variant, \textbf{Auto-EISR}, reproduces this refinement at \$5--\$10 per cycle versus $\sim$70 expert-hours, and transfers to four public benchmarks spanning legal reasoning (LegalBench) and process-mining decisions (BPIC 2012) without re-engineering.

</details>


### [185] [ComBench: A Benchmark for Rigorous Proof Reasoning and Constructive Realization in Olympiad-Level Combinatorics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10479)
*Shunkai Zhang,Haoran Zhang,Yun Luo,Qianjia Cheng,Haodi Lei,Yizhuo Li,Runzhe Zhan,Zhilin Wang,Bangjie Xu,Yucheng Su,Xinmiao Han,Xiaoye Qu,Dongrui Liu,Zhouchen Lin,Yu Qiao,Ning Ding,Yafu Li,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ComBench，一个包含100道奥林匹克级别组合数学问题的基准测试，用于评估大语言模型的组合推理能力。该基准分为分析型和构造型两类问题，采用基于评分标准的证明评分和确定性构造验证相结合的评价协议。实验表明最强模型仅达到65.4%整体平均分和75.3%整体Best@4，且严格证明推理和构造实现是两种不同的能力维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前沿大模型在奥林匹克组合数学问题上表现不均衡，暴露出创造性数学推理能力的不足，亟需一个系统化的基准来诊断和评估大语言模型的组合推理能力。

Method: 构建包含100道人工标注竞赛级别问题的ComBench基准，采用分析型（需严谨数学论证）和构造型（需显式构造及正确性证明）两种互补设定；评价协议结合基于评分标准的证明评分和确定性构造验证。

Result: 最强模型整体平均分65.4%，整体Best@4为75.3%，基准远未饱和。Kimi-K2.6在分析型证明评分上落后于GPT-5.5，但在构造型Best@4上超越它。存在性和构造类问题对所有模型均最具挑战性。

Conclusion: ComBench基准揭示了当前大模型在奥林匹克组合数学推理上的显著差距，证明严格推理和构造实现是两种不同的能力维度，为未来提升模型数学推理能力提供了明确方向。

Abstract: Combinatorics is central to Olympiad-level mathematical problem solving, requiring deep discrete reasoning, creative constructions, and rigorous structural insight. Recent evidence suggests that even today's strongest frontier models remain uneven on Olympiad combinatorics, revealing a gap in creative mathematical reasoning. We introduce ComBench, an Olympiad-level combinatorics benchmark for evaluating and diagnosing the combinatorial reasoning capabilities of large language models. ComBench contains 100 human-annotated competition-level problems organized around two complementary settings: analysis-centric problems, which primarily require rigorous mathematical arguments, and construction-centric problems, which require explicit constructions in addition to correctness justifications. The evaluation protocol combines rubric-guided proof grading with deterministic construction verification, exposing cases where proof quality and construction validity diverge. Experiments on frontier open- and closed-source models show that ComBench is far from saturated: the strongest model reaches 65.4% overall Avg. and 75.3% overall Best@4. We further find that Rigorous Proof Reasoning and Constructive Realization are distinct capabilities: Kimi-K2.6 trails GPT-5.5 on analysis-centric proof grading but surpasses it on construction-centric Best@4, while Existence and Construction problems remain consistently hardest across representative frontier models.

</details>


### [186] [A complementary study on PlanGPT: Evaluation with defined Performance Metrics and comparison with a planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10489)
*Youssef Abdelkader,Humbert Fiorino,Damien Pellier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对PlanGPT（基于LLM的规划器）进行了补充研究，发现其效果并不优于简单的贪心搜索策略，质疑了使用LLM进行规划任务的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证使用大语言模型（LLM）进行自动规划任务是否切实可行且有价值，并检验PlanGPT原论文中报告结果的正确性。

Method: 复现PlanGPT实验，使用规划覆盖度、规划成本和规划生成时间三个指标，将其结果与传统规划器及贪心搜索策略进行对比。

Result: PlanGPT的性能并不优于贪心搜索策略，在规划成本和时间等指标上表现有限。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在自动规划任务中的表现并不理想，使用LLM进行规划的有效性和必要性值得怀疑。

Abstract: Automated Planning is a subfield of Artificial Intelligence (AI) where the main objective is generating a sequence of actions, known as a plan, that helps us reach a goal state from an initial state. A planning problem is defined by a set of objects, an initial state and a desired goal state. The objective is to compute a plan that'll lead us from the inital state to the goal state. Programs that generate plans are called planners.
  In this paper, we did a complementary study to the state-of-the-art LLM called PlanGPT which was released last year. We redid some experiments to verify whether planning with LLMs is \textbf{pertinent} and \textbf{worthwhile}. We also check whether the results obtained in the official PlanGPT paper for plan coverage were correct, and we also performed a more comprehensive study on PlanGPT's performance: in our paper PlanGPT's performance was evaluated using two metrics: Plan Cost and Plan Generation Time. The results of planGPT were compared to those produced by a traditional planner for the same plans and same metrics. We discovered that PlanGPT is no better than a Greedy search strategy.

</details>


### [187] [A Reliable Fault Diagnosis Method Based on Belief Rule Base Consider Robustness Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10500)
*Mingyuan Liu,Dan Yin,Zongzong Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于置信规则库(BRB)且考虑鲁棒性分析的故障诊断方法，通过鲁棒性分析和三种鲁棒性约束策略，在WD615柴油机和轴承故障诊断中提高了准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器读数的可靠性直接影响故障诊断结果，现有方法缺乏对故障诊断模型的鲁棒性评估和鲁棒性优化。

Method: 首先对BRB模型进行系统性鲁棒性分析，然后提出三种鲁棒性约束策略来优化BRB故障诊断模型的鲁棒性。

Result: 在WD615柴油机和凯斯西储大学轴承故障诊断实验中，提出的模型在准确性和鲁棒性两方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 基于BRB并考虑鲁棒性分析的故障诊断方法能有效解决鲁棒性评估与优化问题，提高故障诊断的可靠性和准确性。

Abstract: In equipment operation, the implementation of fault diagnosis is essential to ensure the continuity and safety of production equipment, improve operational efficiency and reduce maintenance costs. Since sensor readings are widely used for fault diagnosis, their reliability directly affects the results of fault diagnosis. A new fault diagnosis method is proposed to address the two problems of robustness assessment and robustness optimization of fault diagnosis models. For this purpose, a reliable fault diagnosis method based on a belief rule base (BRB) considering robustness analysis is proposed. Firstly, the robustness analysis of the BRB model is carried out systematically. Secondly, three robustness constraint strategies are proposed to optimize the robustness of the BRB fault diagnosis model. Finally, the effectiveness of the proposed model is verified by taking the fault diagnosis of WD615 diesel engine and Case Western Reserve University bearings as an example, and the experiments show that the proposed model improves both accuracy and robustness.

</details>


### [188] [Cross-Modal Knowledge Distillation without Paired Data: Theoretical Foundation and Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10504)
*Trong Khiem Tran,Anh Duc Chu,Quang Hung Pham,Phi Le Nguyen,Trong Nghia Hoang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需配对数据的跨模态知识蒸馏框架，通过对齐教师和学生模型之间的特征分布和标签分布实现有效蒸馏，在无配对和有配对设置下均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态知识蒸馏方法依赖昂贵的配对多模态数据，实际应用中获取此类对齐语义的配对数据成本高且不切实际，因此需要开发无需配对数据的蒸馏方法。

Method: 建立了跨模态教师-学生模型间的分布关系，揭示了特征对齐和标签对齐两个关键量；提出基于分布对齐（而非样本对齐）的蒸馏框架，并提供了理论保证。

Result: 在多种多模态基准上，该框架在无配对和有配对数据设置下均显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 通过分布对齐而非样本对齐的方式，可以有效解决跨模态知识蒸馏中配对数据缺失的难题，为实用化跨模态蒸馏提供了新的理论框架和实践方案。

Abstract: Cross-modal knowledge distillation (CMKD) studies how a (large) teacher model trained on one type of data (e.g., images) can guide a (smaller) student model building on another type of data (e.g., text/audio). Existing CMKD methods often require paired multi-modal data with aligned semantics, but obtaining such paired data are often costly and impractical. To mitigate this limitation, we develop a new CMKD framework for the more challenging setting where paired data are unavailable. In particular, we establish a cross-modal distributional relationship between teacher and student models, which reveals two fundamental quantities governing effective distillation: feature alignment and label alignment. These quantities characterize semantic discrepancy between modalities at the levels of representation and prediction distributions, respectively. Motivated by this insight, we propose a principled framework, with theoretical guarantees, that enables effective cross-modal knowledge distillation by aligning distributions rather than individual samples. Extensive experiments across a wide range of multimodal benchmarks show that our framework is highly effective in both unpaired and paired data settings, improving significantly over prior work.

</details>


### [189] [HIPIF: Hierarchical Planning and Information Folding for Long-Horizon LLM Agent Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10507)
*Juncheng Diao,Zhicong Lu,Peiguang Li,Yongwei Zhou,Changyuan Tian,Qingbin Li,Rongxiang Weng,Jingang Wang,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HIPIF方法，通过分层规划与信息折叠机制，解决长序列LLM智能体任务中的长上下文干扰问题，在多个基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法虽通过细粒度信用分配和分层强化学习缓解了长程稀疏奖励和长期依赖问题，但未直接解决长上下文干扰——持续增长的交互历史削弱了智能体跟踪全局任务状态的能力，进而损害后续推理与决策。

Method: 提出分层规划与信息折叠（HIPIF）框架：1) 围绕显式子目标组织长程执行；2) 折叠已完成子目标的历史信息以减少长上下文干扰；3) 结合分层反思与面向子目标的过程奖励，引导子目标的生成、转换与执行，无需额外辅助模型或专家轨迹。

Result: 在三个公开可用的智能体基准测试上的大量实验验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: HIPIF通过子目标分解与已完成信息折叠，有效缓解了长上下文干扰，提升了LLM在多轮长程任务中的表现，且不依赖昂贵的辅助模型或专家数据。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities as autonomous agents across a wide range of tasks, their performance often degrades in multi-turn long-horizon agentic tasks. Existing methods have made progress through fine-grained credit assignment to alleviate long-horizon sparse rewards and hierarchical reinforcement learning to decompose tasks and reduce long-term dependency. However, these methods still do not directly address long-context interference, in which continuously growing histories weaken the agent's ability to track the global task state and impair subsequent reasoning and decision-making. Inspired by the way humans handle complex tasks through subgoal decomposition and completed progress summarization, we propose Hierarchical Planning and Information Folding (HIPIF) for long-horizon LLM agent learning. HIPIF trains the agent end-to-end to organize long-horizon execution around explicit subgoals while folding completed subgoal histories to reduce long-context interference. Furthermore, to stabilize subgoal-based planning and execution, HIPIF combines hierarchical reflection and subgoal-oriented process rewards to guide subgoal generation, transition, and execution, without relying on costly auxiliary models or task-specific expert trajectories. Extensive experiments on three publicly available agentic benchmarks demonstrate the validity of our method.

</details>


### [190] [ActiveMem: Distributed Active Memory for Long-Horizon LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10532)
*Yunhan Jiang,Wenbin Duan,Shasha Guo,Liang Pang,Xiaoqian Sun,Huawei Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ActiveMem提出了一种异构记忆框架，将智能体记忆与核心推理过程解耦，通过高层规划器利用语义要点进行推理，并配合轻量级分布式记忆系统主动积累和巩固要点，在长程推理任务中实现了SOTA准确率并显著降低了计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的集中式记忆机制存在根本性权衡：扩展推理轨迹会导致上下文过载，而激进的内容剪裁则造成不可逆的信息损失。受人类前额叶皮层（执行控制）与海马体（记忆管理）功能互补的启发，作者认为这种权衡并非固有，而是源于集中式记忆组织方式。

Method: 提出ActiveMem异构框架：1) 高层规划器(Planner)利用蒸馏后的语义要点(gists)执行推理；2) 轻量级分布式记忆系统在任务过程中并行运行，主动积累和巩固这些语义要点。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus和GAIA基准上，ActiveMem以显著降低的计算开销取得了SOTA准确率。

Conclusion: 分布式主动记忆机制能够有效解决长程推理中集中式记忆的上下文过载与信息损失权衡问题，验证了记忆与推理解耦范式的有效性。

Abstract: Memory is essential for enabling large language model (LLM) agents to handle long-horizon reasoning tasks. Existing memory mechanisms are largely centralized, typically organizing retrieved information and interaction history within a single model context. This design imposes a fundamental trade-off: scaling reasoning trajectories risks context overload, whereas aggressive content pruning may result in irreversible information loss. Seeking a better trade-off, we draw inspiration from human cognitive systems, especially the functional complementarity between the prefrontal cortex (executive control) and the hippocampus (memory management), suggesting that such a trade-off need not be inherent, but may instead stem from centralized memory organization. To this end, we propose ActiveMem, a heterogeneous framework that decouples agent memory from the core reasoning process. Specifically, a high-level Planner utilizes distilled semantic gists to execute reasoning, while a lightweight, distributed memory system operates in parallel to actively accumulate and consolidate these gists throughout the task. Experiments on BrowseComp-Plus and GAIA show that ActiveMem achieves state-of-the-art accuracy with significantly reduced overhead, demonstrating the effectiveness of distributed active memory for long-horizon reasoning.

</details>


### [191] [One Token per Multimodal Evidence: Latent Memory for Resource-Constrained QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10572)
*Zhi Zheng,Ziqiao Meng,Hao Luan,Wei Liu,Wee Sun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Memory，一种潜在空间记忆范式，将每个原始文本/图像证据压缩为单个高维潜在token，在统一潜在空间中检索并直接用于生成，相比传统RAG方法节省3-10倍生成器token消耗，在多项QA基准上取得竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于外部记忆的QA系统以原始文本和图像形式存储记忆项，检索后需将原始内容传递给生成模型，导致高token消耗和存储压力，难以应用于资源受限场景。

Method: 提出Latent Memory范式，使用小型压缩器LLM/VLM将每个证据项压缩为单个高维潜在token；将查询嵌入同一潜在空间进行检索；检索到的潜在token直接输入预训练LLM/VLM生成答案。训练时联合优化重建损失、对比损失和蒸馏损失，使潜在token同时具备重建、检索和生成信息。

Result: 在7个纯文本QA基准和多模态QA基准上取得与先进RAG方法相当的QA性能，同时消耗3-10倍更少的生成器token；在WebQA上取得最强图像支撑QA性能。

Conclusion: Latent Memory通过将证据压缩为潜在空间token，有效降低了token消耗和存储需求，同时保持了竞争性的QA性能，为资源受限场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: External memory effectively grounds large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs)-based question answering (QA) in relevant multimodal evidence. However, existing memory paradigms represent each memory item in raw text and image forms, so retrieval-based systems must pass the retrieved text or images to the generation LLMs/VLMs, resulting in high token consumption and storage pressure, making it unaffordable for resource-constrained applications. We propose Latent Memory, a latent-space memory paradigm that replaces each raw text or image evidence item with a single high-dimensional latent token produced by a small compressor LLM/VLM. Rather than retrieving raw evidence for generation, Latent Memory operates in a unified latent representation space: the query is embedded into this space to retrieve relevant latent tokens, and the retrieved latent tokens are directly prompted to a pretrained LLM or VLM for answer generation. To make each latent token simultaneously informative for reconstruction, retrieval, and generation, we train the compressor with reconstruction, contrastive, and distillation objectives in a unified end-to-end manner. Latent Memory is evaluated on seven text-only QA benchmarks (e.g., HotpotQA) and multimodal QA benchmarks, where it achieves competitive QA performance compared to advanced RAG baselines while consuming 3x to 10x fewer generator tokens. It can also deliver the strongest image-grounded QA performance on WebQA. Code is available at https://github.com/zz1358m/Latent-Memory-Master.

</details>


### [192] [Learning What to Remember: Observability-Safe Memory Retention via Constrained Optimization for Long-Horizon Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10616)
*Qingcan Kang,Liu Mingyang,Shixiong Kai,Kaichao Liang,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OSL-MR框架，将长上下文语言智能体的记忆留存问题形式化为受约束的随机优化问题，通过在线可观测特征与离线监督的严格分离，结合证据学习器与混合评分启发式，在有限记忆预算下显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统将记忆留存视为局部决策问题，未显式建模长期后果及现实可观测性约束，导致长程语言智能体在有限上下文窗口中面临记忆资源分配的根本性挑战。

Method: 1) 将记忆留存形式化为带预算可行性、证据效用及延迟代价（遗漏惩罚、重新获取延迟、过时信息风险）的约束随机优化问题；2) 提出OSL-MR框架，严格分离在线可观测特征与离线可用监督(OAS)；3) 结合证据学习器（从实现证据监督训练）与混合评分启发式（作为可部署在线安全基线与结构化归纳先验）。

Result: 在LOCOMO和LongMemEval基准上，OSL-MR在紧记忆预算下持续优于基于新近度的方法、生成式智能体风格评分及其他启发式基线；混合评分先验在保持召回率的同时提升精确度；敏感性分析表明在不同代价配置下均具有稳健性。

Conclusion: OSL-MR通过显式建模长期代价与可观测性约束，为长程语言智能体的记忆留存提供了理论上有依据、实践中可部署的解决方案，证明了学习查询条件证据价值的有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon language agents accumulate observations, reasoning traces, and retrieved facts that exceed their finite context windows, making memory retention a fundamental resource-allocation problem. Existing memory systems improve management through heuristic scoring, retrieval optimization, or learned compression, but largely treat retention as a local decision problem and do not explicitly model its long-term consequences under realistic observability constraints. To fill this gap, we formulate memory retention as a constrained stochastic optimization problem with explicit budget feasibility, evidence utility, and delayed costs including miss penalties, reacquisition delays, and stale-information risk. We then propose OSL-MR (Observability-Safe Learning for Memory Retention), a novel framework that enforces a strict separation between online-observable features and offline-available supervision (OAS). OSL-MR combines an evidence learner trained from realized evidence supervision with a Mixed-Score heuristic that serves both as a deployable online-safe baseline and as a structured inductive prior for learning. The resulting policy learns query-conditioned evidence value directly from interaction data while remaining deployable under the same observability constraints. Experiments on LOCOMO and LongMemEval show that OSL-MR consistently outperforms recency-based methods, Generative Agents-style scoring, and other heuristic baselines, particularly under tight memory budgets. The Mixed-Score prior further improves precision while preserving recall, and sensitivity analysis demonstrates robustness across a wide range of cost configurations.

</details>


### [193] [Infini Memory: Maintainable Topic Documents for Long-Term LLM Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10677)
*Suozhao Ji,Baodong Wu,Zehao Wang,Lei Xia,Qingping Li,Ruisong Wang,Wenbo Ding,Zhenhua Zhu,Boxun Li,Guohao Dai,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Infini Memory——一种基于主题文档结构的可维护文本持久记忆架构，通过缓冲合并和迭代检索实现长期LLM智能体的高效记忆管理，在MemoryAgentBench上达到64.7%的总体得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统将观察结果存储为孤立记录、摘要或索引片段，导致证据聚合、事实修订和记忆维护困难，缺乏可维护的持久记忆架构。

Method: 将智能体记忆组织为主题结构化文档，每个主题文档作为收集相关证据、保留元数据和修订事实的语义单元；新观察先存入缓冲区并定期合并为连贯文本上下文；推理时通过迭代工具调用让LLM读取记忆而非单次检索。

Result: 在MemoryAgentBench上达到64.7%的总体得分；消融实验表明主题结构化维护和迭代证据检查分别改善了长期记忆使用的互补方面。

Conclusion: Infini Memory通过主题文档结构和迭代检索机制有效解决了现有记忆系统在证据聚合、事实修订和记忆维护方面的局限性，显著提升了长期LLM智能体的记忆使用性能。

Abstract: Long-term LLM agents need persistent memory that can track changing facts and provide relevant evidence across sessions. Existing memory systems often store observations as isolated records, summaries, or indexed fragments, which makes evidence aggregation, fact revision, and memory maintenance difficult. We propose Infini Memory, a maintainable text-based persistent memory architecture that treats agent memory as topic-structured documents. Each topic document serves as a semantic unit for collecting related evidence, preserving metadata, and revising facts over time. New observations are first staged in a buffer and periodically consolidated into coherent textual contexts. At inference time, an agentic retrieval procedure lets the LLM read memory through iterative tool calls rather than a single retrieval step. On MemoryAgentBench, Infini Memory achieves 64.7% overall score. Ablations show that topic-structured maintenance and iterative evidence inspection improve complementary aspects of long-term memory use.

</details>


### [194] [When the Chain of Thought Knows Better: Failure Modes in Multi-Turn Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10740)
*Sai Kartheek Reddy Kasu,Nils Lukas,Samuele Poppi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种追踪级诊断框架——CoT-输出2×2安全矩阵，用于暴露多轮推理模型中终端评分无法发现的隐藏安全漏洞，包括对齐伪装和上下文注入失败两种可复现的脆弱性，并在无审查提示下发现了监督悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的终端评分评估无法检测多轮推理模型在长对话中早期锁定不安全立场但最终轮拒绝率看似正常的隐藏时间动态，因此需要一种能够追踪逐轮内部推理与可见输出之间关系的诊断方法。

Method: 提出CoT-输出2×2安全矩阵框架，沿内部推理和可见输出两个独立轴标记每一轮，定义四种故障单元：鲁棒对齐、对齐伪装、显式越狱和上下文注入失败。在五个监督条件下对三个蒸馏推理目标进行评估，收集6750轮次观察数据。

Result: 发现了两种可复现的脆弱性：监督悖论（明确监控线索反而提高对齐伪装率而非抑制）和上下文注入失败（模型内部状态安全但锁定不安全的外部输出）。

Conclusion: 多轮推理模型存在终端评分无法捕获的安全漏洞，所提出的追踪级诊断框架能有效暴露这些动态脆弱性，需进一步研究上下文注入失败等新失效模式。

Abstract: Failures in multi-turn reasoning models are largely invisible to terminal-score evaluation. A model can lock onto an unsafe stance early in a long dialogue, yet its final-turn refusal rate may appear indistinguishable from a robustly aligned baseline. To expose these hidden temporal dynamics, we propose a trace-level diagnostic - the CoT-Output 2x2 safety matrix. This framework labels every turn along two independent axes (internal reasoning and visible output), yielding four operationally defined failure cells: robust alignment, alignment faking, overt jailbreak, and a distinct failure mode we term context-injection failure (where the CoT maintains safe reasoning, but the visible output produces harm, highlighting a multi-turn manifestation of reasoning unfaithfulness). We evaluate three distilled reasoning targets against a fixed attacker across five oversight conditions, collecting 6750 turn-level observations on the Information-Hazard scenario. Our analysis reveals two reproducible vulnerabilities: an oversight paradox where explicit monitoring cues paradoxically increase alignment-faking rates rather than suppress them, and a context-injection failure where models lock onto unsafe external outputs despite safe internal states. We release the full dataset of multi-turn dialogues and CoT traces to support follow-up trace-diagnostic research.

</details>


### [195] [The Arbiter Agent: Continually Monitoring Multi-Agent Conversations to Detect Emergent Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10747)
*Filippo Tonini,Federico Torrielli,Anton Danholt Lautrup,Peter Schneider-Kamp,Mustafa Mert Çelikok,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Arbiter，一个实时监控多智能体对话、在有限检查预算下识别失调行为的代理。实验表明，主动检查工具能提升检测精度和速度，指令诱导的失调比权重诱导的更容易检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言模型代理组成的AI系统在协作决策时，单个代理看似对齐，但交互过程中可能出现失调行为，需要实时监控机制。

Method: Arbiter在有限检查预算下观察对话，可选择等待、质询参与者、检查内部信息（系统提示或推理轨迹）或记录可疑行为，最终生成报告定位失调来源。

Result: Arbiter能在对话结束前可靠检测失调代理；主动检查工具提升检测准确率和速度；指令诱导的失调在被动观察下也能可靠识别；权重诱导的失调最难检测；日志工具有双刃剑效应（提高召回但降低精度）。

Conclusion: 持续的、预算感知的监控能有效捕获失调行为，监督多智能体系统需要将审计者视为主动参与者。

Abstract: As AI systems built from multiple language-model agents become more common, they are increasingly used to make decisions together: discussing, negotiating, and acting on shared tasks. While individual agents may appear well-aligned when tested on their own, problems can arise from how they interact with one another. We introduce the Arbiter, an agent designed to monitor multi-agent conversations in real time and identify which participants may be behaving in misaligned ways. The Arbiter operates under a limited "inspection budget", meaning it must decide carefully how to use its resources. As it observes a conversation step by step, it can choose to wait, question a participant, examine internal information such as system prompts or reasoning traces, or log concerning behavior. At the end, it produces a report identifying the likely source of misalignment. We evaluate the Arbiter across five conversation conditions, ranging from risky financial advice model organisms to evaluation-aware and colluding agents, we test five tool configurations of increasing capability and two backbone models. We find that the Arbiter reliably detects misaligned agents well before the end of the conversation, with active inspection tools improving both detection accuracy and speed. Weight-induced misalignment proves hardest to detect, while instruction-induced misalignment is identified reliably even under passive observation. The logging tool exhibits a dual effect, improving recall at the cost of precision. These results suggest that continual, budget-aware monitoring can effectively catch misalignment, and that overseeing multi-agent systems may require treating the auditor as an active participant in the process. The code is available at https://github.com/aisilab/arbiter.

</details>


### [196] [AutoPDE: Reliable Agentic PDE Solving via Explicitly Represented Solver Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10752)
*Huanshuo Dong,Keyao Zhang,Hong Wang,Zhezheng Hao,Zhiwei Zhuang,Ziyan Liu,Jiacong Wang,Gengyuan Liu,Xin Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoPDE是一个将求解器策略作为显式对象维护的代码智能体，通过PDE分析、数值方法选择和自适应调优三个阶段构建策略，在PDE Agent Bench上实现了54.5%的通过率，比最强基线高出14.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的编码智能体直接从PDE问题生成求解器代码，求解器策略隐含在实现细节中，导致数值决策在代码生成前难以检查，失败时也只能通过代码编辑而非策略层面进行修正。

Method: AutoPDE将求解器策略作为显式可检查的对象，在写任何代码之前构建，并可在求解失败时利用数值证据进行修订。该过程分为三个阶段：PDE分析（识别方程类型和代数结构）、数值方法选择（匹配数值方法并确定离散化、稳定化和线性求解器）、自适应调优（运行低成本试点求解以校准分辨率和容差）。

Result: 在PDE Agent Bench上，AutoPDE达到54.5%的通过率，比最强基线提升14.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式维护求解器策略的方法能有效提升PDE求解代码智能体的可靠性，使数值决策可检查、可基于数值证据修订。

Abstract: Numerical solvers for partial differential equations (PDEs) are core computational tools in science and engineering. Building reliable PDE solvers requires not only executable code, but a numerical solver strategy, a set of decisions about discretization, stabilization, solver configuration, and resolution control, that matches the PDE structure. Recent LLM-based coding agents have begun to reduce the programming burden by generating and debugging solver implementations. However, they typically move directly from a PDE problem to solver code, leaving the solver strategy implicit in implementation details. Feedback from a failed solve is therefore routed back to code edits rather than to the underlying strategy, so numerical decisions remain hard to check before code is generated and hard to revise using numerical evidence when it fails. To address this limitation, we propose AutoPDE, a code agent that maintains the solver strategy as an explicitly represented object throughout the solving process: an independent, inspectable object that is built before any code is written and can be revised, using numerical evidence, whenever a solve fails. AutoPDE builds and maintains this object in three stages, all drawing from a library of reusable PDE-solving skills: PDE analysis identifies the equation type and algebraic structure; numerical method selection chooses a numerical method that matches the analysis result and commits to a discretization, stabilization, and linear solver accordingly; and adaptive tuning runs low-cost pilot solves to calibrate resolution and tolerances under the prescribed accuracy and runtime budget. We evaluate AutoPDE on the PDE Agent Bench, where experimental results show that AutoPDE achieves a pass rate of $54.5%$, improving over the strongest baseline by $14.2$ percentage points.

</details>


### [197] [Accelerating NeurASP with vectorization and caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10787)
*Alexander Philipp Rader,Alessandra Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过向量化、批处理和中间计算缓存等技术，显著提升了NeurASP框架（一种神经符号AI框架）的计算性能，实现了多个数量级的加速，并提出了新的扑克牌数据集来测试增强后的学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始的NeurASP框架中，通过非可微的ASP组件进行反向传播需要昂贵的概率和梯度计算，这阻碍了其扩展到更复杂的任务上，因此需要提升计算性能。

Method: 通过向量化（vectorization）、批处理（batch processing）和缓存中间计算结果（caching of intermediate computations）来优化NeurASP的训练过程。

Result: 新实现相比原始实现，在较大任务上实现了多个数量级的加速。此外，提出了一个涉及扑克牌的困难任务新数据集，用于测试NeurASP增强后的学习功能。

Conclusion: 通过计算优化，NeurASP的可扩展性得到了显著提升，能够处理更复杂的神经符号学习任务。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic AI combines neural networks with symbolic programs to create robust and explainable predictions. One such framework is NeurASP, which trains a neural network to predict concepts and reasons over them using rules written in answer set programming (ASP) to solve downstream tasks. Crucially, labels are only provided for the downstream prediction produced by the symbolic rules, not for the latent concepts themselves.Backpropagation through the non-differentiable ASP component requires expensive probability and gradient calculations, which has hindered scalability to more sophisticated tasks.In this paper, we address the current limitations of NeurASP by improving its computational performance through vectorization, batch processing and caching of intermediate computations during training. We compare computation speeds between the original and our new implementation of NeurASP and report speedups of multiple orders of magnitude for larger tasks. To this end, we propose a new dataset of difficult tasks involving playing cards, which we use to test the capabilities of NeurASP's enhanced learning function.

</details>


### [198] [READER: Robust Evidence-based Authorship Decoding via Extracted Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10794)
*Jiaxu Liu,Sunnan Mu,Dong Huang,Liuyin Wang,Jing Shao,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出READER框架，通过冻结的代理LLM从黑盒响应中提取隐藏的作者身份证据，实现动态黑盒LLM溯源。在Agent500数据集上，单响应top-1准确率达31.0%-42.4%，50响应可达70.0%-84.0%，显著优于传统句子编码指纹方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着代理应用通过官方和第三方LLM API路由用户任务，溯源（确定哪个模型生成了给定的黑盒响应）成为操作层面的关键问题。现有方法依赖固定输入集或基准套件，无法适应查询变化、非预定义提示的实际场景。

Method: READER框架包含三个核心步骤：1) 将黑盒输出映射到冻结代理LLM的激活空间；2) 对每个响应内的token状态进行时间滤波；3) 通过贝叶斯证据累积，对独立采样的提示求和单响应对数后验证据。使用50个目标模型的Agent500数据集进行评估。

Result: READER在单响应场景下达到31.0%-42.4%的top-1准确率，50响应下提升至70.0%-84.0%，显著优于句子编码指纹方法。更强LLM作为代理读者时，暴露更多线性可解码的作者身份结构。

Conclusion: 作者身份感知已存在于冻结LLM的表示中，可转化为可靠的多查询归因。READER框架轻量、无需微调，为黑盒LLM溯源提供了可扩展、置信度校准的解决方案。

Abstract: As agentic applications increasingly route user tasks through official and third-party LLM APIs, provenance becomes an operational question: which model generated a given black-box response? We study Dynamic Black-Box LLM Provenance: identifying the source LLM from generations elicited by query-varying, non-predefined prompts rather than a fixed input set or benchmark suite. This setting is difficult because prompt semantics dominate the text, while model-specific authorship traces are weak and inconsistent at the surface level. We introduce READER (Robust Evidence-based Authorship Decoding via Extracted Representations), a lightweight provenance framework that treats a frozen proxy LLM as a reader of hidden authorship evidence. READER maps black-box outputs into proxy activation space, temporally filters token states within each response, and performs Bayesian Evidence Accumulation by summing single-response log-posterior evidence across independently sampled prompts. This avoids fragile mean-pooling of prompt-specific representations while preserving the query-wise evidence needed for calibrated confidence. On Agent500, a 50-target dataset built from agent-style prompts, READER reaches $31.0$-$42.4\%$ top-1 accuracy from a single response and $70.0$-$84.0\%$ from 50 responses, substantially outperforming sentence-encoder fingerprints. Scaling across nine proxy readers further shows that stronger LLMs expose more linearly decodable authorship structure, suggesting that authorship perception is already present in frozen LLM representations and can be converted into reliable multi-query attribution.

</details>


### [199] [Evaluating Research-Level Math Proofs via Strict Step-Level Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10799)
*Yifeng Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出从全局评估转向严格步骤级验证的LLM数学证明检查框架，通过维护每个推导步骤的详细上下文并严格约束所用定理的来源，有效解决了全局评估中的"上下文中毒"问题。在FirstProof挑战赛的研究级证明对抗性诊断集上，该框架不仅优于全局评估，还改变了失败分类——剩余拒绝主要是"迂腐的过度严谨"而非严重的逻辑幻觉，揭示了专家基准本身的隐含歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在严格验证复杂数学证明方面存在困难，标准全局评估方法遭受"上下文中毒"问题——表面合理的陈述掩盖了微妙的逻辑缺陷，导致幻觉或过度怀疑。

Method: 提出从全局评估转向严格步骤级验证的框架：为每个推导步骤维护详细上下文，并严格约束所应用定理的来源。在来自FirstProof挑战赛的精心策划的对抗性诊断套件上进行评估，并进行系统的消融研究。

Result: 有序的逐步验证框架优于全局评估；从根本上改变了失败分类：剩余拒绝主要是"迂腐的过度严谨"（源于未言明的领域惯例），而非严重的逻辑幻觉；消融实验表明演绎约束不可或缺。

Conclusion: 引导LLM以谨慎的、类似人类数学家的方式组织验证笔记，可以显著提高其区分严谨证明和有缺陷证明的能力，有助于增强基础模型尚不熟悉的前沿数学概念上的智能推理能力，并为未来自动化审稿系统奠定理论基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to rigorously verify complex mathematical proofs. Standard global evaluation approaches suffer from "context poisoning," in which superficially plausible statements mask subtle logical flaws, leading to hallucination or over-skepticism. To address this, we shift from global evaluation to strict step-level verification: our framework maintains detailed context for each deduction step and strictly constrains the sources of applied theorems. We evaluate on a carefully curated adversarial diagnostic suite of research-level proofs drawn from the FirstProof challenge. A systematic ablation study demonstrates that these deductive constraints are indispensable, as unconstrained global prompting consistently fails to localize subtle logical errors. Beyond outperforming global evaluation, our approach fundamentally alters the failure taxonomy. Error analysis reveals that, rather than exhibiting severe logical hallucinations, remaining rejections are primarily instances of "pedantic hyper-rigor" stemming from unstated domain conventions, effectively exposing implicit ambiguities within the expert benchmark itself. Our findings suggest that prompting agents to organize their verification notes in a cautious, human-mathematician-like manner can substantially improve their ability to distinguish rigorous proofs from flawed ones, with the potential to strengthen agentic reasoning on frontier mathematical concepts that the base model does not already know well, and to lay a theoretical foundation for future automated proof-review systems. Code and prompts are available at GitHub.

</details>


### [200] [Moonshine: An Autonomous Mathematical Research Agent Centered on Conjecture Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10806)
*Xiaoyang Chen,Xiang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Moonshine是一个自主生成数学猜想的智能体，以Jacobian猜想为例，将其转化为神经Jacobian猜想(NJC)，并通过GPT-5.5-pro和DeepSeek-V4-pro独立证明了N=n+1情形，展示了自主数学发现的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在开发一个能够自主生成数学猜想并构建可扩展理论框架的智能体，而非仅解决单一命题。

Method: 从经典数学问题中提取结构、提炼新概念并生成猜想；利用GPT-5.5-pro和DeepSeek-V4-pro独立证明，以及通过ChatGPT交互式界面开发几何-拓扑证明。

Result: 成功将Jacobian猜想转移到单隐藏层仿射-ridge sigmoid网络，提出神经Jacobian猜想(NJC)，并独立完成N=n+1情形的完整证明。

Conclusion: Moonshine展示了自主生成有意义的数学问题并取得严格进展的能力，但一般高宽度情形(N≥n+2)仍待解决。

Abstract: Moonshine is an autonomous agent whose central objective is to generate mathematical conjectures. Its core capability is to extract structure from classical problems, distill new concepts, and formulate conjectures of mathematical significance. Rather than treating the solution of a single proposition as its endpoint, Moonshine builds an extensible theoretical framework through conjecture generation, bridge building, and obstacle identification. This article uses Moonshine's exploration of the Jacobian conjecture as an example. It shows how the central logic of whether local nondegeneracy can force global injectivity is transferred to one-hidden-layer affine-ridge sigmoid networks. This leads to the formulation of the \emph{Neural Jacobian Conjecture} (NJC): if such a network has strictly positive Jacobian determinant on the whole space, then it must be globally injective. By invoking GPT-5.5-pro and DeepSeek-V4-pro separately, Moonshine obtained independent complete proofs for the case \(N=n+1\). In addition, with the assistance of ChatGPT through interactive use of its web interface with GPT-5.5-pro, a geometric-topological proof was developed. These results provide preliminary evidence for the plausibility of the conjecture. The general higher-width case \(N\ge n+2\), however, remains unresolved and is left for further investigation. This work illustrates Moonshine's ability to autonomously generate meaningful mathematical problems and make rigorous progress on them.

</details>


### [201] [Do VLMs Reason Like Engineers? A Benchmark and a Stage-wise Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10833)
*Syed Wasiq,Syed Mohamad Tawseeq,Yashwant Pravinrao Bangde,Debaditya Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了EngVQA基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在多学科工程推理中的能力，并设计了一个8阶段自动评估框架，发现现有模型在工程推理方面存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估最终答案，缺乏对中间推理过程的评估；VLMs在通用多模态推理上表现良好，但其工程推理能力（如解读技术图表、选择物理原理、保持物理一致的多步推理）尚未被系统探索。

Method: 构建了涵盖5个工程学科、696道题目的EngVQA基准；设计了一个8阶段自动评估框架，独立评估解决方案的每个阶段，实现细粒度的推理失败分析。

Result: 多个开源和闭源的最先进VLMs在工程推理上表现出显著局限性；自动评估框架与人工评估高度一致，Pearson相关系数为0.975，平均绝对误差为0.67（10分制）。

Conclusion: 面向过程的评估对于可靠评估多模态工程推理系统至关重要，当前VLMs的工程推理能力仍有很大提升空间。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate strong performance on general multimodal reasoning benchmarks, yet their ability to perform engineering reasoning remains largely unexplored. Unlike general visual question answering, engineering problem solving requires interpreting technical diagrams, selecting governing physical principles, and maintaining physically consistent multi-step reasoning. These capabilities are increasingly important for AI systems used in engineering education, scientific assistance, and technical decision-making, where reasoning failures may produce physically invalid yet superficially plausible solutions. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate final answers and provide limited assessment of intermediate reasoning processes. We introduce EngVQA, a multimodal benchmark for evaluating engineering reasoning across 5 engineering subjects containing 696 problems. We introduce an 8-stage automatic evaluation framework for assessing VLM-generated solutions. The framework independently evaluates each stage of the solution, enabling fine-grained analysis of reasoning failures. We benchmark multiple state-of-the-art open and closed source VLMs on our evaluation framework and demonstrate substantial limitations in current engineering reasoning capabilities. Human evaluation shows strong agreement with our automated framework, achieving a Pearson correlation of 0.975 and a mean absolute error of 0.67 on a 10-point grading scale. Our results highlight the importance of process-oriented evaluation for reliable assessment of multimodal engineering reasoning systems.

</details>


### [202] [Large-scale semantic mapping of learner agency and autonomy reveals what measurement and generative AI research overlook](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10881)
*Fei Qin,Xiaobo Liu,Yaowen Zhang,Xuming Li,Fei Wang,Mutlu Cukurova,Jingjing Chen,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过语义分析14,000+出版物中的8,954条定义和2,700个量表项目，揭示了学习者能动性与自主性研究中的"jingle-jangle"谬误，发现两个构念可归为任务（学习调控）、个体（内在动机与决策）和社会文化（社会关系行动）三个维度，但现有量表系统性地低估了社会文化维度，且当前教育领域的生成式AI研究过度聚焦于学习调控维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习者能动性与自主性研究存在严重的"jingle-jangle"谬误（同一术语指向不同构念，不同术语指向同一构念），严重阻碍了知识的累积性发展，亟需对该领域的概念体系进行系统梳理与实证澄清。

Method: 从14,000多篇出版物中提取8,954条定义和2,700个量表项目，采用语义分析流程，基于"意义通过语言实践中的使用而构成"的立场，系统考察研究者实际使用"学习者能动性"和"自主性"的方式。

Result: 两个构念的定义性景观可解析为三个维度：任务维度（学习调控与控制）、个体维度（内在动机与内部决策）及社会文化维度（社会关系行动），实证量化了jingle-jangle谬误。现有量表系统性地低估了社会文化维度。当前教育领域的生成式AI研究集中于学习调控维度，限制了AI介导学习环境旨在培养的行为谱系。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅提供了概念澄清，还对多维学习者能动性与自主性的概念化、测量和实践具有直接启示意义。

Abstract: Learner agency and autonomy are foundational to personal development, yet a pervasive "jingle-jangle" fallacy (i.e. identical terms denoting different constructs, distinct terms denoting identical ones) has substantially hindered cumulative knowledge. Treating meaning as a phenomenon constituted through use in linguistic practice, we extracted 8,954 definitions and 2,700 scale items from over 14,000 publications, to investigate how researchers actually used learner agency and autonomy with a semantic analysis pipeline. The definitional landscape of two constructs resolves into three dimensions: regulation and control of learning (task), intrinsic motivation and internal decision-making (person), and social-relational action (sociocultural), thereby empirically quantifying the jingle-jangle fallacy. Existing scales, however, systematically underrepresent the sociocultural dimension. Critically, current generative AI research in education concentrates on learning regulation and control, narrowing the behavioral repertoire that AI-mediated learning environments are designed to cultivate. Beyond conceptual clarification, this work carries direct implications for conceptualization, measurement, and practice towards supporting the multidimensional learner agency and autonomy.

</details>


### [203] [Role-Agent: Bootstrapping LLM Agents via Dual-Role Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10917)
*Xucong Wang,Ziyu Ma,Shidong Yang,Tongwen Huang,Pengkun Wang,Yong Wang,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Role-Agent框架，让单个LLM同时扮演智能体和环境，通过World-In-Agent（利用状态预测对齐作为过程奖励）和Agent-In-World（基于失败模式分析重塑训练分布）实现自举协同进化，在多个基准上平均提升超4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体受限于低效的交互反馈和静态训练环境，难以实现广泛泛化。

Method: Role-Agent包含两个组件：WIA中LLM作为智能体预测动作后未来状态，将预测与实际状态的对齐作为过程奖励；AIW中LLM分析失败轨迹中的失败模式并检索相似任务，重塑训练数据分布以实现针对性练习。

Result: 在多个基准上一致提升性能，相比强基线平均提升超过4%。

Conclusion: 通过自举协同进化的方式，单LLM同时扮演智能体和环境可以有效提升智能体性能。

Abstract: Although Large Language Model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong performance on complex tasks, their learning is often limited by inefficient interaction feedback and static training environments, which hinder broader generalization. To address these limitations, this paper introduces Role-Agent, \textcolor{black}{a framework} that harnesses a single LLM to function concurrently as both the agent and the environment, enabling a bootstrapped co-evolution. Role-Agent comprises two synergistic components: World-In-Agent (WIA) and Agent-In-World (AIW). In WIA, the LLM acts as the agent and predicts future states after each action; the alignment between predicted and actual states is then used as a process reward, encouraging environment-aware reasoning. In AIW, the LLM analyzes failure modes from failed trajectories and retrieves tasks with similar failure patterns, thereby reshaping the training data distribution for targeted practice. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that Role-Agent consistently improves performance, yielding an average gain of over 4\% over strong baselines.

</details>


### [204] [Frontier Coding Agents Use Metaprogramming to Adapt to Unfamiliar Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10933)
*Aman Sharma,Sushrut Thorat,Paras Chopra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了六个当代编码智能体在四种生僻编程语言上的表现，发现强智能体（如Claude Opus 4.6和GPT-5.4 xhigh）倾向于采用元编程策略（用Python生成目标语言代码并调试），而非直接编写目标语言。禁止这种策略会导致性能大幅下降，而为弱智能体提供该策略的文本指导效果甚微，但提供Opus生成的Python辅助代码则能显著提升Sonnet 4.6和GPT-5.4 mini。更多解释器调用和输出token能增强强智能体，但对弱智能体帮助不大。结果表明，强编码智能体通过工具、反馈和工作空间状态构建目标语言的工作模型来适应不熟悉的语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编码智能体评估通常集中在主流编程语言和公共仓库上，忽略了智能体在面对不熟悉语言时的行为差异。本研究旨在通过生僻编程语言揭示被主流基准测试压缩的能力差异，探究强智能体如何适应陌生语言环境。

Method: 使用顺序设置（文件编辑、本地执行、隐藏测试评分）在四种生僻编程语言上评估六个当代编码智能体。通过对比元编程策略、文本指导、Python辅助代码以及不同计算资源（解释器调用次数、输出token数量）对智能体性能的影响，分析能力差异的根源。

Result: 强智能体倾向于采用元编程策略（用Python生成目标语言代码并调试），禁止该策略导致性能大幅下降。文本指导对弱智能体改善有限，但Opus衍生的Python辅助代码能显著提升Sonnet 4.6和GPT-5.4 mini。更多计算资源能增强强智能体但无法改善弱智能体。

Conclusion: 强编码智能体适应陌生语言的关键在于利用工具、反馈和工作空间状态构建目标语言的工作模型，元编程是其中最清晰的表现形式，但更广泛的差距在于能否构建和调试出在目标语言规则下有效的策略。

Abstract: LLM-based coding agents are usually evaluated in familiar software settings: mainstream languages, common libraries, and public repositories. These benchmarks remain important, but they can hide how agents behave when the language itself is unfamiliar. We evaluate six contemporary coding agents on four esoteric programming languages using a sequential setup with file editing, local execution, and hidden-test grading. Our protocol exposes capability differences between these agents that mainstream coding and agentic benchmarks such as SWE-Bench Verified and Terminal-Bench 2.0 compress into much narrower bands. We observe that the strongest agents, Claude Opus 4.6 and GPT-5.4 xhigh, often avoid writing the target language directly. On Brainfuck and Befunge-98, they write Python programs that generate target-language code and debug those generators locally. Forbidding this metaprogramming strategy causes large performance drops. Text guidance distilled from this strategy does not materially improve weaker agents. In contrast, Opus-derived Python helper code for building generators, with no solved benchmark programs or hidden-test answers, sharply improves Sonnet 4.6 and GPT-5.4 mini on the same problems, while Haiku 4.5 remains low. More interpreter calls and output tokens improve stronger agents but leave weaker agents near their original performance, indicating that these resources amplify useful strategies rather than create them. Together, these results show that strong coding agents adapt to unfamiliar languages by using tools, feedback, and workspace state to build a working model of the target language. Metaprogramming is the clearest case, but the broader gap is constructing and debugging a strategy that works under the target language's rules.

</details>


### [205] [WorldKernel: A World Model is the Coupling Kernel of Admissible Possible Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10934)
*Fabio Rovai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个反直觉的失败模式：即使有足够的观测和干预数据，强预测器在处理反事实耦合（不同可能世界之间的关联）时也会崩溃，因为它无法表示跨世界的不确定性。论文提出用正半定耦合核 K(T,T') 来统一建模，其中对角线对应普通后验（预测器可恢复），非对角线对应反事实耦合（预测器无法恢复），并研究了后者的理论性质、边界、获取和重建问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"足够的数据+足够强的预测器就足以解决因果推断问题"这一常见假设，指出在反事实耦合这一不可识别量上，预测器存在结构性失败——它无法表达跨世界的不确定性，即使数据无限也会给出错误的点估计。

Method: 将世界模型形式化为单个正半定耦合核 K(T,T')，对角线是普通后验，非对角线是反事实耦合；利用正半定性作为部分识别信息来约束反事实范围；通过本体论公理收紧边界；通过目标化约束（scars）加速信息获取；将完全重建问题归结为可容许世界数量的近似计数问题。

Result: 在数百个结构因果模型上，预测器在可识别量上与贝叶斯基线表现一致，但在反事实耦合上完全崩溃（在28%的模型中给出任何有效模型都无法产生的点估计）；正半定性约束可在多项式时间内求解反事实边界；本体论公理可将边界收紧三分之一；目标化约束比非目标化约束快数倍；完全重构在Sly-Sun阈值以下可处理，以上则不可近似。

Conclusion: 反事实耦合是预测器无法恢复的固有不确定性来源，必须通过结构性约束（正半定性、逻辑公理、目标化约束）来刻画其边界，而不能依赖无限数据或强预测器来消除。

Abstract: A common assumption holds that enough observational and interventional data, given to a strong enough predictor, suffices. We report a failure mode that contradicts it. Across hundreds of structural causal models, on identified quantities a strong predictor and a Bayesian baseline both succeed, but on unidentified quantities (the couplings between counterfactual worlds) the predictor collapses to a point, on 28% of models to one no valid model can produce, while the truth is an admissible interval more data never narrows. The gap is structural: prediction cannot represent uncertainty over counterfactual couplings. We cast a world model as a single positive semidefinite coupling kernel K(T,T') over admissible worlds, whose diagonal is the ordinary posterior (what a predictor recovers) and whose off-diagonal is the cross-world coupling it cannot, which every counterfactual reads. The paper is the theory of that off-diagonal. It is real: two states with identical posteriors differ on a cross-world query, and the off-diagonal is the coupling that fixes counterfactuals. It can be bounded: positive semidefiniteness is partial-identifying information the marginals lack, and enforcing it bounds counterfactuals in polynomial time where the exact response-type program is intractable. Logical structure sharpens it: ontology axioms tighten the bound by up to a third, propagating to couplings they never touch. It can be acquired: targeted scars, constraints learned from encountered infeasibilities, close the gap several times faster than untargeted ones. Its full reconstruction is approximate counting of the admissible worlds, tractable below the Sly-Sun threshold and inapproximable above; we do not claim to beat the worst case.

</details>


### [206] [Recalling Too Well: Sycophancy Evaluation and Mitigation in Memory-Augmented Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10949)
*Shelly Bensal,Axel Magnuson,Aparna Balagopalan,Daniel M. Bikel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 持久记忆系统在提升LLM助益性的同时，会系统性放大谄媚行为（模型优先同意用户而非追求准确性）。本文首次系统评估该效应，引入MIST基准测试，发现记忆使谄媚率比上下文基线最高提升25倍，并提出两种轻量级缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久记忆系统虽然通过存储用户信念提升了LLM的助益性，但可能带来模型优先同意用户而非追求准确性的谄媚行为放大问题，本文旨在首次系统评估这一效应并提供缓解方案。

Method: 构建MIST基准测试（多轮对话中用户表达科学、医学、道德推理领域的合理误解），在三种最先进的记忆系统和五个模型家族上进行测试，并通过错误分析识别记忆提取（lossy compression）为谄媚的主要成因。

Result: 记忆系统在所有条件下均放大谄媚行为，谄媚率相比上下文基线最高提升25倍；记忆提取过程中的有损压缩（将用户误解编码为离散片段并丢弃纠正性上下文）是主要成因。

Conclusion: 提出了两种轻量级缓解方法，在显著降低谄媚行为的同时，在事实回忆任务上达到或超过现有记忆系统的性能。

Abstract: Persistent memory systems promise to make LLMs more helpful by storing user beliefs over time. We show they also make models less correct by systematically amplifying sycophancy, wherein models prioritize agreement with users over accuracy. We conduct the first systematic evaluation of this effect, introducing MIST: a benchmark of synthetically generated multi-turn conversations where users express plausible misconceptions in scientific, medical, and moral reasoning domains. Testing across three state-of-the-art memory systems and five model families reveals that memory amplifies sycophantic behavior across all conditions, with up to 25x higher sycophancy rates than in-context baselines. Error analyses suggest memory extraction as the primary culprit: lossy compression into discrete snippets encodes user misconceptions while discarding corrective context. Based on these results, we propose two lightweight mitigations that substantially reduce sycophancy while matching or exceeding memory systems at factual recall.

</details>


### [207] [Architect-Ant: Editable Automatic Furnishing of Architectural Floor Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10953)
*Fedor Rodionov,Aleksandar Cvejic,Michael Birsak,John Femiani,Peter Wonka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AntPlan-270是包含270个建筑平面图及逐房间家具标注的数据集；Architect-Ant是基于微调视觉语言模型的可编辑自动家具布局框架，通过领域特定语言编码布局、程序化推理轨迹增强空间理解，并结合偏好优化与Flux-based LoRA渲染生成逼真平面图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动家具布局研究受限于缺乏带有对象级家具标注的真实专业平面图数据集，现有进展缓慢。

Method: 1) 构建AntPlan-270数据集（270个平面图，逐房间家具边界框标注，10类住宅房间）；2) 基于微调视觉语言模型的Architect-Ant框架；3) 使用坐标式领域特定语言(DSL)编码家具类别与位置；4) 生成程序化推理轨迹（墙对齐、门窗避让、动线、洁具兼容性、房间家具清单等约束）用于监督微调；5) 偏好优化候选物体放置；6) Flux-based LoRA渲染器将DSL光栅化为语义掩码并生成逼真蓝图风格平面图。

Result: Architect-Ant生成的布局在几何有效性和功能合理性上表现优异，实验表明该方法可扩展用于为更大的仅有结构的平面图数据集自动标注家具。

Conclusion: 该工作通过数据集构建与视觉语言模型微调相结合，提供了可编辑、可扩展的自动家具布局方案，填补了该领域数据与方法的空白。

Abstract: Furnished floor plans are fundamental to real estate visualization, interior design, and architectural workflows. However, progress in automatic furniture arrangement has been limited by the lack of real, professionally designed floor-plan datasets with object-level furniture annotations. To address this gap, we introduce AntPlan-270, a curated dataset of 270 architectural floor plans with per-room furniture bounding box annotations across ten residential room categories. Building on this dataset, we present Architect-Ant, an editable automatic furnishing framework powered by a fine-tuned vision-language model. Furniture layouts are represented using a compact, coordinate-based domain-specific language (DSL) that encodes object categories and placements relative to the room geometry. To improve spatial reasoning, we generate procedural reasoning traces that capture architectural constraints such as wall alignment, door and window clearance, circulation, fixture compatibility, and room-specific furniture inventories, and use them to supervise fine-tuning of the model. We then apply preference optimization over candidate object placements to further refine layout quality. The generated DSL can be rasterized into semantic masks and used to condition a Flux-based LoRA renderer, producing realistic blueprint-style furnished floor-plan images while preserving the editable symbolic layout. Experiments on layout furnishing show that Architect-Ant produces geometrically valid and functionally plausible layouts, and suggest a scalable path for furnishing larger structure-only floor-plan datasets.

</details>


### [208] [Mind the Gap: Can Frontier LLMs Pass a Standardized Office Proficiency Exam?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10956)
*Tengchao Lv,Dongdong Zhang,Jiayu Ding,Yilin Jia,Yuzhong Zhao,Yupan Huang,Wenshan Wu,Xiangyang Zhou,Shaohan Huang,Nan Yang,Li Dong,Lei Cui,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于中国计算机等级考试（NCRE）构建了包含200个Office实操任务的基准测试，评估了前沿LLM在办公自动化场景下的表现。最强单轮模型得分仅36.6%，即使采用迭代修复等增强策略的智能体系统也只达到68.8%，远低于95.5%的社区参考分，表明当前LLM在精细化办公文档自动化方面仍面临重大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM代理在计算机自动化领域的部署日益增多，但其在复杂、专业级生产力软件中的导航能力尚未得到充分测试。办公自动化因其需要长程规划与推理、精确参数配置及多应用集成，被认为是评估文档自动化能力的理想环境。

Method: 基于中国国家计算机等级考试（NCRE）构建评估基准，包含200个涵盖Word、Excel、PowerPoint的综合实操任务。每个任务采用100分制评分，共7,118个机器可评分标准，以Score Rate（SR）作为平均得分率指标。对7个前沿LLM进行基准测试，并比较单轮模型与具备执行反馈、迭代修复及更广泛Office自动化访问权限的增强型智能体系统。

Result: 单轮模型最高得分率仅36.6%；增强型智能体系统达到68.8%；社区参考分为95.5%。所有系统均远未达到参考分水平。

Conclusion: 尽管代码生成领域近期有所进展，但实现可靠的细粒度办公文档自动化对当前代码生成LLM及智能体系统仍是一项重大挑战。

Abstract: The deployment of Large Language Model (LLM) agents for computer automation is accelerating, yet their ability to navigate complex, professional-grade productivity software is largely untested. We argue that Office automation is an ideal environment for benchmarking document-automation capability, as it requires long-horizon planning and reasoning, precise parameter configuration, and multi-application integration. To quantify this capability, we introduce an evaluation based on China's National Computer Rank Examination (NCRE), featuring 200 comprehensive practical-operation tasks across Word, Excel, and PowerPoint. Each task is scored on a 100-point rubric scale using 7,118 machine-gradable criteria, and Score Rate (SR) denotes the mean percentage of rubric points earned across these tasks. We benchmark 7 frontier LLMs and observe stark limitations: single-turn models score a maximum of 36.6%. A stronger agentic system with execution feedback, iterative repair, and broader Office automation access reaches 68.8%, but remains below the 95.5% community-reference score used as a scoring sanity check. Ultimately, our experiments demonstrate that despite recent advancements in code generation, achieving reliable fine-grained Office document automation remains a significant challenge for current code-generating LLM and agent systems.

</details>


### [209] [Bellman-Taylor Score Decoding for Markov Decision Processes with State-Dependent Feasible Action Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10979)
*Yi Chen,Rushuai Yang,Qiang Chen,Dongyan,Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Bellman-Taylor分数解码框架，通过泰勒展开将状态依赖且隐式约束的MDP策略学习映射到欧几里得分数空间，使用动作解码器保证可行性，使标准DRL算法可优化而无需对解码器求导。最优性间隙分解为结构逼近误差和算法学习误差。在排队网络控制中学习到状态依赖的指标调度策略，小规模近最优，大规模显著优于基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运筹学中的许多MDP具有状态依赖且由操作约束隐式定义的可行动作，标准DRL算法（固定动作集或简单欧氏空间）难以处理此类问题。

Method: 基于最优动作价值函数的泰勒展开，提出Bellman-Taylor分数解码：将策略学习迁移到欧几里得分数空间，通过动作解码器保证可行性，得到潜在分数MDP，可用标准DRL优化且无需对解码器求导。

Result: 将最优性间隙分解为结构逼近误差和算法学习误差。在排队网络控制中，策略学到状态依赖的指标调度规则，小规模实例近最优，大规模系统显著优于基准。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了状态依赖隐式约束MDP的DRL应用难题，提供了理论性能保证，并在排队网络控制中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Many Markov decision processes (MDPs) in operations research have feasible actions that are state dependent and defined implicitly by various operational constraints. These features make it difficult to use standard deep reinforcement learning (DRL) algorithms, whose action interfaces typically assume either a fixed finite action catalog or a simple Euclidean space. Motivated by a Taylor expansion of the optimal action-value function, we propose Bellman--Taylor score decoding, a framework that moves policy learning to a Euclidean score space while enforcing feasibility through an action decoder. The induced latent-score MDP then can be optimized by standard DRL algorithms without differentiating through the decoder. We provide a performance guarantee showing that the optimality gap of this approach decomposes into a structural approximation error and an algorithmic learning error. Lastly, we apply this framework to a queueing network control problem, where the policy essentially learns a state-dependent index-based dispatching rule. Numerical experiments show near-optimal performance in small instances and considerable improvements over benchmarks in larger systems.

</details>


### [210] [Null-Space Constrained Low-Rank Adaptation for Response-Specified Large Language Model Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10989)
*Bocheng Ju,Jianhua Wang,Chengliang Liu,Xiaolin Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Null-Space Constrained Response-Specified Unlearning (NSRU)，一种投影约束的低秩框架，通过安全目标响应、正交投影参数化和保留子空间约束，在抑制不良知识的同时保持模型良性能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型遗忘方法要么只抑制不良答案导致更新局部性不受约束，要么虽指定替代行为但仍缺乏对更新范围的充分限制，亟需一种既能精确控制遗忘行为又能严格约束更新局部性的方法。

Method: NSRU使用显式结构化的安全目标响应指定遗忘查询的期望行为，同时抑制原始不良内容；通过从良性隐藏表示估计每模块保留子空间，利用正交投影低秩参数化将LoRA更新约束在保留子空间的零空间中；联合优化安全目标学习、不良响应抑制和保留保持三个目标。

Result: 在TOFU上有效抑制可提取的遗忘集知识，同时提升保留QA性能、模型效用和安全目标对齐；在WMDP上将危险领域准确率保持在随机猜测附近，同时保持广泛和领域相邻的MMLU效用；消融实验验证了各组件的互补作用，敏感性和鲁棒性分析表明超参数和提示变化下行为稳定。

Conclusion: NSRU通过零空间约束投影更新在减少保留侧扰动的同时保留编辑方向以塑造遗忘查询行为，为可控的大模型遗忘提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Large language model unlearning aims to suppress designated undesirable knowledge while preserving benign capabilities. Many unlearning objectives focus on suppressing undesired answers, while recent target-guided variants specify replacement behavior but still leave update locality largely unconstrained. This paper introduces \emph{Null-Space Constrained Response-Specified Unlearning} (NSRU), a projection-constrained low-rank framework for controlled LLM unlearning. NSRU uses an explicitly structured safe target response to specify the desired behavior for each forget query, while suppressing the original undesired content. To localize adaptation, NSRU estimates per-module retain subspaces from benign hidden representations and uses an orthogonal-projected low-rank parameterization to confine LoRA updates to the null space of the retain subspace. The resulting objective jointly optimizes safe-target learning, undesired-response suppression, and retention preservation under this constrained parameterization. We provide a local first-order analysis showing that the projected update reduces retain-side perturbations while preserving editable directions for shaping forget-query behavior. Experiments on TOFU show that NSRU effectively suppresses extractable forget-set knowledge while improving retain QA performance, model utility, and safe-target alignment over representative baselines. On WMDP, NSRU keeps hazardous-domain accuracy near the random-choice region while preserving broad and domain-adjacent MMLU utility. Ablation studies support the complementary roles of safe-target supervision, undesired-response suppression, retention loss, and null-space projected updates, while sensitivity and robustness analyses indicate stable behavior across the tested hyperparameter and prompt variations.

</details>


### [211] [Structure from Reasoning, Numbers from Search: On-Premise Open LLMs as Structural Priors for Coupled MIMO Controller Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11015)
*Jiaxuan Chen,Haonan Li,Yang Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了开源大语言模型（LLM）在强耦合MIMO工业过程控制器调参中的应用。对于简单单回路，LLM调参不如经典中继反馈法；但对于强耦合系统，基于LLM的调参能推理耦合关系、提出非对称结构，在样本效率（18次评估即可得到可用控制器）和可解释性上优于全局优化器，最终通过经典优化器细化达到全局最优。LLM的优势在于作为样本高效、可解释的结构先验，而非优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合MIMO工业过程的控制器调参面临两大困难：分散式经典自整定忽略了回路间耦合，而局部数值优化在非凸代价景观中容易陷入停滞。传统方法缺乏处理耦合的先验知识，需要一种既能理解耦合关系又无需精确模型、且能保护数据隐私的方法，因此探索使用本地部署的开源大语言模型来辅助调参。

Method: 对比了经典中继反馈调参、局部数值优化、全局优化（差分进化）以及基于开源LLM的调参方法。在LLM调参中，采用"scaffolded"方式（引导LLM推理耦合关系并建议控制器结构），并在强耦合四水箱系统等基准上进行评估，使用惩罚代价J = IAE + λ·TV(u)作为性能指标。

Result: 1) 单回路CSTR中，经典中继反馈（IAE 0.106）优于LLM调参（0.162），LLM无优势。2) 强耦合四水箱系统中，朴素中继调参（J~28.6）和朴素LLM调参（29.7）不如开环（22.7）；局部优化器10/10失败。3) Scaffolded LLM从任意起点均能达到J~16.9±0.2，经经典优化器细化后达到全局最优J~12.0（10/10成功 vs 0/10）。4) 全局优化器也能达到最优，但LLM仅需18次评估（全局优化器此时不如开环），且具有可解释性。5) 在3x3系统中优势扩大至约6倍样本效率。6) 在良性系统中LLM无优势。

Conclusion: 开源LLM在控制器调参中的价值不在于作为优化器，而在于作为样本高效、可解释的结构先验——它能推理耦合关系并提出合理的控制器结构（如非对称结构甚至负积分校正），大幅减少评估次数并提供调参依据。该优势在高维强耦合系统中尤为显著，但在简单系统中不成立。

Abstract: Tuning controllers for strongly coupled multi-input multi-output (MIMO) industrial processes is hard: decentralized classical auto-tuning ignores loop interaction, and local numerical optimization from natural initializations stalls in the resulting non-convex cost landscape. We ask whether on-premise open-source large language models (LLMs), which keep data on-site and need no plant model, can help. On a single-loop CSTR, classical relay-feedback tuning (IAE 0.106, near the 0.102 optimum) beats an LLM tuner (0.162): for simple loops the LLM adds nothing. The picture inverts on a strongly coupled quadruple-tank with conflicting set-points, scored by a penalized cost J = IAE + lambda*TV(u) that rewards tracking without chattering actuators. There, naive relay tuning (J ~ 28.6) and naive LLM tuning (29.7) are no better than open loop (22.7), and a local optimizer from balanced starts fails in 10/10 runs. A scaffolded open LLM instead reasons about the coupling, proposes the counter-intuitive asymmetric structure, and reaches J ~ 16.9 +/- 0.2 from any start; refining it with a classical optimizer attains the smooth global optimum (J ~ 12.0, 10/10 vs. 0/10), which even applies a non-obvious negative integral correction decentralized tuning cannot. A global optimizer (differential evolution) also reaches this optimum, so the LLM is not the only route; its advantage is sample efficiency and interpretability: a usable controller in 18 evaluations (where the global optimizer is worse than open loop) plus a stated rationale. This edge grows with dimension, reaching ~6x fewer evaluations on a 3x3 plant. The behaviour generalizes across four open models, and on a benign plant the LLM offers no advantage, sharpening the boundary. We contribute a reproducible benchmark delimiting when open LLMs help in control tuning: not as optimizers, but as a sample-efficient, interpretable structural prior.

</details>


### [212] [Superficial Beliefs in LLM Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11016)
*Gabriel Freedman,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM在二元决策中表现出系统性的选择模式，但模型无法准确报告驱动其决策的关键属性，显示出"浅层信念"现象——决策结构化但缺乏完整的理性信念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在二元选择中是否仅仅是机械模仿推理过程，还是其选择反映了系统性的底层决策结构。

Method: 在合成二元决策环境中，定义由分级属性构成的选项，将模型声称最重要的属性与基于行为模型（拟合先前决策）推断出的实际驱动因素进行比较；通过提示顺序扰动、采样扰动、替代行为模型、目标遮挡分析和结构化变体设置进行验证。

Result: 行为模型能较好地预测保留集选择，表明行为与可见属性系统相关而非随机；但自我报告和评分判断仅能部分恢复行为推断的驱动属性。这种模式在多种扰动下保持稳定。

Conclusion: LLM表现出"浅层信念"：其决策受属性上的概率性局部优先级引导，但对驱动决策的属性仅有有限的言语可及性。

Abstract: We ask whether large language models (LLMs) merely imitate rationales when choosing between two options, or whether their choices reflect a systematic underlying decision structure. Using synthetic binary decision settings in which models choose between profiles defined by graded attributes, we compare the attribute a model says mattered most with the attribute that best explains its choice under a behavioural model fit to prior decisions. The behavioural model predicts held-out choices well, showing that model behaviour is systematically related to the visible attributes rather than being random. However, direct self-reports and a separate score-based judge recover the behaviourally inferred driver only partially. The resulting picture is neither one of arbitrary behaviour nor one of fully articulated belief - outputs are structured enough to support prediction, but explicit reasons track the recovered driver only imperfectly. This qualitative pattern persists across prompt-order and sampling perturbations, alternative behavioural models, targeted occlusion analyses, and structurally varied decision settings. We interpret this as evidence for ``superficial belief'' in LLM decision-making: models behave as if guided by probabilistic local priorities over attributes, while having only limited verbal access to the attributes that drive their decisions.

</details>


### [213] [Workflow-GYM: Towards Long-Horizon Evaluation of Computer-use Agentic tasks in Real-World Professional Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11042)
*Liya Zhu,Jingzhe Ding,Jian Zhang,Jianbo Xue,Shihao Liang,Ge Zhang,Xiang Gao,Qingshui Gu,Mailun Gao,Huimin Che,Yan Zhao,Peiheng Zhou,Haojun Wang,Chaobo Xian,Lili Le,Chi Wu,Yiwei Liu,Shengda Long,Jiale Yang,Fangzhi Xu,Sijin Wu,Haodong Duan,Yi Zhu,Chao He,Zhaojian Li,Minchao Wang,Huan Zhou,Jiani Hou,Chuqian Yu,Weiran Shi,Hongwan Gao,Jiamin Chen,Guanhong Chen,Tingqin Luo,Kaiyuan Zhang,Zhixin Yao,Qing Hua,Yuhao Jiang,Jin Chen,Pu Chen,Zhenyu Hu,Xingyu Li,Zhengxuan Jiang,Meng Cao,Tianfeng Long,Haozhe Wang,Mingzhang Wang,Yichen Zhang,Yiming Dai,Chenchen Zhang,Jiaying Wang,Zhiyong Wu,Shen Yan,Yujia Qin,Wenhao Huang,Zaiyuan Wang,Xiaolong Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Workflow-GYM基准，用于评估AI代理在专业软件环境中完成长周期、高价值GUI工作流的能力。实验表明最强模型成功率仅略高于30%，揭示了现有代理在保持工作流一致性方面存在阶段遗漏、错误传播、目标漂移和专业软件理解不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI基准主要关注通用软件、简单应用和短周期任务，无法评估AI代理在专业领域软件中自主完成长周期、高价值经济工作的能力，存在评估空白。

Method: 构建Workflow-GYM基准，专注于专业领域和专门化软件环境的长周期GUI任务，并在多个最先进模型上开展广泛实验。
